============================================================
FILE: backend\agents\agent_manifest.py
============================================================
"""
Agent Manifest Definition.

The AgentManifest describes the identity and basic metadata
of an EasyChatBot AI Agent.

The manifest does not execute the agent.
It only defines:
- who the agent is
- what the agent represents
- its version information

Execution is handled by the AI Runtime.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentManifest:
    """
    Immutable definition of an AI Agent.

    Agents are identified through their manifest.
    The Runtime uses this information for controlled execution.

    Attributes:
        agent_id:
            Unique identifier of the agent.

        name:
            Human-readable agent name.

        description:
            Purpose and responsibility of the agent.

        version:
            Version of the agent definition.
    """

    agent_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    version: str = "1.0.0"

============================================================
FILE: backend\agents\agent_result.py
============================================================
"""
Agent Result Contract.

Defines the standard output returned by AI Agents.

All agents should return the same structure
so the Runtime can process results consistently.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentResult:
    """
    Immutable result returned after agent execution.

    Attributes:
        success:
            Indicates whether execution completed successfully.

        agent_id:
            Identifier of the agent that produced the result.

        output:
            Agent response payload.

        error:
            Error message when execution fails.
    """

    success: bool
    agent_id: str
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None

============================================================
FILE: backend\agents\base_agent.py
============================================================
"""
Base AI Agent Contract.

All EasyChatBot AI Agents inherit this contract.

The BaseAgent defines:
- agent identity
- execution interface
- runtime interaction

Agents do not:
- call providers directly
- manage memory
- modify external systems

The AI Runtime controls execution.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext
from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult


class BaseAgent(ABC):
    """
    Abstract base class for EasyChatBot agents.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        manifest: AgentManifest,
    ):
        """
        Initialise an agent.

        Args:
            manifest:
                Agent identity definition.
        """

        self.manifest = manifest

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute the agent.

        Args:
            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            AgentResult.
        """

        pass

============================================================
FILE: backend\agents\conversation_agent.py
============================================================
"""
Conversation Agent.

Provides character-aware conversational responses
for the EasyChatBot hackathon runtime.

The agent remains provider-independent.

Architecture:

ExecutionContext
|
v
ConversationAgent
|
+--> CharacterProfile
|
+--> Optional IntelligenceContext
|
v
ConversationRequest
|
v
LLMService
|
v
LLMProvider
|
v
Concrete LLM Provider
|
v
ProviderResult
|
v
AgentResult

Responsibilities:

- apply character identity
- construct ConversationRequest
- include optional live intelligence
- execute an injected LLMService
- preserve deterministic demo behaviour when no
  LLM service is configured
- return a standard AgentResult

Does not:

- depend directly on OpenAI
- depend directly on Ollama
- call Parallel directly
- manage memory
- perform research
- manage voice
- execute external workflows
- select providers
- execute provider SDKs directly

Architecture boundary:

ConversationAgent
-> LLMService
-> LLMProvider
-> Concrete Provider
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import (
    AgentManifest,
)

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.agents.base_agent import (
    BaseAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.llm.llm_provider import (
    LLMProvider,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


class ConversationAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Character-aware conversation agent.

    The agent depends primarily on the application-level
    LLMService boundary.

    The LLMService is responsible for delegating
    execution to the configured LLMProvider.

    A legacy ``llm_provider`` argument remains accepted
    as a compatibility seam for existing tests and
    callers. When supplied, it is wrapped by LLMService.

    New composition code should inject ``llm_service``.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character: CharacterProfile,
        llm_service: LLMService | None = None,
        llm_provider: LLMProvider | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the Conversation Agent.

        Parameters
        ----------
        character:
            Approved EasyChatBot character profile.

        llm_service:
            Application-level LLM execution service.

        llm_provider:
            Compatibility injection seam.

            When supplied and ``llm_service`` is not supplied,
            the provider is wrapped inside LLMService.

        Raises
        ------
        ValueError
            If both ``llm_service`` and ``llm_provider``
            are supplied simultaneously.
        """

        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="conversation_agent",
                name="Conversation Agent",
                description=(
                    "Provides character-aware "
                    "conversation responses."
                ),
            )
        )

        if (
            llm_service is not None
            and llm_provider is not None
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Provide either llm_service or "
                "llm_provider, not both."
            )

        self.character = character

        if llm_service is not None:
            self.llm_service = llm_service

        elif llm_provider is not None:
            self.llm_service = LLMService(
                llm_provider
            )

        else:
            self.llm_service = None

    def build_request(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ConversationRequest:
        """
        Build a provider-independent conversation request.

        Runtime state and character identity are translated
        into the ConversationRequest contract.

        No provider-specific implementation is introduced.
        """

        system_instruction = (
            f"You are {self.character.name}, "
            f"a character in EasyChatBot. "
            f"Your personality is: "
            f"{self.character.personality} "
            f"Your learning style is: "
            f"{self.character.learning_style} "
            f"Communicate in a way that is consistent "
            f"with this character."
        )

        conversation_context = None

        if context.has_intelligence_context:
            conversation_context = (
                context.intelligence_context.to_context_text()
            )

        return ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction=system_instruction,
            user_prompt=context.request,
            conversation_context=conversation_context,
            character_id=self.character.character_id,
            character_name=self.character.name,
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute the conversation agent.

        Flow:

        ExecutionContext
            |
            v
        ConversationRequest
            |
            v
        LLMService
            |
            v
        LLMProvider
            |
            v
        ProviderResult
            |
            v
        AgentResult

        When no LLMService is configured, the existing
        deterministic hackathon behaviour is preserved.

        When an LLMService is configured, execution
        passes through the application service boundary.
        """

        conversation_request = self.build_request(
            context
        )

        provider_result = None

        if self.llm_service is not None:
            provider_result = (
                await self.llm_service.generate(
                    conversation_request
                )
            )

            if not provider_result.success:
                return AgentResult(
                    success=False,
                    agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
                    output={
                        "character": self.character.name,

                        "character_profile": {
                            "character_id": (
                                self.character.character_id
                            ),
                            "name": self.character.name,
                            "description": (
                                self.character.description
                            ),
                            "personality": (
                                self.character.personality
                            ),
                            "learning_style": (
                                self.character.learning_style
                            ),
                        },

                        "personality": (
                            self.character.personality
                        ),

                        "learning_style": (
                            self.character.learning_style
                        ),

                        "message": (
                            "The conversation provider "
                            "could not generate a response."
                        ),

                        "intelligence_context": (
                            conversation_request
                            .conversation_context
                        ),

                        "conversation_request": (
                            conversation_request
                        ),

                        "provider_id": (
                            provider_result.provider_id
                        ),

                        "provider_error": (
                            provider_result.error
                        ),
                    },
                )

        if provider_result is not None:
            message = provider_result.output

        else:
            message = (
                f"{self.character.name}: "
                f"Let's explore this together. "
                f"You asked: {context.request}"
            )

        output = {
            "character": self.character.name,

            "character_profile": {
                "character_id": (
                    self.character.character_id
                ),
                "name": self.character.name,
                "description": (
                    self.character.description
                ),
                "personality": (
                    self.character.personality
                ),
                "learning_style": (
                    self.character.learning_style
                ),
            },

            "personality": (
                self.character.personality
            ),

            "learning_style": (
                self.character.learning_style
            ),

            "message": message,

            "intelligence_context": (
                conversation_request
                .conversation_context
            ),

            "conversation_request": (
                conversation_request
            ),
        }

        if provider_result is not None:
            output["provider_id"] = (
                provider_result.provider_id
            )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=output,
        )

============================================================
FILE: backend\agents\quality_agent.py
============================================================
"""
Quality Agent.

Internal AI operations agent.

Evaluates AI outputs.

It does not:
- modify responses
- control agents
- interact with users

It provides quality insights.
"""


from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult

from backend.evaluation.quality_result import (
    QualityResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class QualityAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Evaluates AI response quality.
    """


    def __init__(self):

        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="quality_agent",
                name="Quality Agent",
                description=(
                    "Evaluates AI outputs "
                    "for quality and governance."
                ),
            )
        )


    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:

        quality = QualityResult(
            score=0.95,
            passed=True,
            findings=[],
            recommendations=[
                "Continue monitoring response quality"
            ],
        )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output={
                "quality_score": quality.score,
                "passed": quality.passed,
                "findings": quality.findings,
                "recommendations": (
                    quality.recommendations
                ),
            },
        )

============================================================
FILE: backend\agents\research_agent.py
============================================================
"""
Research Agent.

Retrieves external information through approved
knowledge providers and converts the result into
provider-independent IntelligenceContext data.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
ParallelSearchProvider
        |
        v
ParallelClientWrapper
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ResearchAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agent responsible for world information retrieval.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="research_agent",
                name="Research Agent",
                description=(
                    "Retrieves live world information "
                    "for AI learning experiences."
                ),
            )
        )

        self.provider = (
            provider
            or ParallelSearchProvider()
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute research and produce a normalized
        IntelligenceContext.
        """

        result = await self.provider.execute(
            context
        )

        if not result.success:
            return AgentResult(
                success=False,
                agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
                output=None,
                error=result.error,
            )

        intelligence = (
            self._build_intelligence_context(
                query=context.request,
                provider_output=result.output,
            )
        )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=intelligence,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_intelligence_context(
        query: str,
        provider_output,
    ) -> IntelligenceContext:
        """
        Convert provider output into the
        provider-independent IntelligenceContext.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            provider_output,
            dict,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "Research provider output must be a dictionary."
            )

        raw_results = provider_output.get(
            "results",
            {},
        )

        if isinstance(
            raw_results,
            dict,
        ):
            raw_results = raw_results.get(
                "results",
                [],
            )

        if not isinstance(
            raw_results,
            list,
        ):
            raw_results = []

        sources: list[IntelligenceSource] = []

        for raw_result in raw_results:

            if not isinstance(
                raw_result,
                dict,
            ):
                continue

            title = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "title",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "url",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            if not title or not url:
                continue

            content = ResearchAgent._extract_content(
                raw_result
            )

            sources.append(
                IntelligenceSource(
                    title=title,
                    url=url,
                    source="parallel",
                    content=content,
                )
            )

        return IntelligenceContext(
            query=query,
            sources=tuple(sources),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_content(
        raw_result: dict,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Extract available content from a normalized
        Parallel result.
        """

        excerpts = raw_result.get(
            "excerpts",
        )

        if excerpts is None:
            excerpts = raw_result.get(
                "snippet",
                raw_result.get(
                    "content",
                    "",
                ),
            )

        if isinstance(
            excerpts,
            list,
        ):
            return "\n\n".join(
                str(item).strip()
                for item in excerpts
                if str(item).strip()
            )

        return str(
            excerpts or ""
        ).strip()

"""
EasyChatBot API Application.

from fastapi import FastAPI

app = FastAPI(
    title="EasyChatBot",
    description=(
        "AI companion learning platform"
    ),
)

    router,
)

    return {
        "status": "EasyChatBot running",
        "architecture": (
            "Runtime + Agents + Memory"
        ),

============================================================
FILE: backend\api\routes.py
============================================================
"""
EasyChatBot API Routes.

The API layer exposes the application.

Routes do not contain agent logic.

The API composition layer is responsible for wiring
infrastructure dependencies into application services.

Architecture:

API
 |
 v
CompanionService
 |
 v
ConversationAgent
 |
 v
LLMService
 |
 v
LLMProviderFactory
 |
 v
Concrete Provider
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter
from fastapi import HTTPException

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_registry import (
    CharacterRegistry,
)

from backend.providers.llm.llm_provider_factory import (
    LLMProviderFactory,
)

# Compatibility import.
#
# Existing integration tests use this symbol as an
# infrastructure replacement seam through monkeypatch.
#
# Provider creation remains delegated to
# LLMProviderFactory.
from backend.providers.llm.openai_provider import (
    OpenAIProvider,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)

from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


router = APIRouter()

character_registry = CharacterRegistry()


def create_llm_service() -> LLMService:
    """
    Create the application LLM service.

    Provider construction is isolated through the
    LLMProviderFactory.

    The LLMService remains provider-independent.
    """

    llm_provider = (
        LLMProviderFactory.create()
    )

    return LLMService(
        llm_provider
    )


def create_companion_service(
    character,
) -> CompanionService:
    """
    Create the application-level CompanionService.

    This function is the API composition boundary.

    Infrastructure dependencies are created here and
    injected into application services and agents.

    The application service remains unaware of:

    - OpenAI
    - Ollama
    - provider SDKs
    - API credentials
    """

    llm_service = create_llm_service()

    conversation_agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=character,
        llm_service=llm_service,
    )

    return CompanionService(
        character=character,
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
    )


@router.get("/characters")
def get_characters():
    """
    Return available AI companions.
    """

    characters = (
        character_registry.list_characters()
    )

    return {
        "characters": [
            {
                "id": character.character_id,
                "name": character.name,
                "description": character.description,
                "personality": character.personality,
                "learning_style": character.learning_style,
            }
            for character in characters
        ]
    }


@router.post("/chat")
async def chat(
    payload: dict,
):
    """
    Execute an EasyChatBot companion interaction.
    """

    message = payload.get(
        "message"
    )

    if not message:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="message is required",
        )

    character_id = payload.get(
        "character",
        "yeti",
    )

    try:
        character = (
            character_registry.get(
                character_id
            )
        )

    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc

    companion_service = (
        create_companion_service(
            character
        )
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id=payload.get(
            "user_id",
            "demo-user",
        ),
        request=message,
    )

    result = await companion_service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=payload.get(
            "research",
            False,
        ),
    )

    return result

"""
Character Profile Model.

Defines AI companion identity.

Characters are separate from agents.

Character:
- personality
- communication style
- learning approach

Agent:
- execution capability
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CharacterProfile:
    """
    Defines an EasyChatBot AI companion.
    """

    character_id: str

    name: str

    description: str

    personality: str

    learning_style: str

============================================================
FILE: backend\characters\character_registry.py

"""
EasyChatBot Character Registry.

The registry provides the approved characters available
to the application.

Characters define identity and behaviour.

Agents remain responsible for capabilities and execution.
"""

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)


class CharacterRegistry:
    """
    Registry of approved EasyChatBot AI companions.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._characters = {
            "yeti": CharacterProfile(
                character_id="yeti",
                name="Yeti",
                description=(
                    "A friendly AI companion who helps "
                    "you discover knowledge and practice languages."
                ),
                personality=(
                    "Curious, encouraging and supportive."
                ),
                learning_style=(
                    "Conversation based learning."
                ),
            ),
            "clara": CharacterProfile(
                character_id="clara",
                name="Clara",
                description=(
                    "A professional learning companion "
                    "focused on technology and career topics."
                ),
                personality=(
                    "Analytical, structured and supportive."
                ),
                learning_style=(
                    "Structured and practical learning."
                ),
            ),
            "luminate": CharacterProfile(
                character_id="luminate",
                name="Luminate",
                description=(
                    "A creative companion for culture, "
                    "ideas and exploration."
                ),
                personality=(
                    "Creative, inspiring and curious."
                ),
                learning_style=(
                    "Exploration and discussion."
                ),
            ),
        }

    def get(
        self,
        character_id: str,
    ) -> CharacterProfile:
        """
        Return an approved character.

        Raises:
            ValueError:
                If the character does not exist.
        """

        normalized_id = character_id.strip().lower()

        try:
            return self._characters[normalized_id]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Unknown character: {character_id}"
            ) from exc

    def list_characters(
        self,
    ) -> list[CharacterProfile]:
        """
        Return all approved characters.
        """

        return list(self._characters.values())

============================================================
FILE: backend\conversation\conversation_request.py
============================================================
"""
Provider-independent conversation request.

Represents the information required to generate a
character-aware conversational response.

This contract belongs to the application/runtime boundary.

It deliberately does not know about:

- Gemini
- OpenAI
- Ollama
- Parallel
- voice
- HTTP
- persistence
- provider SDKs

Architecture:

CharacterProfile
        +
ExecutionContext
        +
Optional IntelligenceContext
        |
        v
ConversationRequest
        |
        v
Future / existing EasyChatBot LLM service

The production EasyChatBot conversation and voice
architecture remains outside this hackathon runtime.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ConversationRequest:
    """
    Immutable provider-independent conversation request.

    Attributes
    ----------
    system_instruction:
        Stable instruction defining how the character
        should behave.

    user_prompt:
        User-facing request to be answered.

    conversation_context:
        Optional contextual information, such as live
        intelligence retrieved by the Research Agent.

    character_id:
        Identifier of the character producing the response.

    character_name:
        Display name of the character.
    """

    system_instruction: str

    user_prompt: str

    conversation_context: str | None = None

    character_id: str = ""

    character_name: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate request invariants.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            self.system_instruction,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "system_instruction must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.user_prompt,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "user_prompt must be a string."
            )

        system_instruction = (
            self.system_instruction.strip()
        )

        user_prompt = (
            self.user_prompt.strip()
        )

        if not system_instruction:
            raise ValueError(
                "system_instruction cannot be empty."
            )

        if not user_prompt:
            raise ValueError(
                "user_prompt cannot be empty."
            )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "system_instruction",
            system_instruction,
        )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "user_prompt",
            user_prompt,
        )

        if self.conversation_context is not None:
            context = (
                self.conversation_context.strip()
            )

            if not context:
                raise ValueError(
                    "conversation_context cannot be blank."
                )

            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "conversation_context",
                context,
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.character_id,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "character_id must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.character_name,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "character_name must be a string."
            )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "character_id",
            self.character_id.strip(),
        )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "character_name",
            self.character_name.strip(),
        )

    @property
    def has_conversation_context(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether contextual information exists.
        """

        return bool(
            self.conversation_context
        )

    @property
    def context_length(self) -> int:
        """
        Return the length of the conversation context.

        Useful for diagnostics and future token estimation.
        """

        if not self.conversation_context:
            return 0

        return len(
            self.conversation_context
        )

    def summary(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        """
        Return a safe diagnostic summary.

        Prompt and context content are deliberately
        excluded from the summary.
        """

        return {
            "character_id": self.character_id,
            "character_name": self.character_name,
            "has_context": str(
                self.has_conversation_context
            ),
            "system_instruction_length": str(
                len(self.system_instruction)
            ),
            "user_prompt_length": str(
                len(self.user_prompt)
            ),
            "context_length": str(
                self.context_length
            ),
        }

"""

============================================================
FILE: backend\intelligence\intelligence_context.py
============================================================
"""
Intelligence Context.

Provider-independent representation of external
information prepared for AI execution.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext
        |
        v
AI Runtime integration
        |
        v
LLMRequest.context

The context must not expose provider-specific
implementation details to the application layer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class IntelligenceSource:
    """
    Normalised external information source.
    """

    title: str
    url: str
    source: str
    content: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.title.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source title cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source URL cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.source.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source name cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class IntelligenceContext:
    """
    Provider-independent intelligence context.

    Represents external information that may be supplied
    to an existing AI conversation.

    This object deliberately does not contain:
    - provider SDK objects
    - API credentials
    - model information
    - runtime state
    - persistence objects
    """

    query: str

    sources: tuple[IntelligenceSource, ...] = field(
        default_factory=tuple,
    )

    summary: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence query cannot be empty."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.sources,
            tuple,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence sources must be a tuple."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.summary,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence summary must be a string."
            )

    @property
    def has_sources(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether external sources are available.
        """

        return bool(self.sources)

    @property
    def source_count(self) -> int:
        """
        Return the number of external sources.
        """

        return len(self.sources)

    @property
    def has_summary(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether a summary is available.
        """

        return bool(
            self.summary.strip()
        )

    def to_context_text(self) -> str:
        """
        Convert intelligence into a safe textual context.

        The result is suitable for eventual inclusion
        in an existing LLMRequest.context field.
        """

        sections: list[str] = [
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE",
            f"Research query: {self.query.strip()}",
        ]

        if self.summary.strip():
            sections.extend(
                (
                    "",
                    "Summary:",
                    self.summary.strip(),
                )
            )

        if self.sources:
            sections.extend(
                (
                    "",
                    "Sources:",
                )
            )

            for index, source in enumerate(
                self.sources,
                start=1,
            ):
                sections.append(
                    f"{index}. {source.title}"
                )
                sections.append(
                    f"   URL: {source.url}"
                )
                sections.append(
                    f"   Source: {source.source}"
                )

                if source.content.strip():
                    sections.append(
                        f"   Content: {source.content.strip()}"
                    )

        return "\n".join(sections)

============================================================
FILE: backend\intelligence\research_service.py
============================================================
"""
Research Service.

Coordinates the Research Agent and exposes a
provider-independent intelligence capability.

Architecture:

Application / Runtime
        |
        v
ResearchService
        |
        v
ResearchAgent
        |
        v
Search Provider
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext

The service does not know about Parallel SDK details.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ResearchService:
    """
    Application-facing service for live research.

    The service coordinates the Research Agent but does
    not implement provider-specific search behaviour.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        agent: ResearchAgent | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.agent = (
            agent
            or ResearchAgent()
        )

    async def research(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        query: str,
    ) -> IntelligenceContext:
        """
        Execute research and return normalized intelligence.

        Args:
            user_id:
                Identifier for the current user/session.

            query:
                Research question.

        Returns:
            Provider-independent IntelligenceContext.

        Raises:
            ValueError:
                If the query is empty.

            RuntimeError:
                If the Research Agent fails.
        """

        if not query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Research query cannot be empty."
            )

        context = ExecutionContext(
            user_id=user_id,
            request=query,
        )

        result = await self.agent.execute(
            context
        )

        if not result.success:
            raise RuntimeError(
                result.error
                or "Research execution failed."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            result.output,
            IntelligenceContext,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "Research Agent did not return "
                "an IntelligenceContext."
            )

        return result.output

"""
Memory Record Model.

Represents information stored
to personalise future interactions.
"""


from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MemoryRecord:
    """
    Single memory item.
    """

    user_id: str

    category: str

    content: str

    created_at: datetime

============================================================
FILE: backend\memory\memory_service.py
============================================================
"""
Memory Service.

Application-layer memory service.

This service adapts the legacy backend MemoryRecord contract
to the hackathon AI Runtime MemoryEntry / MemoryStore
persistence contract.

Architecture:

Application
    |
    v
MemoryService
    |
    v
MemoryEntry / MemoryType
    |
    v
MemoryStore
    |
    v
SQLite

Responsibilities:
- save application memories
- retrieve application memories
- translate application memory categories
- isolate persistence details from callers

Does not:
- perform semantic retrieval
- call an LLM
- execute agents
- make autonomous memory decisions
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from backend.memory.memory_record import (
    MemoryRecord,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


class MemoryService:
    """
    Controls application memory operations.

    The service keeps the existing MemoryRecord interface
    for the backend application while delegating persistence
    to the current AI Runtime MemoryStore.
    """

    DEFAULT_DATABASE_PATH = (
        Path("data") / "memory.db"
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        store: MemoryStore | None = None,
        database_path: str | Path | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the memory service.

        Args:
            store:
                Optional injected MemoryStore.

                Primarily useful for tests and controlled
                application configuration.

            database_path:
                Optional SQLite database path.

                Ignored when an explicit store is supplied.
        """

        if store is not None:
            self.store = store
            return

        path = (
            Path(database_path)
            if database_path is not None
            else self.DEFAULT_DATABASE_PATH
        )

        path.parent.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True,
        )

        self.store = MemoryStore(
            database_path=path
        )

    def remember(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        category: str,
        content: str,
    ) -> MemoryRecord:
        """
        Save an application memory.

        The legacy category is preserved in metadata while
        the persistence layer receives a supported MemoryType.
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not category.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory category cannot be empty."
            )

        if not content.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory content cannot be empty."
            )

        created_at = datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )

        record = MemoryRecord(
            user_id=user_id,
            category=category,
            content=content,
            created_at=created_at,
        )

        memory_entry = MemoryEntry(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=self._to_memory_type(
                category
            ),
            content=content,
            created_at=created_at,
            updated_at=created_at,
            metadata={
                "category": category,
            },
        )

        self.store.save(
            memory_entry
        )

        return record

    def recall(
        self,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> list[MemoryRecord]:
        """
        Retrieve memories for a user.

        Persistence-specific MemoryEntry objects are
        translated back into the application-facing
        MemoryRecord contract.
        """

        entries = self.store.list_for_user(
            user_id
        )

        return [
            self._to_memory_record(entry)
            for entry in entries
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _to_memory_type(
        category: str,
    ) -> MemoryType:
        """
        Translate application categories into the
        supported AI Runtime memory types.

        The original category remains available in
        MemoryEntry.metadata.
        """

        normalized = category.strip().lower()

        if normalized == "saved_item":
            return MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM

        if normalized == "discovery_context":
            return MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT

        return MemoryType.PREFERENCE

    @staticmethod
    def _to_memory_record(
        entry: MemoryEntry,
    ) -> MemoryRecord:
        """
        Translate a persistent MemoryEntry into the
        application-facing MemoryRecord.
        """

        category = entry.metadata.get(
            "category",
            entry.memory_type.value,
        )

        return MemoryRecord(
            user_id=entry.user_id,
            category=category,
            content=entry.content,
            created_at=entry.created_at,
        )

============================================================
FILE: backend\memory\memory_store.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Memory persistence.

This module provides the minimal persistent Memory Store
required by the hackathon.

Architecture:

MemoryService
    |
    v
MemoryStore
    |
    v
SQLite

The store is deliberately small.

It is responsible only for persistence and retrieval of
MemoryEntry objects.

It does not:

- decide what should be remembered
- call an LLM
- perform semantic retrieval
- execute agents
- perform governance decisions
- manage products
- manage vendor links
- process payments
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


class MemoryStore:
    """
    SQLite-backed persistence for hackathon memories.

    The database schema is created automatically when the
    store is initialised.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        database_path: str | Path,
    ) -> None:
        self._database_path = str(database_path)

        self._initialise_database()

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        """
        Create a SQLite connection.
        """

        connection = sqlite3.connect(
            self._database_path
        )

        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row

        return connection

    def _initialise_database(self) -> None:
        """
        Create the Memory table if it does not exist.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
                    memory_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    memory_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    content TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL
                )
                """
            )

            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
                idx_memories_user_id
                ON memories(user_id)
                """
            )

            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
                idx_memories_user_type
                ON memories(user_id, memory_type)
                """
            )

    def save(
        self,
        memory: MemoryEntry,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Persist a MemoryEntry.

        Existing entries with the same memory_id are
        replaced.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                """
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO memories (
                    memory_id,
                    user_id,
                    memory_type,
                    content,
                    created_at,
                    updated_at,
                    metadata_json
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    memory.memory_id,
                    memory.user_id,
                    memory.memory_type.value,
                    memory.content,
                    memory.created_at.isoformat(),
                    memory.updated_at.isoformat(),
                    json.dumps(
                        memory.metadata,
                        ensure_ascii=False,
                    ),
                ),
            )

        return memory

    def get(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> MemoryEntry | None:
        """
        Retrieve a single memory by identifier.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            row = connection.execute(
                """
                SELECT
                    memory_id,
                    user_id,
                    memory_type,
                    content,
                    created_at,
                    updated_at,
                    metadata_json
                FROM memories
                WHERE memory_id = ?
                """,
                (memory_id,),
            ).fetchone()

        if row is None:
            return None

        return self._row_to_memory(row)

    def list_for_user(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        memory_type: MemoryType | None = None,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve memories belonging to a user.

        Results are returned newest first.
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        with self._connect() as connection:
            if memory_type is None:
                rows = connection.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT
                        memory_id,
                        user_id,
                        memory_type,
                        content,
                        created_at,
                        updated_at,
                        metadata_json
                    FROM memories
                    WHERE user_id = ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                    """,
                    (user_id,),
                ).fetchall()

            else:
                rows = connection.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT
                        memory_id,
                        user_id,
                        memory_type,
                        content,
                        created_at,
                        updated_at,
                        metadata_json
                    FROM memories
                    WHERE user_id = ?
                      AND memory_type = ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                    """,
                    (
                        user_id,
                        memory_type.value,
                    ),
                ).fetchall()

        return [
            self._row_to_memory(row)
            for row in rows
        ]

    def delete(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Delete a memory.

        Returns True when a memory was deleted.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            cursor = connection.execute(
                """
                DELETE FROM memories
                WHERE memory_id = ?
                """,
                (memory_id,),
            )

        return cursor.rowcount > 0

    @staticmethod
    def _row_to_memory(
        row: sqlite3.Row,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Convert a SQLite row into a MemoryEntry.
        """

        return MemoryEntry(
            memory_id=row["memory_id"],
            user_id=row["user_id"],
            memory_type=MemoryType(
                row["memory_type"]
            ),
            content=row["content"],
            created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(
                row["created_at"]
            ),
            updated_at=datetime.fromisoformat(
                row["updated_at"]
            ),
            metadata=json.loads(
                row["metadata_json"]
            ),
        )

============================================================
FILE: backend\providers\base_provider.py
============================================================
"""
Base Provider Contract.

Defines the interface for external AI and knowledge providers.

Examples:
- Gemini
- Parallel Search
- Future LLM providers

The Runtime communicates with providers through this contract.

Providers are replaceable implementations.
The Agent layer must remain independent from provider details.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from backend.providers.provider_result import ProviderResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class BaseProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract base class for EasyChatBot providers.

    Providers are responsible for communicating
    with external services.

    They are NOT responsible for:
    - agent decisions
    - workflow orchestration
    - memory management
    - user interaction
    """

    provider_id: str

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider_id: str,
    ):
        """
        Initialise provider.

        Args:
            provider_id:
                Unique provider identifier.
        """

        self.provider_id = provider_id

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute provider operation.

        Args:
            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            ProviderResult containing provider output.
        """

        pass

"""
LLM Provider Configuration.

Defines approved provider configuration
for the hackathon runtime.

This module intentionally keeps configuration
simple. Future production versions may move
this into external configuration management.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os


DEFAULT_PROVIDER = "openai"

DEFAULT_MODEL = "gpt-5-mini"


def get_llm_provider_name() -> str:
    """
    Return configured LLM provider.

    Environment variable:

        EASYCHATBOT_LLM_PROVIDER

    Default:

        openai
    """

    return (
        os.getenv(
            "EASYCHATBOT_LLM_PROVIDER",
            DEFAULT_PROVIDER,
        )
        .strip()
        .lower()
    )


def get_llm_model() -> str:
    """
    Return configured model.

    Environment variable:

        EASYCHATBOT_LLM_MODEL

    Default:

        gpt-5-mini
    """

    return (
        os.getenv(
            "EASYCHATBOT_LLM_MODEL",
            DEFAULT_MODEL,
        )
        .strip()
    )

============================================================
FILE: backend\providers\llm\llm_provider.py
============================================================
"""
LLM Provider Contract.

Defines the provider-independent contract for language
model providers used by the EasyChatBot hackathon runtime.

The contract sits between the Conversation Agent and
concrete LLM provider implementations.

Architecture:

ConversationAgent
        |
        v
ConversationRequest
        |
        v
LLMProvider
        |
        v
Concrete LLM Provider
        |
        v
ProviderResult

Responsibilities:

- define the LLM generation boundary
- keep ConversationAgent independent from provider SDKs
- provide a stable contract for concrete LLM providers

Does not:

- call an external API
- contain provider SDK logic
- select models
- implement routing
- manage memory
- manage conversations
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC
from abc import abstractmethod

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)


class LLMProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract provider contract for language model generation.

    Concrete implementations may use OpenAI, Ollama,
    Gemini, or another approved LLM provider.

    The application and agent layers depend only on
    this contract.
    """

    provider_id: str

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider_id: str,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the LLM provider.

        Args:
            provider_id:
                Stable identifier for the concrete provider.
        """

        if not provider_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "LLM provider_id cannot be empty."
            )

        self.provider_id = provider_id

    @abstractmethod
    async def generate(
        self,
        request: ConversationRequest,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Generate an LLM response.

        Args:
            request:
                Provider-independent conversation request.

        Returns:
            ProviderResult containing the generated
            response or an error.

        Implementations must not expose provider SDK
        objects through the result.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError

============================================================
FILE: backend\providers\llm\llm_provider_factory.py
============================================================
"""
LLM Provider Factory.

Creates approved LLM provider implementations.

The factory is the composition boundary between
application/runtime code and infrastructure providers.

Architecture:

Application/API
        |
        v
LLMProviderFactory
        |
        v
LLMProvider
        |
        +--> OpenAIProvider
        +--> Future OllamaProvider
        +--> Future GeminiProvider


Responsibilities:

- create configured LLM providers
- isolate provider construction
- provide provider independence

Does not:

- execute LLM requests
- build prompts
- manage conversations
- manage memory
- apply routing decisions
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.providers.llm.config import (
    get_llm_model,
)

from backend.providers.llm.llm_provider import (
    LLMProvider,
)

from backend.providers.llm.openai_provider import (
    OpenAIProvider,
)


class LLMProviderFactory:
    """
    Factory responsible for creating LLM providers.

    Provider selection is centralised here so the
    application layer does not depend on concrete
    infrastructure implementations.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def create(
        provider: str = "openai",
    ) -> LLMProvider:
        """
        Create an LLM provider.

        Parameters
        ----------
        provider:
            Provider identifier.

        Supported providers:

        - openai

        Returns
        -------
        LLMProvider
            Configured provider implementation.

        Raises
        ------
        ValueError
            If provider is invalid or unsupported.
        """

        if provider is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider name cannot be empty."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            provider,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider name must be a string."
            )

        provider_name = (
            provider.strip()
            .lower()
        )

        if not provider_name:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider name cannot be empty."
            )

        if provider_name == "openai":
            return OpenAIProvider(
                model=get_llm_model(),
            )

        raise ValueError(
            (
                "Unsupported LLM provider: "
                f"{provider}"
            )
        )

============================================================
FILE: backend\providers\llm\openai_provider.py
============================================================
"""
OpenAI LLM Provider.

Concrete LLM provider implementation for the EasyChatBot
hackathon runtime.

Architecture:

ConversationAgent
        |
        v
ConversationRequest
        |
        v
LLMProvider
        |
        v
OpenAIProvider
        |
        v
OpenAI API
        |
        v
ProviderResult

Responsibilities:

- execute ConversationRequest through OpenAI
- use the configured OpenAI model
- translate the OpenAI response into ProviderResult
- isolate the OpenAI SDK from the application layer

Does not:

- build prompts
- manage characters
- manage memory
- perform research
- perform routing
- modify application state
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os

from openai import AsyncOpenAI

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.llm.llm_provider import (
    LLMProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class OpenAIProvider(LLMProvider):
    """
    OpenAI implementation of the LLMProvider contract.

    The OpenAI SDK is deliberately isolated inside this
    infrastructure adapter.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: str = "gpt-5-mini",
        client: AsyncOpenAI | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the OpenAI provider.

        Args:
            model:
                OpenAI model identifier.

            client:
                Optional injected AsyncOpenAI client.

                Dependency injection allows unit tests to
                provide a fake client without making a real
                API call.
        """

        super().__init__(
            provider_id="openai",
        )

        if not model.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "OpenAI model cannot be empty."
            )

        self.model = model.strip()

        self._client = (
            client
            if client is not None
            else AsyncOpenAI(
                api_key=os.getenv(
                    "OPENAI_API_KEY"
                )
            )
        )

    async def generate(
        self,
        request: ConversationRequest,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Generate a response using OpenAI.

        The provider translates the provider-independent
        ConversationRequest into the OpenAI Responses API
        request.

        Args:
            request:
                Provider-independent conversation request.

        Returns:
            ProviderResult containing the generated text
            or an error description.
        """

        if request is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Conversation request cannot be None."
            )

        messages = [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": request.system_instruction,
            },
        ]

        if request.conversation_context:
            messages.append(
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": (
                        "Additional context:\n"
                        f"{request.conversation_context}"
                    ),
                }
            )

        messages.append(
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": request.user_prompt,
            }
        )

        try:
            response = await self._client.responses.create(
                model=self.model,
                input=messages,
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception(
                "OpenAI provider execution failed."
            )

            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                error=str(exc),
            )

        output_text = (
            getattr(
                response,
                "output_text",
                None,
            )
        )

        if not output_text:
            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                error=(
                    "OpenAI returned an empty response."
                ),
            )

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id=self.provider_id,
            output=output_text,
        )

============================================================
FILE: backend\providers\parallel\parallel_client.py
============================================================
"""
Parallel Search API Client Wrapper.

This module isolates the official Parallel SDK.

The rest of EasyChatBot does not directly depend on
the Parallel SDK implementation.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
Parallel Provider
        |
        v
Parallel Client Wrapper
        |
        v
Parallel API
"""

import asyncio
import os
from typing import Any

from dotenv import load_dotenv


load_dotenv()


class ParallelClientWrapper:
    """
    Wrapper around the official Parallel Search SDK.

    Responsibilities:
    - authenticate with Parallel
    - execute searches
    - normalize SDK responses

    Does not:
    - create learning content
    - manage agents
    - decide workflows
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str | None = None,
    ):
        """
        Initialise the Parallel SDK client.
        """

        self.api_key = (
            api_key
            or os.getenv("PARALLEL_API_KEY")
        )

        if not self.api_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "PARALLEL_API_KEY environment variable is required"
            )

        self._client = self._create_client()


    def _create_client(self):
        """
        Create the official Parallel SDK client.

        SDK:
            parallel-web 1.1.0
        """

        from parallel import Parallel

        return Parallel(
            api_key=self.api_key
        )


    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_response(
        response: Any,
    ) -> Any:
        """
        Convert the Parallel SDK response into
        application-safe data.

        Pydantic SDK models are converted to dictionaries.
        Other JSON-compatible values are returned unchanged.
        """

        if hasattr(response, "model_dump"):
            return response.model_dump()

        if hasattr(response, "dict"):
            return response.dict()

        return response


    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Execute a Parallel web search.

        Args:
            query:
                Natural-language research objective.

        Returns:
            Normalized application-level search result.
        """

        search_query = self._build_search_query(
            query
        )

        response = await asyncio.to_thread(
            self._client.search,
            search_queries=[
                search_query
            ],
            mode="advanced",
            objective=query,
        )

        return {
            "query": query,
            "search_queries": [
                search_query
            ],
            "results": self._normalize_response(
                response
            ),
        }


    @staticmethod
    def _build_search_query(
        query: str,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build a concise search query from the
        user's research request.

        The full request remains the Parallel
        objective while the search query is kept
        concise.
        """

        words = query.strip().split()

        if len(words) <= 6:
            return query.strip()

        return " ".join(words[:6])

============================================================
FILE: backend\providers\parallel\parallel_provider.py
============================================================
"""
Parallel Search Provider.

Provides live world information
through the Parallel Search API.
"""

from backend.providers.base_provider import (
    BaseProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ParallelSearchProvider(BaseProvider):
    """
    Provider implementation using Parallel Search.

    The SDK client is created only when required.
    This allows tests to run without API credentials.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        client=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            provider_id="parallel_search"
        )

        self.client = client


    def _get_client(self):
        """
        Lazy-load the real Parallel client.

        Avoids requiring API keys during unit tests.
        """

        if self.client is None:

            from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
                ParallelClientWrapper,
            )

            self.client = ParallelClientWrapper()

        return self.client


    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute a live Parallel search.
        """

        try:

            client = self._get_client()

            result = await client.search(
                query=context.request
            )

            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=result,
            )

        except Exception as exc:

            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=None,
                error=str(exc),
            )

============================================================
FILE: backend\providers\provider_result.py
============================================================
"""
Provider Result Contract.

Defines the standard response returned
by external providers.

All providers return this structure so
the Runtime can process results consistently.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderResult:
    """
    Immutable provider execution result.

    Attributes:

        success:
            Indicates whether provider execution succeeded.

        provider_id:
            Identifier of the provider.

        output:
            Provider response payload.

        error:
            Error details if execution failed.
    """

    success: bool
    provider_id: str
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None

============================================================
FILE: backend\runtime\context\execution_context.py
============================================================
"""
Runtime Execution Context.

Provides immutable runtime context shared with agents.

Responsibilities:
- identify an execution
- carry user request information
- provide runtime metadata
- optionally carry live intelligence context

Does not:
- execute providers
- manage memory
- modify systems
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExecutionContext:
    """
    Runtime execution context shared with agents.

    Attributes
    ----------
    user_id:
        Identifier of the user initiating the execution.

    request:
        User request being processed.

    execution_id:
        Unique identifier for this execution.

    created_at:
        UTC timestamp when the context was created.

    metadata:
        Runtime metadata.

    intelligence_context:
        Optional provider-independent live intelligence
        generated by the Research Agent.

    Responsibilities:
    - identify an execution
    - carry user request information
    - provide runtime metadata
    - optionally carry live intelligence context

    Does not:
    - execute providers
    - manage memory
    - modify systems
    """

    user_id: str

    request: str

    execution_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    created_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )

    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    intelligence_context: Any | None = None

    @property
    def has_intelligence_context(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether live intelligence is attached
        to this execution.
        """

        return self.intelligence_context is not None

"""
Model Identifier.

Defines a stable identifier for AI models.

The identifier is independent from provider execution logic.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelId:
    """
    Unique identifier for an AI model.

    Examples:

    gemini-2.5-flash
    gpt-5-mini
    llama3

    """

    provider: str
    name: str

    def full_name(self) -> str:
        """
        Returns the complete model identifier.
        """

        return f"{self.provider}:{self.name}"

============================================================
FILE: backend\runtime\runtime_engine.py

"""
EasyChatBot Runtime Engine.

The Runtime is responsible for orchestrating
agent execution.

Responsibilities:
- receive execution requests
- provide execution context
- call approved agents
- return structured results

The Runtime does not:
- implement business logic
- call providers directly
- manage user interfaces
"""

from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class RuntimeEngine:
    """
    Central execution orchestrator.

    Agents execute through the Runtime.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        agent: BaseAgent,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute an agent using runtime control.

        Args:
            agent:
                Agent implementation.

            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            AgentResult.
        """

        return await agent.execute(context)

FILE: backend\services\__init__.py
