    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[DiscussionQuestion]:

        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la réglementation de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                ),
                difficulty=learner_level,
            ),
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Quels sont les avantages d'une "
                    "réglementation européenne de l'IA ?"
                ),
                difficulty=learner_level,
            ),
        ]


def create_governance_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Governance policy for the live intelligence demo.

    Live research may access external information but
    must never create external side effects.
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


async def create_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Execute the H1 learning challenge flow.
    """

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    return await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )


async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete hackathon demonstration.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EasyChatBot — HACKATHON DEMONSTRATION")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 1 — Governance
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 1 — GOVERNANCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    governance_engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    governance_result = governance_engine.evaluate(
        create_governance_policy()
    )

    print(
        f"Capability: {governance_result.capability}"
    )

    print(
        f"Risk level: "
        f"{governance_result.risk_level.value}"
    )

    print(
        f"Decision: "
        f"{governance_result.decision.value.upper()}"
    )

    if governance_result.decision != GovernanceDecision.ALLOW:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Hackathon demo stopped because governance "
            "did not allow the capability."
        )

    print("Governance check: PASSED")
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 2 — Live Intelligence + Challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 2 — LIVE INTELLIGENCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    challenge = await create_learning_challenge()

    print(f"Topic: {challenge.topic}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Learner level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Summary:")
    print(challenge.summary)
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  • {item.term}: {item.definition}")

    print()

    print("Discussion questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(
            f"  {index}. {question.question}"
        )

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 3 — Quality Evaluation
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 3 — AI QUALITY EVALUATION")
    print("-" * 72)

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    report = EvaluationReport().generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4 — PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY")
    print("-" * 72)

    # Build the same domain inputs that a real discovery
    # application flow would receive.
    demo_deal = build_demo_deal()
    demo_memory = build_demo_memory()

    discovery_request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
        deal=demo_deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    flash_deal_card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        discovery_request,
        memories=[demo_memory],
    )

    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card(
            flash_deal_card
        )
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4A — CHARACTER DISCOVERY NOTIFICATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    discovery_notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="demo-notification-001",
            user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
            deal=flash_deal_card,
        )
    )

    print()
    print("CHARACTER NOTIFICATION")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(
        discovery_notification.render()
    )
    print()

    assert (
        discovery_notification.user_id
        == demo_memory.user_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.character_id
        == flash_deal_card.character_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.deal
        is flash_deal_card
    )

    assert flash_deal_card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert flash_deal_card.deal_id == demo_deal.deal_id
    assert flash_deal_card.product_name
    assert demo_memory.user_id == "demo-user"
    assert flash_deal_card.relevance_reason
    assert flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    assert flash_deal_card.more_information_url

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION")
    print("-" * 72)

    commerce_action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=flash_deal_card,
    )

    print(
        f"Product: {flash_deal_card.product_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vendor: {flash_deal_card.vendor_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Commerce action: "
        f"{commerce_action.action_type.value.upper()}"
    )

    commerce_result = CommerceService().initiate(
        commerce_action
    )

    print(
        f"Status: "
        f"{commerce_result.status.value.upper()}"
    )

    print()
    print("GET DEAL")
    print("  -> Approved commerce boundary")
    print("  -> Vendor handoff")
    print()
    print(
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
    )

    assert (
        commerce_result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.deal_id
        == flash_deal_card.deal_id
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.next_url
        == flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 6 — Final result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS")
        print()
        # ASCII-safe output keeps the executable reliable when
        # stdout is captured by Windows pytest/subprocess.
        # Preserve the stable hackathon readiness marker.
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        )
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
            "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        )
        print(
            "All demonstrated capabilities completed successfully."
        )
    else:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: QUALITY CHECK FAILED")

    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    assert governance_result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert evaluation_result.passed is True


def main() -> None:
    """
    Application entry point.
    """

    asyncio.run(run_demo())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# Integration Notes

This slice is intentionally isolated from the already-green commerce and
Flash Deal flows.

Before changing the main judge demo:

1. Run `pytest tests/ai_runtime/podcast -v`
2. Run `python examples/podcast_personalisation_demo.py`
3. Review the language-filter tests
4. Only then integrate the podcast vertical slice into the judge demo.

The intended architecture is:

Memory -> Podcast Agent -> Search Intent -> Provider Layer
-> Normalised Results -> Recommendation Service -> Character -> User Choice

Do not let the Agent call Apple/Spotify directly.
Do not let providers access Memory.
Do not let the character perform search.
Do not add Spotify user-library OAuth in this MVP.

pythonpath = .
testpaths = tests
asyncio_mode = auto

# EasyChatBot — AI Learning Intelligence Platform

EasyChatBot is an AI-powered learning platform that combines **live intelligence, personalised learning content, AI quality evaluation, and explicit AI governance controls**.

For the hackathon, the project demonstrates a complete vertical flow:

```text
User Request
     ↓
Governance
     ↓
Live Intelligence
     ↓
Learning Challenge
     ↓
AI Quality Evaluation
     ↓
Final Result
```

The implementation is deliberately modular: live intelligence, evaluation, governance, and the final demonstration remain separate capabilities that can evolve independently.

---

## Hackathon Demonstration

The main executable demonstration is:

```text
examples/hackathon_demo.py
```

Run it with:

```powershell
python examples/hackathon_demo.py
```

A successful execution ends with:

```text
HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS
```

and demonstrates:

```text
Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation
```

---

# 1. What the Project Demonstrates

## 1.1 Live Intelligence

EasyChatBot can use live web intelligence to transform a current topic into learning material.

The hackathon implementation includes:

* provider-independent search abstraction
* Parallel Search provider integration
* response normalisation
* live intelligence processing
* learning challenge generation
* vocabulary generation
* discussion-question generation

The Parallel integration is isolated behind the provider layer rather than exposing the external SDK throughout the application.

Relevant implementation:

```text
src/providers/search_provider/
src/ai_agents/live_intelligence_agent/
```

Demonstration:

```text
examples/live_challenge_demo.py
```

---

# 2. AI Quality Evaluation

The project includes an independent evaluation workstream for assessing generated learning challenges.

The evaluation architecture is:

```text
Learning Challenge
        ↓
Deterministic Evaluator
        ↓
Criterion Scores
        ↓
Evaluation Engine
        ↓
Evaluation Result
        ↓
Quality Report
```

Current evaluation dimensions include:

* completeness
* language level
* educational value
* source coverage metadata

The evaluation engine applies explicit criterion weights and produces a normalised overall score.

Relevant implementation:

```text
src/evaluation/
```

Demonstration:

```text
examples/evaluation_demo.py
```

Example output:

```text
Overall Score: 1.00
Status: PASS
Evaluator: deterministic
```

### Evaluation Scope

The current deterministic evaluator intentionally does **not** claim to prove factual correctness.

Factuality verification requires evaluation against the underlying research evidence and is therefore outside the current deterministic structural evaluator.

This limitation is explicit rather than hidden.

---

# 3. AI Safety & Governance

EasyChatBot includes a separate governance workstream for controlling AI capabilities.

The governance model is:

```text
AI Capability
      ↓
Governance Policy
      ↓
Governance Policy Engine
      ↓
ALLOW / REVIEW / BLOCK
```

The current decision model is:

```text
Uncontrolled side effects
        ↓
      BLOCK

Critical risk
        ↓
      BLOCK

High risk
        ↓
     REVIEW

Explicit human review
        ↓
     REVIEW

Permitted capability
        ↓
      ALLOW
```

This creates an explicit control boundary before an AI capability is allowed to proceed.

Relevant implementation:

```text
src/governance/
```

Demonstration:

```text
examples/governance_demo.py
```

---

# 4. Unified Hackathon Flow

The unified demonstration combines the independently implemented capabilities.

```text
                 ┌────────────────────┐
                 │    User Request     │
                 └─────────┬──────────┘
                           ↓
                 ┌────────────────────┐
                 │     Governance     │
                 │   ALLOW / REVIEW   │
                 │       / BLOCK      │
                 └─────────┬──────────┘
                           ↓
                 ┌────────────────────┐
                 │ Live Intelligence │
                 │  Web Intelligence  │
                 └─────────┬──────────┘
                           ↓
                 ┌────────────────────┐
                 │ Learning Challenge │
                 │ Vocabulary + Q&A   │
                 └─────────┬──────────┘
                           ↓
                 ┌────────────────────┐
                 │ Quality Evaluation │
                 │ Score + Criteria   │
                 └─────────┬──────────┘
                           ↓
                 ┌────────────────────┐
                 │   Final Result     │
                 └────────────────────┘
```

The unified demonstration intentionally uses controlled demo providers so that the hackathon presentation is deterministic and does not depend on external credentials.

The real Parallel integration is validated independently through integration tests.

---

# 5. Architecture

The hackathon implementation preserves separation between the major responsibilities.

## Live Intelligence

```text
Live Intelligence Agent
        ↓
Search Provider Interface
        ↓
Parallel Provider Adapter
        ↓
Parallel API
```

The provider abstraction prevents the rest of the application from depending directly on the Parallel SDK.

## Evaluation

```text
Learning Challenge
        ↓
Deterministic Evaluator
        ↓
Criterion Scores
        ↓
Evaluation Engine
        ↓
Evaluation Result
        ↓
Quality Report
```

## Governance

```text
AI Capability
        ↓
Governance Policy
        ↓
Governance Policy Engine
        ↓
Governance Result
```

## Demonstration

```text
Governance
    ↓
Live Intelligence
    ↓
Learning Challenge
    ↓
Quality Evaluation
```

The unified demo orchestrates these capabilities without merging their responsibilities.

# 10. Real Parallel Integration

The Parallel provider is tested independently from the deterministic hackathon demo.

The provider abstraction allows the application to consume normalised search results without depending on the external SDK's response format.

The real provider test requires:

```text
PARALLEL_API_KEY
```

The deterministic unified demonstration does not require the external provider.

This separation provides both:

1. real external-provider validation
2. repeatable hackathon demonstration

---

# 11. Security & Governance Principles

The hackathon implementation follows several explicit principles:

* API credentials are loaded through environment configuration.
* Secrets are not hard-coded.
* External providers are isolated behind interfaces.
* AI capabilities are subject to explicit governance policies.
* Uncontrolled external side effects are blocked.
* High-risk capabilities require review.
* Critical-risk capabilities are blocked.
* The deterministic demonstration does not create external side effects.
* Evaluation does not modify the evaluated challenge.

---

# 12. Current Scope

The hackathon implementation focuses on demonstrating the core vertical slice.

### Included

* Live intelligence
* Parallel provider integration
* Learning challenge generation
* AI quality evaluation
* Governance policy enforcement
* Unified demonstration
* Automated validation

### Intentionally out of scope

* unrestricted autonomous agents
* automatic production code modification
* uncontrolled production side effects
* full enterprise governance platform
* production learner-facing UI
* complete factuality verification
* unrestricted multi-agent collaboration

These limitations are deliberate and keep the demonstrated architecture controlled and testable.

---

# 13. Hackathon Story

The central idea is:

> **AI should not only generate learning content. It should be able to use live information, transform that information into a useful learning experience, evaluate the quality of what it produced, and operate within explicit governance controls.**

EasyChatBot demonstrates this as one executable flow:

```text
Govern
   ↓
Research
   ↓
Teach
   ↓
Evaluate
```

The result is a learning system that treats **intelligence, quality, and governance as connected but separate engineering capabilities**.

# EasyChatBot — Podcast Learning Challenge MVP

## User experience

```text
Memory + Learning World
        |
        v
Podcast Agent
        |
        v
Apple / Spotify
        |
        v
Language + preference filtering
        |
        v
Character recommendation
        |
        v
+-----------------------------+
| 🎧 PODCAST FIND             |
|                             |
| [ LISTEN ]                  |
| [ START CHALLENGE ]         |
+-----------------------------+
        |
        v
PodcastLearningChallenge
        |
        +--> Vocabulary
        +--> Comprehension
        +--> Discussion
        |
        v
ChallengeResult
```

## Scope

This is a hackathon MVP. The challenge is created from **verified podcast
metadata** and the learner's explicit language/level context.

It does not claim to have listened to or transcribed the podcast.

Out of scope:

- audio download
- transcription
- transcript semantic search
- autonomous playback
- Spotify user history
- subscriptions
- persistent memory writes

## Validation

Run:

```powershell
pytest tests/ai_runtime/podcast -v
```

Then:

```powershell
python src/ai_runtime/podcast/challenge_demo.py
```

The demo is deterministic and does not require network access.


============================================================
FILE: README_PODCAST_MVP.md
============================================================
# EasyChatBot — Personalised Podcast Discovery MVP

## Canonical flow

```text
Memory + Learning World
        |
        v
PodcastDiscoveryAgent
        |
        v
PodcastSearchIntent
        |
        v
Apple / Spotify Provider Layer
        |
        v
Normalised PodcastSearchResult
        |
        v
PodcastRecommendationService
        |
        +--> HARD TARGET-LANGUAGE FILTER
        +--> USER-PREFERENCE MATCHING
        +--> LEARNING-WORLD RELEVANCE
        |
        v
PodcastRecommendation
        |
        v
Character
        |
        v
LISTEN / MORE INFORMATION
```

## Critical product rule

EasyChatBot is a language-learning platform. Therefore the target
language is a **hard eligibility constraint**, not merely a search term.

A French World learner must not receive an English podcast simply because
the topic matches.

Unknown-language candidates are also rejected when `language_required=True`.

## Provider independence

The Agent does not know whether Apple or Spotify is used. It produces
`PodcastSearchIntent`; providers translate that intent into their own
API syntax and return normalised `PodcastSearchResult` objects.

## Memory

Only explicit `MemoryType.PREFERENCE` memories are treated as preferences,
consistent with the existing discovery implementation. Optional metadata:

```text
topics=artificial intelligence, European regulation
keywords=technology,current affairs
```

Agents do not write persistent memory.

## Apple

The Apple adapter uses the iTunes Search API with `media=podcast`.

Apple documents approximately 20 Search API calls/minute and recommends
limiting results and caching.

## Spotify

The MVP searches Spotify **episodes**, because episode metadata exposes
`languages`. User-specific Spotify library/playback access is out of scope.

Use an injected access token. Credential acquisition belongs outside this
adapter. Spotify documents Client Credentials for server-to-server access
when user information is not required.

## Security / governance

The podcast capability is read-only. It does not:

- subscribe to podcasts
- download audio
- modify Spotify libraries
- start playback
- write memory
- execute purchases

The user remains responsible for the final LISTEN action.

## Validation

```powershell
pytest tests/ai_runtime/podcast -v
python examples/podcast_personalisation_demo.py
```

The tests and demo are deterministic and do not require network access.


============================================================
FILE: src\ai_agents\live_intelligence_agent\handler.py
============================================================
"""
Live Intelligence Agent Handler.

Responsible for executing the Live Intelligence Agent workflow.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Live Intelligence Agent Handler
    |
    +--> Search Provider Interface
    |
    +--> Knowledge Processing
    |
    v
Structured Intelligence Output

Responsibilities:
- Validate agent input
- Call approved provider interfaces
- Normalise research output
- Return explicit structured results

Non-responsibilities:
- Provider SDK management
- Direct external API handling
- Autonomous actions
- Configuration changes
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Protocol

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceRequest:
    """
    Input contract for Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    language: str = "French"
    learner_level: str = "B2"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceResponse:
    """
    Output contract from Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    summary: str
    sources: List[SearchResult]
    key_information: List[str]


class IntelligenceProcessor(Protocol):
    """
    Contract for future LLM-based processing.

    Keeps the agent independent from
    any specific model/provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: List[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, List[str]]:
        ...


class LiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Live Intelligence Agent implementation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        search_provider: SearchProvider,
        processor: IntelligenceProcessor,
    ) -> None:
        self._search_provider = search_provider
        self._processor = processor

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceResponse:
        """
        Execute research workflow.

        Flow:

        Request
          |
          v
        Search Provider
          |
          v
        Intelligence Processor
          |
          v
        Structured Response
        """

        self._validate_request(request)

        search_results = await self._search_provider.search(
            query=request.topic,
            max_results=5,
        )

        summary, key_information = await self._processor.summarise(
            results=search_results,
            language=request.language,
            learner_level=request.learner_level,
        )

        return LiveIntelligenceResponse(
            topic=request.topic,
            summary=summary,
            sources=search_results,
            key_information=key_information,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_request(
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate required input.
        """

        if not request.topic.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Topic cannot be empty"
            )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\capabilities\errors.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime capability errors.

These exceptions provide explicit Runtime-level failure semantics.

The Runtime must distinguish between:

- invalid requests
- unknown capabilities
- duplicate capability registration
- governance rejection
- capability execution failure

Provider-specific exceptions must not leak through the Runtime
boundary.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


class RuntimeCapabilityError(Exception):
    """
    Base exception for Runtime capability failures.
    """


class InvalidCapabilityRequestError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when a capability request is invalid.
    """


class CapabilityNotRegisteredError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when no executor is registered for a capability.
    """


class CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when an executor is registered more than once.
    """


class CapabilityExecutionError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when a capability executor fails during execution.

    The original provider/implementation exception is retained
    as the exception cause, but callers receive a Runtime-level
    error type.
    """


class CapabilityGovernanceError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when governance evaluation itself fails.

    This is deliberately different from a governance rejection.

    Rejection:
        governance worked and said no.

    Governance error:
        governance could not produce a valid decision.
    """

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\capabilities\executor.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime capability execution contract.

This module defines the interface between the AI Runtime
and concrete capability executors.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Capability Executor Interface
    |
    +---- Live Intelligence Executor
    +---- Learning Executor
    +---- Evaluation Executor
    +---- Future Executors

The Runtime depends on this contract.

It does not depend directly on:
- agents
- providers
- SDKs
- external APIs

Concrete executors are responsible for translating the
provider-independent CapabilityRequest into the appropriate
capability implementation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class CapabilityExecutor(ABC):
    """
    Abstract executor for one Runtime capability.

    Each concrete executor must declare which capability
    it handles and implement asynchronous execution.
    """

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        """
        Return the capability handled by this executor.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Execute a capability request.

        Implementations must return a provider-independent
        CapabilityResult.

        They must not bypass the Runtime governance boundary.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\capabilities\gateway.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime Capability Gateway.

Controlled execution boundary between applications and
concrete AI capabilities.

Flow:

Application
    ↓
CapabilityRequest
    ↓
CapabilityGateway
    ↓
Governance
    ↓
CapabilityRegistry
    ↓
CapabilityExecutor
    ↓
CapabilityResult
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class GovernanceDecisionResult(Protocol):
    """
    Minimal governance result required by the Runtime.
    """

    @property
    def allowed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Whether execution is permitted.
        """
        ...


class CapabilityGovernance(Protocol):
    """
    Governance boundary required by the Runtime gateway.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> GovernanceDecisionResult:
        """
        Evaluate whether a capability may execute.
        """
        ...


class CapabilityGateway:
    """
    Controlled Runtime entry point for capability execution.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        registry: CapabilityRegistry,
        governance: CapabilityGovernance,
    ) -> None:
        self._registry = registry
        self._governance = governance

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Execute an approved capability.

        Governance is evaluated before resolving and executing
        the capability.
        """

        try:
            governance_result = (
                self._governance.evaluate(request)
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise CapabilityGovernanceError(
                "Governance evaluation failed."
            ) from exc

        if not governance_result.allowed:
            return CapabilityResult(
                request_id=request.request_id,
                capability=request.capability,
                status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED,
                error=(
                    "Capability execution was rejected "
                    "by governance."
                ),
                metadata={
                    "governance_decision": "rejected",
                },
            )

        executor = self._registry.resolve(
            request.capability
        )

        try:
            return await executor.execute(request)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise CapabilityExecutionError(
                "Capability executor failed: "
                f"{request.capability.value}"
            ) from exc

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\capabilities\live_intelligence_executor.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime executor for Live Intelligence.

This adapter bridges the provider-independent AI Runtime
capability contract with the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
CapabilityRequest
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceExecutor
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceAgent
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceResponse

The adapter:

- validates the Runtime capability
- translates Runtime payloads into LiveIntelligenceRequest
- invokes the existing Live Intelligence Agent
- translates the agent response into CapabilityResult

It does not:

- implement intelligence logic
- call Parallel directly
- modify the LiveIntelligenceAgent
- perform governance decisions
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Runtime adapter for the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        agent: LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    ) -> None:
        self._agent = agent

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        """
        Return the capability handled by this executor.
        """

        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Translate and execute a Live Intelligence request.
        """

        if (
            request.capability
            != CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "LiveIntelligenceExecutor received "
                "an unsupported capability: "
                f"{request.capability.value}"
            )

        agent_request = (
            self._build_agent_request(request)
        )

        response = await self._agent.execute(
            agent_request
        )

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output=response,
            metadata={
                "executor": (
                    "live_intelligence_executor"
                ),
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_agent_request(
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceRequest:
        """
        Translate Runtime payload into the existing
        LiveIntelligenceRequest contract.
        """

        payload = request.payload

        topic = payload.get("topic")

        if not isinstance(topic, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence capability requires "
                "a string 'topic'."
            )

        language = payload.get(
            "language",
            "French",
        )

        learner_level = payload.get(
            "learner_level",
            "B2",
        )

        if not isinstance(language, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'language' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            learner_level,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'learner_level' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        return LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic=topic,
            language=language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
        )

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\capabilities\models.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime capability contracts.

This module defines the provider-independent contract used by
the AI Runtime to request execution of an approved capability.

Architecture:

Application
    |
    v
AI Runtime
    |
    v
Capability Request
    |
    v
Governance
    |
    v
Capability Execution

This module contains contracts only.

It does not:
- execute agents
- call providers
- evaluate governance policies
- perform external side effects
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any, Mapping


class CapabilityType(StrEnum):
    """
    Supported runtime capability categories.

    The list is intentionally small for the initial framework
    implementation. New capabilities should be added explicitly.
    """

    LIVE_INTELLIGENCE = "live_intelligence"
    LEARNING_GENERATION = "learning_generation"
    EVALUATION = "evaluation"


class CapabilityExecutionStatus(StrEnum):
    """
    Runtime-level execution status.
    """

    ACCEPTED = "accepted"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    REJECTED = "rejected"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Provider-independent request submitted to the AI Runtime.

    Attributes:
        capability:
            Logical capability requested by the application.

        request_id:
            Correlation identifier for the execution.

        payload:
            Explicit capability input.

        metadata:
            Non-business execution metadata.

        requested_by:
            Component or application requesting execution.
    """

    capability: CapabilityType
    request_id: str
    payload: Mapping[str, Any]
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )
    requested_by: str = "application"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.request_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability request_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.requested_by.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability requested_by cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CapabilityResult:
    """
    Provider-independent result returned by the AI Runtime.

    The result contains execution information but does not expose
    provider-specific response objects.
    """

    request_id: str
    capability: CapabilityType
    status: CapabilityExecutionStatus
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    @property
    def succeeded(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution completed successfully.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
        )

    @property
    def failed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution failed.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.FAILED
        )

    @property
    def rejected(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution was rejected before execution.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
        )

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\capabilities\registry.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime capability registry.

The registry maps logical Runtime capabilities to their
concrete executors.

The registry:
- resolves executors by capability
- prevents duplicate registrations
- provides explicit dependency injection
- does not execute capabilities
- does not perform governance decisions
- does not know about providers
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityType,
)


class CapabilityRegistry:
    """
    Dependency-injection registry for Runtime capabilities.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        executors: list[CapabilityExecutor] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._executors: dict[
            CapabilityType,
            CapabilityExecutor,
        ] = {}

        for executor in executors or []:
            self.register(executor)

    def register(
        self,
        executor: CapabilityExecutor,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Register an executor for its declared capability.
        """

        capability = executor.capability

        if capability in self._executors:
            raise CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError(
                "Capability executor already registered: "
                f"{capability.value}"
            )

        self._executors[capability] = executor

    def resolve(
        self,
        capability: CapabilityType,
    ) -> CapabilityExecutor:
        """
        Resolve the executor responsible for a capability.
        """

        try:
            return self._executors[capability]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise CapabilityNotRegisteredError(
                "No executor registered for capability: "
                f"{capability.value}"
            ) from exc

    def contains(
        self,
        capability: CapabilityType,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether an executor is registered.
        """

        return capability in self._executors

    def capabilities(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[CapabilityType, ...]:
        """
        Return all registered capabilities.
        """

        return tuple(self._executors.keys())

FILE: src\ai_runtime\commerce\flash_deal_commerce.py
============================================================
"""
Flash Deal commerce integration boundary.

Converts a user-facing FlashDealCard into explicit commerce
contracts.

Architecture:

FlashDealCard
      |
      v
FlashDealCommerce
      |
      +----> CommerceAction
      |
      +----> PaymentPass
                    |
                    v
             CommerceService
                    |
                    v
             Vendor / Checkout

This module does not:
- process payments
- store payment information
- call vendor APIs
- execute autonomous purchases
- decide whether a product should be recommended

The discovery layer remains responsible for discovery.
The commerce layer remains responsible for commerce.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentPass,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


class FlashDealCommerce:
    """
    Application boundary between a FlashDealCard and
    the commerce subsystem.
    """

    def create_action(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> CommerceAction:
        """
        Create an explicit purchase action from a Flash Deal.

        No purchase occurs here.
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        return CommerceAction(
            user_id=user_id,
            deal_id=card.deal_id,
            action_type=(
                CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
            ),
            destination_url=card.get_deal_url,
            metadata={
                "source": "flash_deal_card",
                "character_id": card.character_id,
                "vendor": card.vendor_name,
            },
        )

    def create_payment_pass(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        card: FlashDealCard,
        payment_pass_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> PaymentPass:
        """
        Create a demo-only payment handoff from a Flash Deal.

        No payment occurs here.

        The resulting PaymentPass is always:
        - READY
        - DEMO
        - not executed
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        return PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id=(
                payment_pass_id
                if payment_pass_id is not None
                else f"payment-pass-{card.deal_id}"
            ),
            deal_id=card.deal_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            product_name=card.product_name,
            vendor_name=card.vendor_name,
            amount=card.current_price,
            currency=card.currency,
            checkout_url=card.get_deal_url,
        )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\commerce\models.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon commerce contracts.

These contracts define the boundary between EasyChatBot's
discovery experience and an eventual commerce/payment
integration.

EasyChatBot does not implement a marketplace here.

The external commerce provider is responsible for the
actual payment and vendor transaction.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from uuid import uuid4


class CommerceActionType(str, Enum):
    """
    Supported commerce actions.
    """

    VIEW_PRODUCT = "view_product"

    INITIATE_PURCHASE = "initiate_purchase"


class CommerceActionStatus(str, Enum):
    """
    Result status of a commerce action.
    """

    READY = "ready"

    REDIRECT = "redirect"

    REJECTED = "rejected"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommerceAction:
    """
    Explicit request to perform a commerce action.

    This is an application-level contract.

    It is intentionally independent from Stripe, Amazon,
    OLX, or any other vendor.
    """

    user_id: str

    deal_id: str

    action_type: CommerceActionType

    destination_url: str

    action_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.deal_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action deal_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.destination_url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action destination URL "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not (
            self.destination_url.startswith(
                "https://"
            )
            or self.destination_url.startswith(
                "http://"
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action destination URL "
                "must use HTTP or HTTPS."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommerceActionResult:
    """
    Provider-independent result of a commerce action.
    """

    action_id: str

    deal_id: str

    status: CommerceActionStatus

    next_url: str | None = None

    message: str | None = None

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\commerce\payment_pass.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Payment Pass.

This module defines the minimal payment handoff contract required
for the EasyChatBot hackathon.

Architecture:

FlashDealCard
    |
    v
CommerceService
    |
    v
PaymentPass
    |
    v
Vendor / Checkout URL

The PaymentPass is a DEMO-ONLY checkout handoff.

It does not:
- execute payments
- call payment providers
- store payment credentials
- store card numbers
- store bank information
- create payment transactions
- confirm a purchase
- perform autonomous commerce actions

Important invariant:

PaymentPass != PaymentTransaction

The hackathon can demonstrate that a checkout/payment journey is
ready without actually charging the user.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum


class PaymentPassStatus(str, Enum):
    """Status of the demo payment handoff."""

    READY = "READY"


class PaymentExecutionMode(str, Enum):
    """Execution mode for the hackathon payment boundary."""

    DEMO = "DEMO"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PaymentPass:
    """
    Immutable payment handoff contract for the hackathon.

    This object represents a checkout journey that is ready to be
    handed to the vendor. It does not represent an executed payment.
    """

    payment_pass_id: str
    deal_id: str
    user_id: str

    product_name: str
    vendor_name: str

    amount: float
    currency: str

    checkout_url: str

    status: PaymentPassStatus = PaymentPassStatus.READY
    execution_mode: PaymentExecutionMode = (
        PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """Validate the payment handoff contract."""

        required_text_fields = {
            "payment_pass_id": self.payment_pass_id,
            "deal_id": self.deal_id,
            "user_id": self.user_id,
            "product_name": self.product_name,
            "vendor_name": self.vendor_name,
            "currency": self.currency,
            "checkout_url": self.checkout_url,
        }

        for field_name, value in required_text_fields.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"PaymentPass {field_name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if self.amount < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "PaymentPass amount cannot be negative."
            )

        if not (
            self.checkout_url.startswith("https://")
            or self.checkout_url.startswith("http://")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "PaymentPass checkout_url must use "
                "HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

        if self.status is not PaymentPassStatus.READY:
            raise ValueError(
                "Hackathon PaymentPass status must be READY."
            )

        if self.execution_mode is not PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO:
            raise ValueError(
                "Hackathon PaymentPass execution_mode must be DEMO."
            )

    @property
    def is_ready(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether the checkout handoff is ready."""

        return self.status is PaymentPassStatus.READY

    @property
    def is_demo_only(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether the pass is restricted to demo execution."""

        return (
            self.execution_mode
            is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
        )

    @property
    def payment_executed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Explicitly indicate whether payment was executed.

        The hackathon PaymentPass can never represent an executed
        payment. It is a checkout handoff only.
        """

        return False

    def display_amount(self) -> str:
        """Return a UI-friendly amount."""

        return (
            f"{self.currency} "
            f"{self.amount:.2f}"
        )

    def render_demo(self) -> str:
        """
        Render the payment handoff for the hackathon demonstration.

        The output deliberately makes the non-execution boundary
        explicit to judges and users.
        """

        return "\n".join(
            [
                "PAYMENT PASS",
                "-" * 72,
                f"Product: {self.product_name}",
                f"Vendor: {self.vendor_name}",
                f"Amount: {self.display_amount()}",
                "",
                f"Payment status: {self.status.value}",
                (
                    "Execution mode: "
                    f"{self.execution_mode.value}"
                ),
                "",
                "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]",
                "",
                "No payment has been executed.",
                (
                    "Vendor checkout remains outside "
                    "the hackathon demo."
                ),
            ]
        )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\commerce\service.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Commerce Service.

Provides the controlled application boundary for commerce
actions and demo-only payment handoffs.

The service does not process money itself.

It prepares commerce actions and payment handoffs for an
approved integration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionResult,
    CommerceActionStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentPass,
)


class CommerceService:
    """
    Controlled commerce application service.

    For the hackathon, the service supports:
    - safe external-vendor handoff
    - demo-only payment preparation

    A future payment/partner adapter can be injected without
    changing DealCard or FlashDeal.
    """

    def initiate(
        self,
        action: CommerceAction,
    ) -> CommerceActionResult:
        """
        Prepare a commerce action.

        This establishes the external-vendor boundary rather
        than executing a payment.
        """

        return CommerceActionResult(
            action_id=action.action_id,
            deal_id=action.deal_id,
            status=CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT,
            next_url=action.destination_url,
            message=(
                "Continue to the approved vendor "
                "to complete the purchase."
            ),
        )

    def prepare_payment(
        self,
        payment_pass: PaymentPass,
    ) -> PaymentPass:
        """
        Prepare a demo-only payment handoff.

        No payment provider is called and no transaction is
        executed. The PaymentPass contract itself enforces
        READY + DEMO semantics.
        """

        if not payment_pass.is_ready:
            raise ValueError(
                "PaymentPass must be READY."
            )

        if not payment_pass.is_demo_only:
            raise ValueError(
                "Hackathon payment must use DEMO execution mode."
            )

        return payment_pass

FILE: src\ai_runtime\discovery\flash_deal_card.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Flash Deal Card presentation contract.

This module defines the user-facing data contract for the
EasyChatBot Flash Deal Card shown by a character.

Architecture:

Parallel / Discovery
        |
        v
     DealCard
        |
        +---- Memory Context
        |
        +---- Character
        |
        v
  FlashDealCard
        |
        +---- GET DEAL
        |
        +---- MORE INFORMATION
        |
        v
 External Vendor / Future Commerce Flow

The model is presentation-oriented.

It does not:
- call providers
- access SQLite
- perform payments
- execute agents
- scrape vendor pages
- invent product information
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import DealCard


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FlashDealCard:
    """
    User-facing Flash Deal Card.

    This contract maps directly to the hackathon UX:

    - character discovery
    - product information
    - pricing
    - discount
    - personal relevance
    - vendor
    - GET DEAL action
    - MORE INFORMATION action

    The card deliberately contains no payment implementation.
    """

    deal_id: str
    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    character_message: str

    product_name: str
    product_image_url: str
    tagline: str
    highlights: tuple[str, ...]

    current_price: float
    currency: str
    previous_price: float | None
    discount_percentage: float | None
    savings: float | None

    relevance_reason: str

    vendor_name: str
    more_information_url: str
    get_deal_url: str

    badge: str = "NEW FIND!"
    card_id: str | None = None
    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the presentation contract.
        """

        required_text_fields = {
            "deal_id": self.deal_id,
            "character_id": self.character_id,
            "character_name": self.character_name,
            "character_message": self.character_message,
            "product_name": self.product_name,
            "product_image_url": self.product_image_url,
            "tagline": self.tagline,
            "currency": self.currency,
            "relevance_reason": self.relevance_reason,
            "vendor_name": self.vendor_name,
            "more_information_url": self.more_information_url,
            "get_deal_url": self.get_deal_url,
        }

        for field_name, value in required_text_fields.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"FlashDealCard {field_name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if not self.highlights:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard must contain at least one highlight."
            )

        if any(
            not highlight.strip()
            for highlight in self.highlights
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard highlights cannot contain empty values."
            )

        if self.current_price < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard current_price cannot be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.previous_price is not None
            and self.previous_price < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard previous_price cannot be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and not 0 <= self.discount_percentage <= 100
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard discount_percentage must be "
                "between 0 and 100."
            )

        if self.savings is not None and self.savings < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard savings cannot be negative."
            )

        self._validate_url(
            self.product_image_url,
            "product_image_url",
        )
        self._validate_url(
            self.more_information_url,
            "more_information_url",
        )
        self._validate_url(
            self.get_deal_url,
            "get_deal_url",
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_url(
        url: str,
        field_name: str,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate a user-facing URL.
        """

        if not (
            url.startswith("https://")
            or url.startswith("http://")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"FlashDealCard {field_name} must use "
                "HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

    @classmethod
    def from_deal(
        cls,
        *,
        deal: DealCard,
        character_id: str,
        character_name: str,
        character_message: str,
        relevance_reason: str,
        product_image_url: str,
        tagline: str,
        highlights: list[str] | tuple[str, ...],
        badge: str = "NEW FIND!",
        metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> "FlashDealCard":
        """
        Build a presentation card from a domain DealCard.

        Product, pricing and vendor information comes from the
        DealCard. Character and personalisation information is
        supplied explicitly by the application layer.

        No product or pricing information is invented here.
        """

        savings: float | None = None

        if (
            deal.previous_price is not None
            and deal.previous_price > deal.price
        ):
            savings = round(
                deal.previous_price - deal.price,
                2,
            )

        return cls(
            deal_id=deal.deal_id,
            character_id=character_id,
            character_name=character_name,
            character_message=character_message,
            product_name=deal.product_name,
            product_image_url=product_image_url,
            tagline=tagline,
            highlights=tuple(highlights),
            current_price=deal.price,
            currency=deal.currency,
            previous_price=deal.previous_price,
            discount_percentage=deal.discount_percentage,
            savings=savings,
            relevance_reason=relevance_reason,
            vendor_name=deal.vendor,
            more_information_url=(
                deal.commerce_link.destination_url
            ),
            get_deal_url=(
                deal.commerce_link.click_url
            ),
            badge=badge,
            metadata=(
                dict(metadata)
                if metadata is not None
                else {}
            ),
        )

    @property
    def has_discount(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether a verified discount exists."""

        return (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and self.discount_percentage > 0
        )

    @property
    def has_previous_price(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether a previous price is available."""

        return self.previous_price is not None

    @property
    def display_discount(self) -> str | None:
        """Return a UI-friendly discount label."""

        if self.discount_percentage is None:
            return None

        return f"{self.discount_percentage:.0f}% OFF"

    @property
    def display_savings(self) -> str | None:
        """Return a UI-friendly savings label."""

        if self.savings is None:
            return None

        return f"Save {self.currency} {self.savings:.2f}"

FILE: src\ai_runtime\discovery\flash_deal_demo.py

"""
Hackathon Flash Deal Card demo builder.

This module provides a deterministic demonstration flow for the
user-facing Flash Deal Card.

Flow:

Memory
    |
    v
DealCard
    |
    v
Personal relevance
    |
    v
Character
    |
    v
FlashDealCard

The module is intentionally deterministic for the hackathon.

It does not:
- call external APIs
- call Parallel
- process payments
- access SQLite directly
- execute agents
- create real orders
- pretend that demo pricing is live vendor data

Production discovery should provide the real DealCard and
verified source information.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


DEMO_PRODUCT_IMAGE_URL = (
    "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
)

DEMO_VENDOR_PRODUCT_URL = (
    "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
)


def build_demo_deal() -> DealCard:
    """
    Create deterministic demo discovery data.

    This is presentation/demo data only. It must not be
    interpreted as a live product offer.
    """

    commerce_link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Demo Vendor",
        destination_url=DEMO_VENDOR_PRODUCT_URL,
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor="Demo Vendor",
        price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        commerce_link=commerce_link,
        source="hackathon_demo",
        description=(
            "Demo product used to demonstrate the "
            "EasyChatBot discovery experience."
        ),
    )


def build_demo_memory() -> MemoryEntry:
    """
    Create deterministic demo user memory.

    The memory represents an explicit user preference that
    can be used to explain why the discovery is relevant.
    """

    return MemoryEntry(
        user_id="demo-user",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content=(
            "prefers powerful mobile photography "
            "and high-performance devices"
        ),
    )


def build_demo_flash_deal_card(
    deal: DealCard | None = None,
) -> FlashDealCard:
    """
    Build the complete deterministic hackathon Flash Deal Card.

    This function demonstrates the intended vertical slice:

        Memory
          +
        DealCard
          +
        Character
          =
        Personalised FlashDealCard
    """

    if deal is None:
        deal = build_demo_deal()

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    relevance_reason = (
        "You often look for powerful mobile photography "
        "and high-performance devices."
    )

    return FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "Look what I found! "
            "This could be perfect for you."
        ),
        relevance_reason=relevance_reason,
        product_image_url=DEMO_PRODUCT_IMAGE_URL,
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ],
        badge="NEW FIND!",
        metadata={
            "demo": "true",
            "memory_type": MemoryType.PREFERENCE.value,
            "memory_id": memory.memory_id,
            "source": "hackathon_demo",
        },
    )


def render_demo_flash_deal_card(
    card: FlashDealCard | None = None,
) -> str:
    """
    Render a terminal-friendly representation of the Flash
    Deal Card.

    This is useful for the hackathon demonstration before a
    graphical frontend is connected.
    """

    if card is None:
        card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    lines = [
        "",
        "=" * 72,
        "EASYCHATBOT — FLASH DEAL",
        "=" * 72,
        "",
        f"[ {card.badge} ]",
        "",
        f"{card.character_name}",
        f'"{card.character_message}"',
        "",
        f"{card.product_name}",
        card.tagline,
        "",
        "HIGHLIGHTS",
        "-" * 72,
    ]

    for highlight in card.highlights:
        lines.append(f"  • {highlight}")

    lines.extend(
        [
            "",
            "PRICE",
            "-" * 72,
            f"  Previous price: "
            f"{_format_price(card.previous_price, card.currency)}",
            f"  Discount: "
            f"{card.display_discount or 'No verified discount'}",
            f"  Current price: "
            f"{_format_price(card.current_price, card.currency)}",
            f"  Savings: "
            f"{card.display_savings or 'Not available'}",
            "",
            "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU",
            "-" * 72,
            f"  {card.relevance_reason}",
            "",
            f"AVAILABLE FROM: {card.vendor_name}",
            "",
            "[ GET DEAL ]",
            f"  {card.get_deal_url}",
            "",
            "[ MORE INFORMATION ]",
            f"  {card.more_information_url}",
            "",
            "NOTE: This is deterministic hackathon demo data.",
            "=" * 72,
        ]
    )

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _format_price(
    value: float | None,
    currency: str,
) -> str:
    """
    Format an optional price for terminal output.
    """

    if value is None:
        return "N/A"

    return f"{currency} {value:,.2f}"


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card()
    )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\discovery\flash_deal_presenter.py
============================================================
"""
Flash Deal Card presenter.

Pure presentation layer for the EasyChatBot hackathon.

Architecture:

FlashDealCard
      |
      v
FlashDealPresenter
      |
      v
Markdown / terminal output

Responsibilities:
- render an existing FlashDealCard
- present verified product and pricing information
- present character context
- present personal relevance
- expose the existing vendor actions

Non-responsibilities:
- discovery
- memory access
- provider calls
- database access
- commerce execution
- payment processing
- URL generation
- product data generation

The presenter must never mutate the FlashDealCard.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


class FlashDealPresenter:
    """
    Pure renderer for the user-facing Flash Deal Card.

    The presenter consumes the existing FlashDealCard contract
    and produces deterministic Markdown suitable for the
    hackathon terminal demonstration or a future chat UI.
    """

    def render(
        self,
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Render a FlashDealCard as user-facing Markdown.

        No external calls or business decisions are performed.
        """

        sections: list[str] = []

        sections.append(
            f"## {card.character_name}"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"> {card.character_message}"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"### {card.badge}"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"**{card.product_name}**"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"*{card.tagline}*"
        )

        sections.append(
            self._render_highlights(card)
        )

        sections.append(
            self._render_price(card)
        )

        sections.append(
            "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        )

        sections.append(
            card.relevance_reason
        )

        sections.append(
            "### AVAILABLE FROM"
        )

        sections.append(
            card.vendor_name
        )

        sections.append(
            self._render_actions(card)
        )

        return "\n\n".join(sections)

    @staticmethod
    def _render_highlights(
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """Render the product highlights."""

        lines = ["### HIGHLIGHTS"]

        lines.extend(
            f"- {highlight}"
            for highlight in card.highlights
        )

        return "\n".join(lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _render_price(
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """Render verified pricing information."""

        lines = ["### PRICE"]

        if card.previous_price is not None:
            lines.append(
                "Previous price: "
                f"{card.currency} "
                f"{card.previous_price:.2f}"
            )

        lines.append(
            "Current price: "
            f"{card.currency} "
            f"{card.current_price:.2f}"
        )

        if card.display_discount is not None:
            lines.append(
                f"Discount: {card.display_discount}"
            )

        if card.display_savings is not None:
            lines.append(
                f"Savings: {card.display_savings}"
            )

        return "\n".join(lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _render_actions(
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Render vendor action destinations.

        The presenter only exposes URLs already present on the
        FlashDealCard. It does not create or transform them.
        """

        return "\n".join(
            [
                "### ACTIONS",
                "",
                f"[GET DEAL]({card.get_deal_url})",
                "",
                (
                    "[MORE INFORMATION]"
                    f"({card.more_information_url})"
                ),
            ]
        )
