FILE: src\ai_runtime\discovery\flash_idea.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon personalised Flash Idea builder.

Combines:
- a discovered DealCard
- user memory
- character identity

into a character-led discovery message.

This module does not:
- process payments
- call vendors
- call external APIs
- write memory
- execute agents
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    DealCard,
    FlashIdea,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


class FlashIdeaBuilder:
    """
    Builds a personalised FlashIdea from a deal
    and explicit user memories.
    """

    def build(
        self,
        *,
        character_id: str,
        deal: DealCard,
        memories: list[MemoryEntry],
    ) -> FlashIdea:
        """
        Build a personalised flash idea.

        Preference memories are used to explain why
        the discovery may be relevant to the user.
        """

        preferences = [
            memory
            for memory in memories
            if memory.memory_type
            == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
        ]

        if preferences:
            preference = preferences[0].content

            message = (
                f"I remembered that {preference.lower()} "
                f"I found this {deal.product_name} "
                f"at {deal.price:.2f} {deal.currency} "
                f"from {deal.vendor}."
            )
        else:
            message = (
                f"I found something you might like: "
                f"{deal.product_name} "
                f"at {deal.price:.2f} {deal.currency} "
                f"from {deal.vendor}."
            )

        if deal.discount_percentage is not None:
            message += (
                f" It's currently "
                f"{deal.discount_percentage:.0f}% off."
            )

        return FlashIdea(
            character_id=character_id,
            deal_id=deal.deal_id,
            title="Flash idea",
            message=message,
            metadata={
                "vendor": deal.vendor,
                "commerce_url": (
                    deal.commerce_link.click_url
                ),
            },
        )

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\discovery\models.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Discovery domain models.

These models represent the small discovery layer required
for the hackathon experience.

The hackathon is NOT a marketplace.

EasyChatBot:
    discovers products
    evaluates opportunities
    presents character-led flash ideas
    provides an outbound vendor link

The external vendor:
    owns the product page
    owns checkout
    owns payment
    fulfils the purchase

Architecture:

Discovery Intelligence
        |
        v
     DealCard
        |
        +--> CommerceLink
        |
        v
    FlashIdea
        |
        v
     Character
        |
        v
       User
        |
        v
 External Vendor
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from uuid import uuid4


class CommerceLinkType(str, Enum):
    """
    Type of outbound commerce link.
    """

    DIRECT = "direct"

    AFFILIATE = "affiliate"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommerceLink:
    """
    Outbound link to the external vendor.

    EasyChatBot does not process the transaction.

    The destination remains the vendor's website.
    """

    vendor: str

    destination_url: str

    link_type: CommerceLinkType = (
        CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
    )

    tracking_url: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the commerce link.
        """

        if not self.vendor.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce vendor cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.destination_url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce destination URL "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not (
            self.destination_url.startswith(
                "https://"
            )
            or self.destination_url.startswith(
                "http://"
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce destination URL must "
                "use HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

        if self.tracking_url is not None:
            if not self.tracking_url.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    "Commerce tracking URL cannot "
                    "be empty when provided."
                )

            if not (
                self.tracking_url.startswith(
                    "https://"
                )
                or self.tracking_url.startswith(
                    "http://"
                )
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Commerce tracking URL must "
                    "use HTTP or HTTPS."
                )

    @property
    def click_url(self) -> str:
        """
        Return the URL that should be opened by the user.

        A tracking URL takes precedence when one is
        available.
        """

        return (
            self.tracking_url
            if self.tracking_url is not None
            else self.destination_url
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DealCard:
    """
    Compact representation of a discovered product opportunity.

    This is intentionally not a product catalogue entity.

    It represents a single discovery opportunity surfaced
    to a user.
    """

    product_name: str

    vendor: str

    price: float

    currency: str

    commerce_link: CommerceLink

    deal_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    previous_price: float | None = None

    discount_percentage: float | None = None

    source: str | None = None

    description: str | None = None

    discovered_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the DealCard.
        """

        if not self.product_name.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal product name cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.vendor.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal vendor cannot be empty."
            )

        if self.price < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal price cannot be negative."
            )

        if not self.currency.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal currency cannot be empty."
            )

        if (
            self.previous_price is not None
            and self.previous_price < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal previous price cannot "
                "be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and not 0 <= self.discount_percentage <= 100
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal discount percentage must "
                "be between 0 and 100."
            )

        if (
            self.commerce_link.vendor
            != self.vendor
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal vendor must match the "
                "commerce link vendor."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FlashIdea:
    """
    Character-led presentation of a discovered deal.

    A FlashIdea is the user-facing discovery message.

    It is not a transaction.
    """

    character_id: str

    deal_id: str

    message: str

    title: str = "Flash idea"

    idea_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    created_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the FlashIdea.
        """

        if not self.character_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashIdea character_id "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.deal_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashIdea deal_id "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.message.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashIdea message "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\discovery\notification.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Discovery Notification.

Defines the lightweight user notification produced from a
personalised FlashDealCard.

Architecture:

DiscoveryService
      |
      v
FlashDealCard
      |
      v
DiscoveryNotification
      |
      +--> Telegram
      +--> Web UI
      +--> Future mobile UI

The notification layer is presentation/delivery oriented.

It does not:
- search for products
- access providers
- decide recommendations
- access memory directly
- process payments
- execute purchases
- build a shopping dashboard

The FlashDealCard remains the source of truth for the
discovered offer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveryNotification:
    """
    Lightweight character-driven notification.

    Designed for a single discovery event rather than a
    persistent shopping dashboard.
    """

    notification_id: str
    user_id: str
    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    title: str
    message: str
    deal: FlashDealCard

    @classmethod
    def from_flash_deal(
        cls,
        *,
        notification_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        deal: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> "DiscoveryNotification":
        """
        Create a notification from an existing FlashDealCard.

        No discovery or commerce logic occurs here.
        """

        if not notification_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Notification notification_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Notification user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        return cls(
            notification_id=notification_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            character_id=deal.character_id,
            character_name=deal.character_name,
            title=(
                f"{deal.character_name} found something for you"
            ),
            message=deal.character_message,
            deal=deal,
        )

    @property
    def product_name(self) -> str:
        """Return the discovered product name."""

        return self.deal.product_name

    @property
    def relevance_reason(self) -> str:
        """Return the personalisation explanation."""

        return self.deal.relevance_reason

    @property
    def get_deal_url(self) -> str:
        """Return the controlled commerce destination."""

        return self.deal.get_deal_url

    @property
    def more_information_url(self) -> str:
        """Return the vendor information destination."""

        return self.deal.more_information_url

    def render(self) -> str:
        """
        Render a compact notification suitable for a chat UI.

        The full FlashDealCard remains available for the richer
        product presentation.
        """

        discount = (
            self.deal.display_discount
            or "NEW FIND"
        )

        return "\n".join(
            [
                f"{self.character_name}:",
                self.message,
                "",
                f"{discount} — {self.product_name}",
                f"{self.deal.currency} "
                f"{self.deal.current_price:.2f}",
                "",
                "Why this may interest you:",
                self.relevance_reason,
            ]
        )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\discovery\service.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Discovery Service.

This module provides the application-level orchestration boundary
for personalised product discovery.

Architecture:

Discovery inputs
    |
    +--> DealCard
    |
    +--> Memory context
    |
    +--> Character context
    |
    v
DiscoveryService
    |
    v
FlashDealCard

Responsibilities:
- orchestrate existing discovery components
- apply explicit memory context
- apply explicit character context
- create the user-facing FlashDealCard
- preserve domain data and identifiers

Non-responsibilities:
- provider SDK calls
- web searching
- memory persistence
- payment processing
- vendor APIs
- autonomous purchasing
- character business logic

The service deliberately receives already-discovered DealCard data.
Provider integration remains in the provider/agent layers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CharacterContext:
    """
    Explicit character presentation context.

    The character is a presentation/personality concern.
    It does not perform discovery or commerce operations.
    """

    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    message: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.character_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Character context character_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.character_name.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Character context character_name cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.message.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Character context message cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveryRequest:
    """
    Application-level request for personalised discovery.

    The request contains already-discovered product information
    and explicit user context.

    No external search is performed by DiscoveryService.
    """

    user_id: str
    deal: DealCard
    character: CharacterContext
    product_image_url: str
    tagline: str
    highlights: tuple[str, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.product_image_url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request product_image_url "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.tagline.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request tagline cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.highlights:
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request must contain at least "
                "one highlight."
            )


class DiscoveryService:
    """
    Orchestrates personalised discovery.

    This service intentionally contains no provider logic and no
    persistence logic. It composes the existing DealCard,
    MemoryEntry and FlashDealCard contracts.
    """

    def create_flash_deal(
        self,
        request: DiscoveryRequest,
        memories: list[MemoryEntry] | None = None,
    ) -> FlashDealCard:
        """
        Create a personalised FlashDealCard.

        Memory is read-only input to this operation.

        Preference memory is used to generate the personal
        relevance explanation. Other memory types are not
        automatically treated as preferences.
        """

        memory_context = memories or []

        relevance_reason = self._build_relevance_reason(
            memory_context
        )

        return FlashDealCard.from_deal(
            deal=request.deal,
            character_id=request.character.character_id,
            character_name=request.character.character_name,
            character_message=request.character.message,
            relevance_reason=relevance_reason,
            product_image_url=request.product_image_url,
            tagline=request.tagline,
            highlights=request.highlights,
            metadata={
                "user_id": request.user_id,
                "discovery_service": "hackathon",
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_relevance_reason(
        memories: list[MemoryEntry],
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build a deterministic relevance explanation.

        Only explicit preference memories are used.

        This is deliberately deterministic for the hackathon;
        an LLM-based personalisation layer can be introduced later
        behind an explicit interface.
        """

        preferences = [
            memory
            for memory in memories
            if memory.memory_type
            == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
        ]

        if not preferences:
            return (
                "This discovery may be relevant to your "
                "interests."
            )

        preference = preferences[0].content.strip()

        return (
            "This may be relevant because you "
            f"{preference.rstrip('.') }."
        )

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\memory\models.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Memory contracts.

This module defines the minimal persistent memory model
required by the hackathon discovery flow.

This is intentionally lightweight.

It is NOT the production EasyChatBot memory architecture.

Hackathon responsibilities:

- represent user memories
- distinguish memory categories
- provide stable identifiers
- preserve timestamps
- support future persistence

Out of scope:

- vector embeddings
- semantic retrieval
- LLM-generated memory extraction
- autonomous memory writes
- purchase transactions
- production learner-memory architecture
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from uuid import uuid4


class MemoryType(str, Enum):
    """
    Supported hackathon memory categories.
    """

    PREFERENCE = "preference"

    DISCOVERY_CONTEXT = "discovery_context"

    SAVED_ITEM = "saved_item"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MemoryEntry:
    """
    Persistent user memory entry.

    Memory is explicit application state.

    Agents must not directly mutate this object or
    the underlying persistence layer.
    """

    user_id: str

    memory_type: MemoryType

    content: str

    memory_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    created_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    updated_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the memory contract.
        """

        if not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.content.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory content cannot be empty."
            )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """
        Convert the memory entry into a persistence-safe
        representation.
        """

        return {
            "memory_id": self.memory_id,
            "user_id": self.user_id,
            "memory_type": self.memory_type.value,
            "content": self.content,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "updated_at": self.updated_at.isoformat(),
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
        }

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\memory\service.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Memory Service.

Application-facing service for the lightweight hackathon
memory capability.

Architecture:

Character / Discovery
        |
        v
MemoryService
        |
        v
MemoryStore
        |
        v
SQLite

The service owns memory application behaviour.

It does not:
- access SQLite directly
- call providers
- call agents
- generate memories autonomously
- implement shopping logic
- implement recommendation logic
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.store import (
    MemoryStore,
)


class MemoryService:
    """
    Application service for hackathon memory.

    Memory writes are explicit.

    This is intentional: an agent should not silently
    create persistent user state merely because it generated
    a response.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        store: MemoryStore,
    ) -> None:
        self._store = store

    def remember(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        memory_type: MemoryType,
        content: str,
        metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Create and persist a new memory entry.
        """

        memory = MemoryEntry(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=memory_type,
            content=content,
            metadata=(
                dict(metadata)
                if metadata is not None
                else {}
            ),
        )

        return self._store.save(
            memory
        )

    def get(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> MemoryEntry | None:
        """
        Retrieve a memory by identifier.
        """

        return self._store.get(
            memory_id
        )

    def list_for_user(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        memory_type: MemoryType | None = None,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve memories for a user.

        Optionally restrict results to one memory type.
        """

        return self._store.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=memory_type,
        )

    def preferences(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve user preference memories.
        """

        return self.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        )

    def discovery_context(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve current discovery context.
        """

        return self.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        )

    def saved_items(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve saved discovery items.
        """

        return self.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        )

    def delete(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Delete a memory.
        """

        return self._store.delete(
            memory_id
        )

============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\memory\store.py
============================================================
"""
Hackathon Memory persistence.

This module provides the minimal persistent Memory Store
required by the hackathon.

Architecture:

MemoryService
      |
      v
MemoryStore
      |
      v
SQLite

The store is deliberately small.

It is responsible only for persistence and retrieval of
MemoryEntry objects.

It does not:
- decide what should be remembered
- call an LLM
- perform semantic retrieval
- execute agents
- perform governance decisions
- manage purchases
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


class MemoryStore:
    """
    SQLite-backed persistence for hackathon memories.

    The database schema is created automatically when the
    store is initialised.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        database_path: str | Path,
    ) -> None:
        self._database_path = str(database_path)

        self._initialise_database()

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        """
        Create a SQLite connection.
        """

        connection = sqlite3.connect(
            self._database_path
        )

        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row

        return connection

    def _initialise_database(self) -> None:
        """
        Create the Memory table if it does not exist.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
                    memory_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    memory_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    content TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL
                )
                """
            )

            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
                idx_memories_user_id
                ON memories(user_id)
                """
            )

            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
                idx_memories_user_type
                ON memories(user_id, memory_type)
                """
            )

    def save(
        self,
        memory: MemoryEntry,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Persist a MemoryEntry.

        Existing entries with the same memory_id are
        replaced.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                """
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO memories (
                    memory_id,
                    user_id,
                    memory_type,
                    content,
                    created_at,
                    updated_at,
                    metadata_json
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    memory.memory_id,
                    memory.user_id,
                    memory.memory_type.value,
                    memory.content,
                    memory.created_at.isoformat(),
                    memory.updated_at.isoformat(),
                    json.dumps(
                        memory.metadata,
                        ensure_ascii=False,
                    ),
                ),
            )

        return memory

    def get(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> MemoryEntry | None:
        """
        Retrieve a single memory by identifier.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            row = connection.execute(
                """
                SELECT
                    memory_id,
                    user_id,
                    memory_type,
                    content,
                    created_at,
                    updated_at,
                    metadata_json
                FROM memories
                WHERE memory_id = ?
                """,
                (memory_id,),
            ).fetchone()

        if row is None:
            return None

        return self._row_to_memory(row)

    def list_for_user(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        memory_type: MemoryType | None = None,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve memories belonging to a user.

        Results are returned newest first.
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        with self._connect() as connection:

            if memory_type is None:
                rows = connection.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT
                        memory_id,
                        user_id,
                        memory_type,
                        content,
                        created_at,
                        updated_at,
                        metadata_json
                    FROM memories
                    WHERE user_id = ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                    """,
                    (user_id,),
                ).fetchall()

            else:
                rows = connection.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT
                        memory_id,
                        user_id,
                        memory_type,
                        content,
                        created_at,
                        updated_at,
                        metadata_json
                    FROM memories
                    WHERE user_id = ?
                      AND memory_type = ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                    """,
                    (
                        user_id,
                        memory_type.value,
                    ),
                ).fetchall()

        return [
            self._row_to_memory(row)
            for row in rows
        ]

    def delete(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Delete a memory.

        Returns True when a memory was deleted.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            cursor = connection.execute(
                """
                DELETE FROM memories
                WHERE memory_id = ?
                """,
                (memory_id,),
            )

        return cursor.rowcount > 0

    @staticmethod
    def _row_to_memory(
        row: sqlite3.Row,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Convert a SQLite row into a MemoryEntry.
        """

        return MemoryEntry(
            memory_id=row["memory_id"],
            user_id=row["user_id"],
            memory_type=MemoryType(
                row["memory_type"]
            ),
            content=row["content"],
            created_at=(
                __import__("datetime")
                .datetime.fromisoformat(
                    row["created_at"]
                )
            ),
            updated_at=(
                __import__("datetime")
                .datetime.fromisoformat(
                    row["updated_at"]
                )
            ),
            metadata=json.loads(
                row["metadata_json"]
            ),
        )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\agent.py

"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — Podcast Discovery Agent.

The Agent converts explicit learner context and preference memory into
provider-neutral search intent.

It does not call Apple/Spotify, write memory, select a provider, or invent
podcast metadata.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import MemoryEntry, MemoryType
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
)


class PodcastDiscoveryAgent:
    """Build provider-neutral podcast discovery intent."""

    _STOP_WORDS = {
        "a", "an", "and", "are", "be", "for", "from", "i", "in", "is",
        "like", "of", "on", "podcasts", "prefers", "prefer", "the", "to",
        "with", "you", "your",
    }

    def build_search_intent(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        learning_context: LearningContext,
        memories: list[MemoryEntry],
        max_results: int = 10,
    ) -> PodcastSearchIntent:
        """
        Convert explicit preferences + learning-world context into intent.

        Only PREFERENCE memories are used, matching the existing
        EasyChatBot discovery contract.
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("Podcast Agent user_id cannot be empty.")

        preferences = [
            memory
            for memory in memories
            if memory.user_id == user_id
            and memory.memory_type == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
        ]

        topics: list[str] = []
        keywords: list[str] = []

        for memory in preferences:
            topics.extend(
                self._metadata_terms(
                    memory.metadata,
                    "topics",
                    "interests",
                    "preferred_topics",
                )
            )
            keywords.extend(
                self._metadata_terms(
                    memory.metadata,
                    "keywords",
                )
            )

            if not topics and not keywords:
                keywords.extend(
                    self._content_keywords(memory.content)
                )

        # The learning world is always part of the search context.
        topics.append(learning_context.world_id)

        # Target language is deliberately a hard eligibility constraint,
        # not just another search keyword.
        return PodcastSearchIntent(
            user_id=user_id,
            learning_context=learning_context,
            topics=tuple(self._dedupe(topics)),
            keywords=tuple(self._dedupe(keywords)),
            language_required=True,
            max_results=max_results,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _metadata_terms(
        metadata: dict[str, str],
        *keys: str,
    ) -> list[str]:
        terms: list[str] = []
        for key in keys:
            raw = metadata.get(key, "")
            if raw:
                terms.extend(
                    item.strip()
                    for item in re.split(r"[,;|]", raw)
                    if item.strip()
                )
        return terms

    @classmethod
    def _content_keywords(cls, content: str) -> list[str]:
        words = re.findall(
            r"[A-Za-zÀ-ÿ0-9][A-Za-zÀ-ÿ0-9'-]{2,}",
            content.lower(),
        )
        return [
            word for word in words
            if word not in cls._STOP_WORDS
        ][:8]

    @staticmethod
    def _dedupe(values: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        seen: set[str] = set()
        result: list[str] = []
        for value in values:
            key = value.strip().lower()
            if key and key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                result.append(value.strip())
        return result

FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\apple.py

"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — Apple Podcasts catalogue provider.

Uses Apple's iTunes Search API for read-only podcast catalogue discovery.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
    normalise_language,
)


class ApplePodcastProvider:
    """Read-only Apple podcast catalogue provider."""

    BASE_URL = "https://itunes.apple.com/search"

    def __init__(self, *, timeout_seconds: float = 10.0) -> None:
        self._timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds

    def search(
        self,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        query_terms = list(intent.topics) + list(intent.keywords)
        params = {
            "term": " ".join(query_terms[:6]),
            "country": intent.learning_context.country_code.upper(),
            "media": "podcast",
            "entity": "podcast",
            "limit": str(intent.max_results),
            "explicit": "No",
        }

        request = Request(
            f"{self.BASE_URL}?{urlencode(params)}",
            headers={
                "Accept": "application/json",
                "User-Agent": "EasyChatBot-Hackathon/1.0",
            },
            method="GET",
        )

        with urlopen(
            request,
            timeout=self._timeout_seconds,
        ) as response:
            payload = json.loads(
                response.read().decode("utf-8")
            )

        return [
            self._normalise(item)
            for item in payload.get("results", [])
            if self._is_podcast_result(item)
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _is_podcast_result(item: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
        return (
            str(item.get("kind", "")).lower() == "podcast"
            or str(item.get("wrapperType", "")).lower() == "track"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise(
        item: dict[str, object],
    ) -> PodcastSearchResult:
        language = normalise_language(
            str(item.get("language") or "unknown")
        )

        return PodcastSearchResult(
            provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
            external_id=str(
                item.get("collectionId")
                or item.get("trackId")
                or ""
            ),
            title=str(
                item.get("collectionName")
                or item.get("trackName")
                or ""
            ),
            description=str(
                item.get("description")
                or item.get("longDescription")
                or ""
            ),
            publisher=str(
                item.get("artistName")
                or "Unknown publisher"
            ),
            languages=(language,),
            url=str(
                item.get("collectionViewUrl")
                or item.get("trackViewUrl")
                or ""
            ),
            image_url=str(
                item.get("artworkUrl600")
                or item.get("artworkUrl100")
                or ""
            ) or None,
            published_at=str(
                item.get("releaseDate") or ""
            ) or None,
            explicit=(
                str(
                    item.get("trackExplicitness", "")
                ).lower() == "explicit"
            ),
            metadata={"provider": "apple_podcasts"},
        )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\challenge.py
============================================================
"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — Podcast Learning Challenge contracts.

Presentation/learning contract only. This module does not download audio,
transcribe podcasts, call providers, or execute external actions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VocabularyItem:
    """A vocabulary item presented to the learner."""

    term: str
    meaning: str
    example: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name, value in {
            "term": self.term,
            "meaning": self.meaning,
            "example": self.example,
        }.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"VocabularyItem {name} cannot be empty."
                )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ComprehensionQuestion:
    """A multiple-choice comprehension question."""

    question: str
    options: tuple[str, ...]
    correct_option: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.question.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "ComprehensionQuestion question cannot be empty."
            )

        if len(self.options) < 2:
            raise ValueError(
                "ComprehensionQuestion requires at least two options."
            )

        if any(not option.strip() for option in self.options):
            raise ValueError(
                "ComprehensionQuestion options cannot be empty."
            )

        if not 0 <= self.correct_option < len(self.options):
            raise ValueError(
                "ComprehensionQuestion correct_option is out of range."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PodcastLearningChallenge:
    """Learner-facing challenge derived from a verified podcast recommendation."""

    challenge_id: str
    user_id: str
    podcast_id: str
    podcast_title: str
    podcast_url: str
    target_language: str
    learner_level: str
    vocabulary: tuple[VocabularyItem, ...]
    comprehension_questions: tuple[ComprehensionQuestion, ...]
    discussion_prompt: str
    introduction: str
    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        required = {
            "challenge_id": self.challenge_id,
            "user_id": self.user_id,
            "podcast_id": self.podcast_id,
            "podcast_title": self.podcast_title,
            "podcast_url": self.podcast_url,
            "target_language": self.target_language,
            "learner_level": self.learner_level,
            "discussion_prompt": self.discussion_prompt,
            "introduction": self.introduction,
        }

        for name, value in required.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"PodcastLearningChallenge {name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if not (
            self.podcast_url.startswith("https://")
            or self.podcast_url.startswith("http://")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastLearningChallenge podcast_url must use HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

        if not self.vocabulary:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastLearningChallenge requires vocabulary."
            )

        if not self.comprehension_questions:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastLearningChallenge requires comprehension questions."
            )

    @classmethod
    def create(
        cls,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        podcast_id: str,
        podcast_title: str,
        podcast_url: str,
        target_language: str,
        learner_level: str,
        vocabulary: tuple[VocabularyItem, ...],
        comprehension_questions: tuple[ComprehensionQuestion, ...],
        discussion_prompt: str,
        introduction: str,
        metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> "PodcastLearningChallenge":
        return cls(
            challenge_id=str(uuid4()),
            user_id=user_id,
            podcast_id=podcast_id,
            podcast_title=podcast_title,
            podcast_url=podcast_url,
            target_language=target_language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
            vocabulary=vocabulary,
            comprehension_questions=comprehension_questions,
            discussion_prompt=discussion_prompt,
            introduction=introduction,
            metadata=dict(metadata or {}),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChallengeResult:
    """Result of a learner completing the comprehension part."""

    challenge_id: str
    user_id: str
    score: int
    total_questions: int
    passed: bool
    vocabulary_review_required: bool
    message: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.total_questions < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "ChallengeResult total_questions must be at least 1."
            )

        if not 0 <= self.score <= self.total_questions:
            raise ValueError(
                "ChallengeResult score must be between 0 and total_questions."
            )

        if not self.message.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "ChallengeResult message cannot be empty."
            )

"""
Deterministic integration demo:
recommendation -> character -> podcast learning challenge -> result.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import MemoryEntry, MemoryType
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.agent import PodcastDiscoveryAgent
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.challenge_presenter import (
    PodcastLearningChallengePresenter,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.challenge_service import (
    PodcastLearningChallengeService,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.service import PodcastRecommendationService


class DemoProvider:
    def search(
        self,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        return [
            PodcastSearchResult(
                provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
                external_id="apple-fr-001",
                title="L'IA en Europe",
                description=(
                    "Artificial intelligence and European regulation "
                    "in French."
                ),
                publisher="Demo Publisher",
                languages=("fr",),
                url="https://podcasts.apple.com/demo/ai-europe",
                metadata={
                    "learner_level": "B2",
                },
            )
        ]


def main() -> None:
    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="demo-user",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers technology and current affairs.",
        metadata={
            "topics": (
                "artificial intelligence, European regulation"
            ),
        },
    )

    learning_context = LearningContext(
        world_id="French World",
        target_language="fr",
        learner_level="B2",
        learning_goal="Learn French through current events.",
        country_code="LU",
    )

    intent = PodcastDiscoveryAgent().build_search_intent(
        user_id="demo-user",
        learning_context=learning_context,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    recommendation = PodcastRecommendationService(
        [DemoProvider()]
    ).recommend(
        intent=intent,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "I found a podcast I think you'll enjoy. "
            "Listen to it, then let's turn it into a French challenge!"
        ),
    )

    challenge = PodcastLearningChallengeService().create_challenge(
        recommendation=recommendation,
    )

    result = PodcastLearningChallengeService().evaluate(
        challenge=challenge,
        answers=(0, 0),
    )

    presenter = PodcastLearningChallengePresenter()

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EASYCHATBOT — PODCAST → LEARNING CHALLENGE")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()
    print("PERSONALISED RECOMMENDATION")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(f"{recommendation.character_name}:")
    print(f'"{recommendation.character_message}"')
    print()
    print(
        f"[PASS] Podcast selected: "
        f"{recommendation.result.title}"
    )
    print(
        f"[PASS] Language: "
        f"{recommendation.result.languages[0]}"
    )
    print(
        f"[PASS] Preference relevance: "
        f"{recommendation.relevance_reason}"
    )

    print(
        presenter.render(challenge)
    )

    print(
        presenter.render_result(result)
    )

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("PODCAST LEARNING CHALLENGE: SUCCESS")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\challenge_presenter.py
============================================================
"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — Podcast Learning Challenge presentation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.challenge import (
    ChallengeResult,
    PodcastLearningChallenge,
)


class PodcastLearningChallengePresenter:
    """Render the learner-facing challenge for the hackathon demo."""

    def render(
        self,
        challenge: PodcastLearningChallenge,
    ) -> str:
        lines = [
            "",
            "=" * 72,
            "[PODCAST] PODCAST LEARNING CHALLENGE",
            "=" * 72,
            "",
            challenge.introduction,
            "",
            f"Podcast: {challenge.podcast_title}",
            f"Language: {challenge.target_language}",
            f"Level: {challenge.learner_level}",
            "",
            "[ LISTEN ]",
            f"  {challenge.podcast_url}",
            "",
            "VOCABULARY",
            "-" * 72,
        ]

        for index, item in enumerate(
            challenge.vocabulary,
            start=1,
        ):
            lines.extend(
                [
                    f"{index}. {item.term}",
                    f"   {item.meaning}",
                    f"   Example: {item.example}",
                    "",
                ]
            )

        lines.extend(
            [
                "COMPREHENSION",
                "-" * 72,
            ]
        )

        for index, question in enumerate(
            challenge.comprehension_questions,
            start=1,
        ):
            lines.append(f"{index}. {question.question}")
            for option_index, option in enumerate(
                question.options,
                start=1,
            ):
                lines.append(f"   {option_index}. {option}")
            lines.append("")

        lines.extend(
            [
                "DISCUSSION",
                "-" * 72,
                challenge.discussion_prompt,
                "",
            ]
        )

        return "\n".join(lines)

    def render_result(
        self,
        result: ChallengeResult,
    ) -> str:
        return "\n".join(
            [
                "",
                "CHALLENGE RESULT",
                "-" * 72,
                f"Score: {result.score}/{result.total_questions}",
                f"Status: {'PASS' if result.passed else 'REVIEW'}",
                result.message,
                (
                    "Vocabulary review: YES"
                    if result.vocabulary_review_required
                    else "Vocabulary review: NOT REQUIRED"
                ),
            ]
        )


============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\challenge_service.py
============================================================
"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — Podcast Learning Challenge service.

MVP scope:
    verified podcast metadata
        -> level/language-aware challenge

It intentionally does not:
- download audio
- transcribe episodes
- infer facts from unavailable audio
- call Apple/Spotify
- write persistent memory
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.challenge import (
    ChallengeResult,
    ComprehensionQuestion,
    PodcastLearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import PodcastRecommendation


class PodcastLearningChallengeService:
    """Creates and evaluates small deterministic learning challenges."""

    _LEVEL_GUIDANCE = {
        "a1": "Keep the language simple and concrete.",
        "a2": "Use common vocabulary and short explanations.",
        "b1": "Use practical vocabulary and clear contextual questions.",
        "b2": "Use contextual vocabulary and inference-oriented questions.",
        "c1": "Use nuanced vocabulary and analytical questions.",
        "c2": "Use advanced nuance, register, and critical analysis.",
    }

    def create_challenge(
        self,
        *,
        recommendation: PodcastRecommendation,
    ) -> PodcastLearningChallenge:
        """
        Build a challenge from verified recommendation metadata.

        The content is intentionally deterministic for the hackathon demo.
        It does not claim to have analysed the podcast audio.
        """

        language = recommendation.result.languages[0]
        level = recommendation.result.metadata.get(
            "learner_level",
            "B2",
        )

        topic = recommendation.result.title

        vocabulary = (
            VocabularyItem(
                term="réglementation",
                meaning="Ensemble des règles qui encadrent une activité.",
                example=(
                    "La réglementation européenne encadre "
                    "l'intelligence artificielle."
                ),
            ),
            VocabularyItem(
                term="intelligence artificielle",
                meaning=(
                    "Technologies permettant à des machines "
                    "d'effectuer des tâches associées à l'intelligence humaine."
                ),
                example=(
                    "L'intelligence artificielle transforme "
                    "de nombreux secteurs."
                ),
            ),
            VocabularyItem(
                term="enjeu",
                meaning="Question importante qui présente un intérêt majeur.",
                example=(
                    "La protection des données est un enjeu important."
                ),
            ),
        )

        questions = (
            ComprehensionQuestion(
                question=(
                    f"Quel est le thème principal associé à « {topic} » ?"
                ),
                options=(
                    "L'intelligence artificielle et la réglementation",
                    "La cuisine française",
                    "Les transports urbains",
                ),
                correct_option=0,
            ),
            ComprehensionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la réglementation peut-elle être importante "
                    "pour l'intelligence artificielle ?"
                ),
                options=(
                    "Pour encadrer son utilisation et ses risques",
                    "Pour supprimer toutes les technologies",
                    "Pour empêcher les utilisateurs d'apprendre",
                ),
                correct_option=0,
            ),
        )

        introduction = (
            f"Écoutez le podcast en {language}, puis revenez ici pour "
            f"votre défi {level}. Faites attention aux mots clés et aux "
            "idées principales."
        )

        discussion_prompt = (
            "Après votre écoute, expliquez en quelques phrases votre "
            "opinion sur le sujet et utilisez au moins deux nouveaux mots."
        )

        return PodcastLearningChallenge.create(
            user_id=recommendation.user_id,
            podcast_id=recommendation.result.external_id,
            podcast_title=recommendation.result.title,
            podcast_url=recommendation.result.url,
            target_language=language,
            learner_level=level,
            vocabulary=vocabulary,
            comprehension_questions=questions,
            discussion_prompt=discussion_prompt,
            introduction=introduction,
            metadata={
                "source": "podcast_recommendation",
                "provider": recommendation.result.provider.value,
                "challenge_type": "podcast_learning_mvp",
                "content_basis": "verified_podcast_metadata",
            },
        )

    def evaluate(
        self,
        *,
        challenge: PodcastLearningChallenge,
        answers: tuple[int, ...],
    ) -> ChallengeResult:
        """Evaluate comprehension answers without external side effects."""

        total = len(challenge.comprehension_questions)

        if len(answers) != total:
            raise ValueError(
                "Number of answers must equal the number of questions."
            )

        score = sum(
            answer == question.correct_option
            for answer, question in zip(
                answers,
                challenge.comprehension_questions,
            )
        )

        passed = score / total >= 0.5
        vocabulary_review_required = score < total

        if passed:
            message = (
                f"Challenge passed: {score}/{total}. "
                "Review the vocabulary and keep practising."
            )
        else:
            message = (
                f"Keep practising: {score}/{total}. "
                "Review the vocabulary and try again."
            )

        return ChallengeResult(
            challenge_id=challenge.challenge_id,
            user_id=challenge.user_id,
            score=score,
            total_questions=total,
            passed=passed,
            vocabulary_review_required=vocabulary_review_required,
            message=message,
        )

FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\models.py

"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — provider-neutral podcast contracts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import Final
from uuid import uuid4


class PodcastProviderName(str, Enum):
    APPLE = "apple_podcasts"
    SPOTIFY = "spotify"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LearningContext:
    """Explicit learning-world context for podcast discovery."""

    world_id: str
    target_language: str
    learner_level: str
    learning_goal: str = ""
    preferred_languages: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    country_code: str = "LU"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.world_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("LearningContext world_id cannot be empty.")
        if not self.target_language.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "LearningContext target_language cannot be empty."
            )
        if not self.learner_level.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "LearningContext learner_level cannot be empty."
            )
        if len(self.country_code.strip()) != 2:
            raise ValueError(
                "LearningContext country_code must be a two-letter code."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PodcastSearchIntent:
    """
    Provider-neutral search intent produced by the Podcast Agent.

    The Agent does not know whether Apple or Spotify will execute it.
    """

    user_id: str
    learning_context: LearningContext
    topics: tuple[str, ...]
    keywords: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    language_required: bool = True
    max_results: int = 10
    intent_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("PodcastSearchIntent user_id cannot be empty.")
        if not self.topics and not self.keywords:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastSearchIntent requires at least one topic or keyword."
            )
        if self.max_results < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastSearchIntent max_results must be at least 1."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PodcastSearchResult:
    """Normalised podcast/episode candidate from any provider."""

    provider: PodcastProviderName
    external_id: str
    title: str
    description: str
    publisher: str
    languages: tuple[str, ...]
    url: str
    image_url: str | None = None
    published_at: str | None = None
    duration_seconds: int | None = None
    explicit: bool = False
    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name, value in {
            "external_id": self.external_id,
            "title": self.title,
            "publisher": self.publisher,
            "url": self.url,
        }.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"PodcastSearchResult {name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if not (
            self.url.startswith("https://")
            or self.url.startswith("http://")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastSearchResult url must use HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

        if not self.languages:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastSearchResult requires at least one language."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PodcastRecommendation:
    """Character-facing recommendation selected from verified candidates."""

    recommendation_id: str
    user_id: str
    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    character_message: str
    result: PodcastSearchResult
    relevance_reason: str
    match_score: float

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for name, value in {
            "recommendation_id": self.recommendation_id,
            "user_id": self.user_id,
            "character_id": self.character_id,
            "character_name": self.character_name,
            "character_message": self.character_message,
            "relevance_reason": self.relevance_reason,
        }.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"PodcastRecommendation {name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if not 0 <= self.match_score <= 100:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastRecommendation match_score must be between 0 and 100."
            )


LANGUAGE_ALIASES: Final[dict[str, str]] = {
    "fr": "fr", "fra": "fr", "fre": "fr",
    "french": "fr", "français": "fr", "francais": "fr",
    "en": "en", "eng": "en", "english": "en",
    "es": "es", "spa": "es", "spanish": "es", "español": "es",
    "de": "de", "deu": "de", "ger": "de", "german": "de",
    "it": "it", "ita": "it", "italian": "it",
    "ru": "ru", "rus": "ru", "russian": "ru",
    "pt": "pt", "por": "pt", "portuguese": "pt",
}


def normalise_language(value: str) -> str:
    """Return a stable ISO-639-1-style code when recognised."""
    return LANGUAGE_ALIASES.get(
        value.strip().lower(),
        value.strip().lower(),
    )

class PodcastProvider(Protocol):

    Apple and Spotify adapters implement this boundary; the Agent never
    depends on either provider.
    """

    def search(
        self,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        """Search the provider catalogue using provider-neutral intent."""
        ...


============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\service.py
============================================================
"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — personalised podcast recommendation service.

This is the application-level selection boundary.

Language is a HARD filter because EasyChatBot is a language-learning
platform. Preference/topic relevance is evaluated only after a candidate
is language-eligible.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from uuid import uuid4

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastRecommendation,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
    normalise_language,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.providers import PodcastProvider


class PodcastRecommendationService:
    """Search providers and select a personalised candidate."""

    def __init__(self, providers: list[PodcastProvider]) -> None:
        if not providers:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastRecommendationService requires at least one provider."
            )
        self._providers = list(providers)

    def recommend(
        self,
        *,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
        character_id: str,
        character_name: str,
        character_message: str,
    ) -> PodcastRecommendation:
        candidates: list[PodcastSearchResult] = []

        for provider in self._providers:
            candidates.extend(provider.search(intent))

        ranked = self.rank_candidates(
            candidates=candidates,
            intent=intent,
        )

        if not ranked:
            raise LookupError(
                "No podcast matched the learner's language and preferences."
            )

        result, score, reason = ranked[0]

        return PodcastRecommendation(
            recommendation_id=str(uuid4()),
            user_id=intent.user_id,
            character_id=character_id,
            character_name=character_name,
            character_message=character_message,
            result=result,
            relevance_reason=reason,
            match_score=score,
        )

    @classmethod
    def rank_candidates(
        cls,
        *,
        candidates: list[PodcastSearchResult],
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[tuple[PodcastSearchResult, float, str]]:
        ranked: list[tuple[PodcastSearchResult, float, str]] = []

        target_language = normalise_language(
            intent.learning_context.target_language
        )

        preference_terms = [
            term.lower()
            for term in list(intent.topics) + list(intent.keywords)
            if term.strip()
        ]

        for candidate in candidates:
            candidate_languages = {
                normalise_language(language)
                for language in candidate.languages
            }

            # Critical learning-platform rule:
            # unknown/wrong-language content is not eligible.
            if (
                intent.language_required
                and target_language not in candidate_languages
            ):
                continue

            haystack = " ".join(
                [
                    candidate.title,
                    candidate.description,
                    candidate.publisher,
                ]
            ).lower()

            matched_terms = [
                term
                for term in preference_terms
                if cls._term_matches(term, haystack)
            ]

            score = 50.0

            if matched_terms:
                score += min(30.0, len(matched_terms) * 10.0)

            if intent.learning_context.world_id.lower() in haystack:
                score += 10.0

            level = intent.learning_context.learner_level.lower()
            if level and level in haystack:
                score += 10.0

            reason = cls._build_reason(
                language=intent.learning_context.target_language,
                matched_terms=matched_terms,
                world_id=intent.learning_context.world_id,
            )

            ranked.append(
                (candidate, min(score, 100.0), reason)
            )

        ranked.sort(
            key=lambda item: (
                item[1],
                item[0].published_at or "",
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )

        return ranked[: intent.max_results]

    @staticmethod
    def _term_matches(term: str, haystack: str) -> bool:
        term = term.strip().lower()
        if not term:
            return False
        if term in haystack:
            return True

        words = [
            word
            for word in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9À-ÿ'-]+", term)
            if len(word) > 2
        ]
        return bool(words) and any(
            word in haystack
            for word in words
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_reason(
        *,
        language: str,
        matched_terms: list[str],
        world_id: str,
    ) -> str:
        reasons = [f"It is available in {language}."]

        if matched_terms:
            reasons.append(
                "It matches your interests in "
                + ", ".join(matched_terms[:3])
                + "."
            )

        reasons.append(
            f"It fits your {world_id} learning world."
        )

        return " ".join(reasons)


============================================================
FILE: src\ai_runtime\podcast\spotify.py
============================================================
"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon — Spotify podcast catalogue provider.

The MVP searches episodes, because episode metadata exposes a `languages`
field. This allows EasyChatBot's language-learning rule to remain strict.

Credentials are injected as an access token; this module does not manage
OAuth state or user accounts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
    normalise_language,
)


class SpotifyPodcastProvider:
    """Read-only Spotify catalogue provider."""

    BASE_URL = "https://api.spotify.com/v1/search"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        access_token: str,
        timeout_seconds: float = 10.0,
        market: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if not access_token.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "SpotifyPodcastProvider access_token cannot be empty."
            )
        self._access_token = access_token
        self._timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds
        self._market = market

    def search(
        self,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        query_terms = list(intent.topics) + list(intent.keywords)
        params = {
            "q": " ".join(query_terms[:6]),
            "type": "episode",
            "limit": str(intent.max_results),
        }

        market = self._market or intent.learning_context.country_code
        if market:
            params["market"] = market.upper()

        request = Request(
            f"{self.BASE_URL}?{urlencode(params)}",
            headers={
                "Accept": "application/json",
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {self._access_token}",
                "User-Agent": "EasyChatBot-Hackathon/1.0",
            },
            method="GET",
        )

        with urlopen(
            request,
            timeout=self._timeout_seconds,
        ) as response:
            payload = json.loads(
                response.read().decode("utf-8")
            )

        episodes = payload.get("episodes", {})
        if not isinstance(episodes, dict):
            return []

        items = episodes.get("items", [])
        if not isinstance(items, list):
            return []

        results: list[PodcastSearchResult] = []

        for item in items:
            if not isinstance(item, dict):
                continue

            result = self._normalise_episode(item)
            if result is not None:
                results.append(result)

        return results

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_episode(
        item: dict[str, object],
    ) -> PodcastSearchResult | None:
        external_id = str(item.get("id") or "")
        title = str(item.get("name") or "")

        external_urls = item.get("external_urls")
        url = ""
        if isinstance(external_urls, dict):
            url = str(
                external_urls.get("spotify") or ""
            )

        if not external_id or not title or not url:
            return None

        raw_languages = item.get("languages", [])
        if not isinstance(raw_languages, list):
            return None

        languages = tuple(
            normalise_language(str(language))
            for language in raw_languages
            if str(language).strip()
        )

        # Unknown language is not invented. The recommendation service
        # will reject a candidate if language_required=True.
        if not languages:
            return None

        images = item.get("images", [])
        image_url = None
        if isinstance(images, list) and images:
            first = images[0]
            if isinstance(first, dict):
                image_url = str(first.get("url") or "") or None

        duration_ms = item.get("duration_ms")
        duration_seconds = (
            int(duration_ms) // 1000
            if isinstance(duration_ms, (int, float))
            else None
        )

        return PodcastSearchResult(
            provider=PodcastProviderName.SPOTIFY,
            external_id=external_id,
            title=title,
            description=str(item.get("description") or ""),
            publisher="Spotify Podcast",
            languages=languages,
            url=url,
            image_url=image_url,
            published_at=str(
                item.get("release_date") or ""
            ) or None,
            duration_seconds=duration_seconds,
            explicit=bool(item.get("explicit", False)),
            metadata={
                "provider": "spotify",
                "content_type": "episode",
            },
        )


============================================================
FILE: src\challenge_engine\generator.py
============================================================
"""
Challenge Engine Generator.

Transforms structured intelligence output into
a learner-facing challenge.

Architecture:

Live Intelligence Agent
        |
        v
Challenge Generator
        |
        v
LearningChallenge

Responsibilities:
- Convert research intelligence into learning structure
- Apply learner level/language context
- Produce deterministic challenge objects

Non-responsibilities:
- Search
- Provider calls
- Agent orchestration
- Direct LLM dependency

Future extension:
- Inject LLM-based content generation service
- Add templates/configuration-driven challenges
"""

from typing import Protocol, List

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceResponse,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)


class ChallengeContentGenerator(Protocol):
    """
    Contract for optional AI-powered content generation.

    Keeps Challenge Engine independent from
    a specific LLM provider.
    """

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> List[VocabularyItem]:
        ...

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> List[DiscussionQuestion]:
        ...


class ChallengeGenerator:
    """
    Generates learning challenges from intelligence output.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        content_generator: ChallengeContentGenerator,
    ) -> None:
        self._content_generator = content_generator

    async def generate(
        self,
        intelligence: LiveIntelligenceResponse,
        language: str = "French",
        learner_level: str = "B2",
    ) -> LearningChallenge:
        """
        Transform intelligence into learning challenge.

        Flow:

        Research Output
              |
              v
        Vocabulary Generation
              |
              v
        Discussion Generation
              |
              v
        Learning Challenge
        """

        if not intelligence.summary.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence summary cannot be empty"
            )

        vocabulary = await self._content_generator.generate_vocabulary(
            summary=intelligence.summary,
            language=language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
        )

        questions = await self._content_generator.generate_questions(
            summary=intelligence.summary,
            language=language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
        )

        return LearningChallenge(
            title=f"{intelligence.topic} Learning Challenge",
            topic=intelligence.topic,
            language=language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
            summary=intelligence.summary,
            vocabulary=vocabulary,
            discussion_questions=questions,
        )

"""
AI Evaluation Criteria.

Defines the standard quality criteria used by the EasyChatBot
evaluation framework.

This module contains evaluation configuration only.

- call AI providers
- execute agents
- perform searches
- modify application state
- depend on the Conversation Agent

from src.evaluation.models import EvaluationCriterion


FACTUALITY = EvaluationCriterion(

        "Information should be accurate and supported by "

)

    name="language_level",

        "The learning content should be appropriate for "
