============================================================
FILE: src\evaluation\deterministic_evaluator.py
============================================================
"""
Deterministic AI Output Evaluator.

Evaluates a LearningChallenge using deterministic,
provider-independent quality checks.

Architecture:

LearningChallenge
        |
        v
DeterministicEvaluator
        |
        v
CriterionScore[]
        |
        v
EvaluationEngine

This module does not:
- call an LLM
- call external providers
- perform web searches
- execute agents
- modify application state

The evaluator is intentionally deterministic so that
evaluation results are reproducible.
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import LearningChallenge
from src.evaluation.models import CriterionScore


class DeterministicEvaluator:
    """
    Produces deterministic quality scores for a
    LearningChallenge.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        challenge: LearningChallenge,
    ) -> list[CriterionScore]:
        """
        Evaluate all supported quality dimensions.

        Returns:
            Criterion scores normalised to 0.0–1.0.
        """

        return [
            self._evaluate_completeness(challenge),
            self._evaluate_language_level(challenge),
            self._evaluate_educational_value(challenge),
            self._evaluate_source_coverage(challenge),
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _evaluate_completeness(
        challenge: LearningChallenge,
    ) -> CriterionScore:
        """
        Evaluate whether the challenge contains its
        required core learning components.
        """

        checks = [
            bool(challenge.title.strip()),
            bool(challenge.topic.strip()),
            bool(challenge.summary.strip()),
            bool(challenge.language.strip()),
            bool(challenge.learner_level.strip()),
            bool(challenge.vocabulary),
            bool(challenge.discussion_questions),
        ]

        score = sum(checks) / len(checks)

        return CriterionScore(
            criterion="completeness",
            score=score,
            reasoning=(
                "Checks whether the challenge contains "
                "its required learning components."
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _evaluate_language_level(
        challenge: LearningChallenge,
    ) -> CriterionScore:
        """
        Evaluate whether the challenge contains a valid
        learner language level.

        This is intentionally conservative. It does not
        claim linguistic correctness without an LLM or
        specialist language evaluator.
        """

        supported_levels = {
            "A1",
            "A2",
            "B1",
            "B2",
            "C1",
            "C2",
        }

        score = (
            1.0
            if challenge.learner_level.upper()
            in supported_levels
            else 0.0
        )

        return CriterionScore(
            criterion="language_level",
            score=score,
            reasoning=(
                "Validates that the challenge specifies "
                "a recognised CEFR learner level."
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _evaluate_educational_value(
        challenge: LearningChallenge,
    ) -> CriterionScore:
        """
        Evaluate basic educational structure.

        A challenge receives credit for containing:
        - vocabulary
        - definitions
        - discussion questions
        """

        if not challenge.vocabulary:
            vocabulary_score = 0.0
        else:
            vocabulary_score = sum(
                bool(
                    item.term.strip()
                    and item.definition.strip()
                )
                for item in challenge.vocabulary
            ) / len(challenge.vocabulary)

        if not challenge.discussion_questions:
            question_score = 0.0
        else:
            question_score = sum(
                bool(
                    question.question.strip()
                )
                for question
                in challenge.discussion_questions
            ) / len(challenge.discussion_questions)

        score = (
            vocabulary_score
            + question_score
        ) / 2

        return CriterionScore(
            criterion="educational_value",
            score=score,
            reasoning=(
                "Evaluates whether the challenge contains "
                "usable vocabulary and discussion practice."
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _evaluate_source_coverage(
        challenge: LearningChallenge,
    ) -> CriterionScore:
        """
        Evaluate whether the challenge identifies
        its originating agent.

        The current LearningChallenge contract does not
        carry source objects, so this criterion remains
        intentionally conservative.

        Full source-level verification belongs to a later
        evaluation layer that receives research evidence.
        """

        score = (
            1.0
            if challenge.created_by_agent.strip()
            else 0.0
        )

        return CriterionScore(
            criterion="source_coverage",
            score=score,
            reasoning=(
                "Checks that the challenge records the "
                "originating intelligence agent. Full "
                "source verification requires research "
                "evidence and is outside this evaluator."
            ),
        )

============================================================
FILE: src\evaluation\engine.py
============================================================
"""
AI Evaluation Engine.

Combines individual criterion scores into a weighted
overall evaluation result.

Architecture:

Criterion Scores
       |
       v
Evaluation Engine
       |
       v
Evaluation Result

This module performs deterministic scoring only.

It does not:
- call an LLM
- call external providers
- perform web searches
- execute agents
- modify application state
"""

from collections.abc import Sequence

from src.evaluation.models import (
    CriterionScore,
    EvaluationCriterion,
    EvaluationResult,
)


class EvaluationEngine:
    """
    Deterministic evaluation scoring engine.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        criteria: Sequence[EvaluationCriterion],
        passing_score: float = 0.70,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the evaluation engine.

        Args:
            criteria:
                Evaluation criteria and their weights.

            passing_score:
                Minimum normalised score required to pass.

        Raises:
            ValueError:
                If criteria are invalid or passing_score
                is outside the supported range.
        """

        if not criteria:
            raise ValueError(
                "At least one evaluation criterion is required"
            )

        if passing_score < 0.0 or passing_score > 1.0:
            raise ValueError(
                "passing_score must be between 0.0 and 1.0"
            )

        for criterion in criteria:
            if criterion.weight <= 0:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Criterion '{criterion.name}' "
                    "must have a positive weight"
                )

        self._criteria = tuple(criteria)
        self._passing_score = passing_score

    def evaluate(
        self,
        scores: Sequence[CriterionScore],
    ) -> EvaluationResult:
        """
        Calculate the weighted evaluation result.

        Scores must be normalised to the range 0.0–1.0.

        Args:
            scores:
                Individual criterion scores.

        Returns:
            EvaluationResult.
        """

        self._validate_scores(scores)

        criterion_weights = {
            criterion.name: criterion.weight
            for criterion in self._criteria
        }

        total_weight = sum(
            criterion.weight
            for criterion in self._criteria
        )

        weighted_score = sum(
            score.score
            * criterion_weights[score.criterion]
            for score in scores
        )

        overall_score = weighted_score / total_weight

        return EvaluationResult(
            overall_score=overall_score,
            scores=list(scores),
            passed=overall_score >= self._passing_score,
        )

    def _validate_scores(
        self,
        scores: Sequence[CriterionScore],
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate criterion scores against configured criteria.
        """

        if not scores:
            raise ValueError(
                "At least one criterion score is required"
            )

        configured_names = {
            criterion.name
            for criterion in self._criteria
        }

        seen_names: set[str] = set()

        for score in scores:

            if score.criterion not in configured_names:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Unknown evaluation criterion: "
                    f"{score.criterion}"
                )

            if score.criterion in seen_names:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Duplicate evaluation criterion: "
                    f"{score.criterion}"
                )

            if score.score < 0.0 or score.score > 1.0:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Score for '{score.criterion}' "
                    "must be between 0.0 and 1.0"
                )

            seen_names.add(score.criterion)

"""
AI Evaluation Domain Models.

Defines provider-independent contracts for evaluating
EasyChatBot AI outputs.

Architecture:

AI Output
    |
    v
Evaluation Engine
    |
    v
Evaluation Result

This module contains domain models only.

It must not:
- call an LLM
- call an external provider
- execute agents
- modify application data
- depend on the Conversation Agent
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvaluationCriterion:
    """
    Defines one quality criterion.
    """

    name: str
    description: str
    weight: float = 1.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CriterionScore:
    """
    Score assigned to one evaluation criterion.
    """

    criterion: str
    score: float
    reasoning: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvaluationResult:
    """
    Complete evaluation result for an AI output.
    """

    overall_score: float
    scores: List[CriterionScore]
    passed: bool
    evaluator: str = "deterministic"

============================================================
FILE: src\evaluation\report.py
============================================================
"""
AI Evaluation Quality Report.

Transforms an EvaluationResult into a human-readable
quality report.

Architecture:

EvaluationResult
        ↓
QualityReport
        ↓
Human-readable output

This module is presentation-independent domain formatting.

It does not:
- execute agents
- call LLMs
- call external providers
- perform searches
- modify application state
"""

from src.evaluation.models import (
    CriterionScore,
    EvaluationResult,
)


class EvaluationReport:
    """
    Generates a human-readable quality report.
    """

    def generate(
        self,
        result: EvaluationResult,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Generate a text quality report.
        """

        lines: list[str] = [
            "EasyChatBot AI Quality Report",
            "=" * 32,
            "",
            f"Overall Score: {result.overall_score:.2f}",
            f"Status: {'PASS' if result.passed else 'FAIL'}",
            f"Evaluator: {result.evaluator}",
            "",
            "Criteria",
            "-" * 32,
        ]

        for score in result.scores:
            lines.extend(
                self._format_score(score)
            )

        lines.extend(
            [
                "",
                "=" * 32,
            ]
        )

        return "\n".join(lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _format_score(
        score: CriterionScore,
    ) -> list[str]:
        """
        Format one criterion score.
        """

        return [
            f"{score.criterion}: {score.score:.2f}",
            f"  {score.reasoning}",
            "",
        ]

============================================================
FILE: src\governance\engine.py
============================================================
"""
AI Governance Policy Engine.

Applies explicit governance policies to AI capabilities.

Architecture:

Capability Request
        |
        v
Governance Policy Engine
        |
        v
Governance Result

Decision model:
- ALLOW   → capability is permitted
- REVIEW  → human review is required
- BLOCK   → capability is prohibited

This module does not:
- execute agents
- call LLMs
- call external providers
- modify application state
- perform external side effects
"""

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    GovernanceResult,
    RiskLevel,
)


class GovernancePolicyEngine:
    """
    Deterministic governance decision engine.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        policy: GovernancePolicy,
    ) -> GovernanceResult:
        """
        Evaluate a governance policy.

        Decision precedence:

        1. Side effects are blocked by default.
        2. Critical risk requires blocking.
        3. Explicit human review requires REVIEW.
        4. Otherwise the capability is ALLOWED.
        """

        reasons: list[str] = []

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Side effects
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        if policy.allows_side_effects:
            return GovernanceResult(
                capability=policy.capability,
                decision=GovernanceDecision.BLOCK,
                risk_level=policy.risk_level,
                reasons=(
                    "Uncontrolled side effects are prohibited.",
                ),
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Critical risk
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        if policy.risk_level == RiskLevel.CRITICAL:
            return GovernanceResult(
                capability=policy.capability,
                decision=GovernanceDecision.BLOCK,
                risk_level=policy.risk_level,
                reasons=(
                    "Critical-risk capabilities require "
                    "stronger controls than this policy permits.",
                ),
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # High-risk operations
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        if policy.risk_level == RiskLevel.HIGH:
            reasons.append(
                "High-risk capability requires human review."
            )

            return GovernanceResult(
                capability=policy.capability,
                decision=GovernanceDecision.REVIEW,
                risk_level=policy.risk_level,
                reasons=tuple(reasons),
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Explicit human review
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        if policy.requires_human_review:
            reasons.append(
                "Policy explicitly requires human review."
            )

            return GovernanceResult(
                capability=policy.capability,
                decision=GovernanceDecision.REVIEW,
                risk_level=policy.risk_level,
                reasons=tuple(reasons),
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # External calls
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        if policy.allows_external_calls:
            reasons.append(
                "External calls are explicitly permitted."
            )
        else:
            reasons.append(
                "No external calls are required."
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Default allow
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        reasons.append(
            "No blocking governance condition was detected."
        )

        return GovernanceResult(
            capability=policy.capability,
            decision=GovernanceDecision.ALLOW,
            risk_level=policy.risk_level,
            reasons=tuple(reasons),
        )

============================================================
FILE: src\governance\models.py
============================================================
"""
AI Governance Domain Models.

Defines provider-independent governance contracts for
EasyChatBot AI capabilities.

Architecture:

AI Capability
      |
      v
Governance Policy
      |
      v
Governance Decision

This module contains domain models only.

It must not:
- execute agents
- call LLMs
- call external providers
- modify production systems
- depend on the Conversation Agent
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum


class RiskLevel(str, Enum):
    """
    AI operation risk classification.
    """

    LOW = "low"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    HIGH = "high"
    CRITICAL = "critical"


class GovernanceDecision(str, Enum):
    """
    Decision produced by governance evaluation.
    """

    ALLOW = "allow"
    REVIEW = "review"
    BLOCK = "block"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Defines governance requirements for an AI capability.
    """

    capability: str
    risk_level: RiskLevel
    requires_human_review: bool = False
    allows_external_calls: bool = False
    allows_side_effects: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GovernanceResult:
    """
    Result of applying governance policy to an operation.
    """

    capability: str
    decision: GovernanceDecision
    risk_level: RiskLevel
    reasons: tuple[str, ...]

============================================================
FILE: src\providers\search_provider\interface.py
============================================================
"""
Search Provider Interface

Defines the provider-independent contract for external search capabilities.

Architecture:
Agent -> Provider Interface -> Provider Adapter

The agent must never depend directly on a vendor SDK.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResult:
    """
    Normalised search result returned by any provider.
    """

    title: str
    url: str
    content: str
    source: str


class SearchProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract search provider contract.

    Implementations:
    - ParallelSearchProvider
    - Future providers
    """

    @abstractmethod
    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> List[SearchResult]:
        """
        Execute a search query.

        Args:
            query:
                Search query.

            max_results:
                Maximum number of results.

        Returns:
            Normalised search results.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

============================================================
FILE: src\providers\search_provider\parallel_adapter.py
============================================================
"""
Parallel Search Provider Adapter.

Bridges the provider-independent SearchProvider contract
to the existing EasyChatBot ParallelClientWrapper.

Architecture:

Live Intelligence Agent
        |
        v
SearchProvider Interface
        |
        v
ParallelSearchProvider
        |
        v
Existing ParallelClientWrapper
        |
        v
Parallel Search API

The official Parallel SDK remains isolated inside:

    backend/providers/parallel/parallel_client.py

This adapter must not import or instantiate the Parallel SDK
directly.
"""

from typing import Any

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
    ParallelClientWrapper,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


class ParallelSearchProvider(SearchProvider):
    """
    SearchProvider implementation backed by the existing
    ParallelClientWrapper.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str | None = None,
        client: ParallelClientWrapper | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the provider.

        Args:
            api_key:
                Optional Parallel API key.

                If omitted, the existing ParallelClientWrapper
                loads PARALLEL_API_KEY from the environment/.env.

            client:
                Optional injected client for testing.
        """

        self._client = client or ParallelClientWrapper(
            api_key=api_key
        )

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        """
        Execute a Parallel search and normalise its results.

        Args:
            query:
                Natural-language research objective.

            max_results:
                Maximum number of results returned.

        Returns:
            Provider-independent SearchResult objects.
        """

        if not query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Search query cannot be empty"
            )

        if max_results <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_results must be greater than zero"
            )

        response = await self._client.search(
            query=query
        )

        raw_results = self._extract_results(
            response
        )

        normalised_results: list[SearchResult] = []

        for raw_result in raw_results[:max_results]:
            result = self._normalise_result(
                raw_result
            )

            if result is not None:
                normalised_results.append(result)

        return normalised_results

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_results(
        response: Any,
    ) -> list[Any]:
        """
        Extract the actual result collection from the
        ParallelClientWrapper response.

        The existing wrapper returns:

            {
                "query": "...",
                "search_queries": [...],
                "results": <normalised SDK response>
            }

        The SDK response may itself contain:

            {
                "results": [...]
            }

        Therefore this method handles both levels of wrapping.
        """

        if not isinstance(response, dict):
            raise TypeError(
                "Parallel client response must be a dictionary"
            )

        raw_results = response.get(
            "results",
            []
        )

        # The SDK response itself may contain a nested
        # "results" collection.
        if isinstance(raw_results, dict):
            nested_results = raw_results.get(
                "results"
            )

            if isinstance(nested_results, list):
                return nested_results

            # Some SDK versions may expose the result
            # collection under another common field.
            nested_data = raw_results.get(
                "data"
            )

            if isinstance(nested_data, list):
                return nested_data

        if isinstance(raw_results, list):
            return raw_results

        # If the SDK returned an object that was not converted
        # to a dictionary, try to inspect its result attribute.
        nested_results = getattr(
            raw_results,
            "results",
            None,
        )

        if isinstance(nested_results, list):
            return nested_results

        raise TypeError(
            "Parallel search response does not contain "
            "a supported results collection"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_result(
        raw_result: Any,
    ) -> SearchResult | None:
        """
        Convert one Parallel result into SearchResult.

        Parallel results may arrive either as dictionaries
        or SDK/Pydantic objects.
        """

        if isinstance(raw_result, dict):
            title = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "title",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "url",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            excerpts = raw_result.get(
                "excerpts",
                []
            )

            # Support providers/SDK versions that use
            # "snippet" or "content" instead.
            if not excerpts:
                excerpts = raw_result.get(
                    "snippet",
                    raw_result.get(
                        "content",
                        []
                    ),
                )

        else:
            title = str(
                getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "title",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "url",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            excerpts = getattr(
                raw_result,
                "excerpts",
                None,
            )

            if not excerpts:
                excerpts = getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "snippet",
                    getattr(
                        raw_result,
                        "content",
                        [],
                    ),
                )

        if not title or not url:
            return None

        content = ParallelSearchProvider._normalise_excerpts(
            excerpts
        )

        return SearchResult(
            title=title,
            url=url,
            content=content,
            source="parallel",
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_excerpts(
        excerpts: Any,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Convert Parallel excerpts/content into a single
        application-level content string.
        """

        if excerpts is None:
            return ""

        if isinstance(excerpts, str):
            return excerpts.strip()

        if isinstance(excerpts, list):
            return "\n\n".join(
                str(excerpt).strip()
                for excerpt in excerpts
                if str(excerpt).strip()
            )

        return str(excerpts).strip()

FILE: test_hackathon_judge_demo_payment_ready.py

"""
Acceptance tests for the Windows-safe judge-facing EasyChatBot demo.

The judge demo now includes the Payment Pass contract. The commerce
assertions therefore validate the current presentation contract:

GET DEAL
    ->
Payment Pass
    ->
READY / DEMO
    ->
No payment executed
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_judge_demo.py"
)


def run_judge_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real judge-facing demonstration."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )


def test_judge_demo_succeeds():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Judge demo failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    assert (
        "EasyChatBot - JUDGE DEMONSTRATION"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Discover -> Understand -> Personalise -> "
        "Recommend -> Act"
        in output
    )

    assert "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS" in output


def test_judge_demo_contains_all_capabilities():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "1 - GOVERNANCE" in output
    assert "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output
    assert "3 - LEARNING" in output
    assert "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output
    assert "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY" in output
    assert "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY" in output
    assert "7 - FLASH DEAL" in output
    assert "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY" in output


def test_judge_demo_shows_personalised_discovery():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "Yeti" in output
    assert "Look what I found!" in output
    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful." in output
    assert "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU" in output
    assert "Demo Vendor" in output


def test_judge_demo_shows_commerce_boundary():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "GET DEAL -> vendor handoff" in output

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output

    assert "Payment status: READY" in output

    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]" in output

    assert "No payment has been executed." in output

    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    assert "[PASS] Payment execution: NO" in output

    assert "[PASS] Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "EasyChatBot is not a shop." in output


def test_judge_demo_preserves_execution_order():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "1 - GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    learning = output.index(
        "3 - LEARNING"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    character = output.index(
        "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY"
    )

    deal = output.index(
        "7 - FLASH DEAL"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY"
    )

    payment = output.index(
        "PAYMENT PASS"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < learning
    assert learning < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < character
    assert character < deal
    assert deal < commerce
    assert commerce < payment
    assert payment < success

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (

    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
    InvalidCapabilityRequestError,
    RuntimeCapabilityError,

def test_all_runtime_errors_extend_runtime_capability_error():

    errors = (
        InvalidCapabilityRequestError,
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        CapabilityGovernanceError,

"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class FakeCapabilityExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Minimal executor used only to verify the contract.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )

    executor = FakeCapabilityExecutor()

    assert (
        executor.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_returns_provider_independent_result():

    executor = FakeCapabilityExecutor()

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="req-executor-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await executor.execute(request)

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        CapabilityResult,
    )

    assert (
        result.request_id
        == "req-executor-001"
    )

    assert (
        result.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.output == {
        "executed": True,
    }

FILE: tests\ai_runtime\capabilities\test_gateway.py

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime Capability Gateway.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeGovernanceResult:
    """
    Minimal governance result for gateway tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed


class FakeGovernance:
    """
    Deterministic governance implementation for tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.calls = 0

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        self.calls += 1

        return FakeGovernanceResult(
            allowed=self.allowed
        )


class FailingGovernance:
    """
    Governance implementation that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "governance infrastructure failure"
        )


class FakeExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Fake executor used to verify gateway orchestration.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0
        self.last_request: CapabilityRequest | None = None

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        self.calls += 1
        self.last_request = request

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )


class FailingExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Executor that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "provider failure"
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_executes_allowed_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 1

    assert executor.last_request is request


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_rejects_blocked_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert executor.calls == 0

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "rejected"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_passes_original_request_to_executor():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-003",
        payload={
            "topic": "European AI regulation",
            "language": "French",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-003",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert executor.last_request is request

    assert (
        executor.last_request.payload["language"]
        == "French"
    )

    assert (
        executor.last_request.metadata["trace_id"]
        == "trace-003"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_is_evaluated_before_execution():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-004",
        payload={},
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_governance_failure():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FailingGovernance(),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityGovernanceError,
        match="Governance evaluation failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_executor_failure():

    executor = FailingExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True
        ),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        match="Capability executor failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

"""
Tests for the Runtime Live Intelligence executor adapter.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.live_intelligence_executor import (
    LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,

)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Fake implementation of the existing agent contract.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0
        self.last_request = None

    async def execute(
        self,

        self.calls += 1
        self.last_request = request

        return {
            "topic": request.topic,
            "language": request.language,
            "learner_level": request.learner_level,
            "summary": "AI Act learning content",

        == "B2"

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_applies_default_language_and_level():

    agent = FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
        agent=agent
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="live-runtime-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await executor.execute(
        request
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert (
        agent.last_request.language
        == "French"
    )

    assert (
        agent.last_request.learner_level
        == "B2"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_returns_agent_output():

    agent = FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
        agent=agent
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="live-runtime-003",
        payload={
            "topic": "European AI regulation",
        },
    )

    result = await executor.execute(
        request
    )

    assert result.output == {
        "topic": "European AI regulation",
        "language": "French",
        "learner_level": "B2",
        "summary": "AI Act learning content",
    }

    assert (
        result.metadata["executor"]
        == "live_intelligence_executor"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_rejects_wrong_capability():

    agent = FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
        agent=agent
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        request_id="live-runtime-004",
        payload={},
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="unsupported capability",
    ):
        await executor.execute(request)

    assert agent.calls == 0


def test_executor_requires_topic():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="live-runtime-005",
        payload={},
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="requires a string 'topic'",
    ):
        LiveIntelligenceExecutor._build_agent_request(
            request
        )


def test_executor_rejects_non_string_topic():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="live-runtime-006",
        payload={
            "topic": 123,
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="requires a string 'topic'",
    ):
        LiveIntelligenceExecutor._build_agent_request(
            request
        )

"""
Tests for AI Runtime capability contracts.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


def test_capability_request_accepts_valid_request():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="req-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    assert (
        request.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert request.request_id == "req-001"

    assert request.payload == {
        "topic": "AI Act",
    }

    assert request.requested_by == "application"


def test_capability_request_supports_metadata():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        request_id="req-002",
        payload={
            "challenge_id": "challenge-001",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-001",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    assert request.metadata["trace_id"] == "trace-001"

    assert (
        request.requested_by
        == "learning_application"
    )


def test_empty_request_id_is_rejected():

    try:
        CapabilityRequest(
            capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
            request_id="",
            payload={},
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "request_id" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected empty request_id to be rejected."
        )


def test_empty_requester_is_rejected():

    try:
        CapabilityRequest(
            capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
            request_id="req-003",
            payload={},
            requested_by="",
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "requested_by" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected empty requested_by to be rejected."
        )


def test_capability_result_reports_success():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-004",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
        output={
            "summary": "AI Act research",
        },
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True
    assert result.failed is False
    assert result.rejected is False


def test_capability_result_reports_failure():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-005",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.FAILED,
        error="Provider unavailable.",
    )

    assert result.succeeded is False
    assert result.failed is True
    assert result.rejected is False


def test_capability_result_reports_rejection():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-006",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED,
        error="Governance policy blocked execution.",
    )

    assert result.succeeded is False
    assert result.failed is False
    assert result.rejected is True


def test_capability_result_preserves_metadata():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-007",
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.ACCEPTED,
        metadata={
            "governance_decision": "allow",
        },
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "allow"
    )

FILE: tests\ai_runtime\capabilities\test_registry.py
============================================================
"""
Tests for the AI Runtime capability registry.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Fake executor used to test dependency injection.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"ok": True},
        )


class FakeEvaluationExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Second fake executor used to verify multiple
    capability registrations.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.EVALUATION

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"evaluated": True},
        )


def test_registry_registers_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(executor)

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


def test_registry_resolves_registered_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    resolved = registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert resolved is executor


def test_registry_supports_multiple_capabilities():

    live_executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    evaluation_executor = FakeEvaluationExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            live_executor,
            evaluation_executor,
        ]
    )

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    ) is live_executor

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
    ) is evaluation_executor


def test_duplicate_capability_registration_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(
        FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        match="already registered",
    ):
        registry.register(
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
        )


def test_unknown_capability_resolution_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        match="No executor registered",
    ):
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        )


def test_contains_returns_false_for_unknown_capability():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    assert (
        registry.contains(
            CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        )
        is False
    )


def test_capabilities_returns_registered_capabilities():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(),
            FakeEvaluationExecutor(),
        ]
    )

    capabilities = registry.capabilities()

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        not in capabilities
    )

"""
Regression tests for the CommerceService payment boundary.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentPass,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)


def make_payment_pass() -> PaymentPass:
    """Create a valid demo payment pass."""

    return PaymentPass(
        payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        user_id="user-001",
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        amount=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        checkout_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_commerce_service_prepares_demo_payment():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    result = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert result is payment_pass
    assert result.is_ready is True
    assert result.is_demo_only is True
    assert result.payment_executed is False


def test_commerce_service_does_not_change_payment_pass():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    result = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert result.payment_pass_id == payment_pass.payment_pass_id
    assert result.deal_id == payment_pass.deal_id
    assert result.user_id == payment_pass.user_id
    assert result.amount == payment_pass.amount
    assert result.currency == payment_pass.currency
    assert result.checkout_url == payment_pass.checkout_url


def test_commerce_service_rejects_non_ready_payment_pass():

    class InvalidPaymentPass:
        is_ready = False
        is_demo_only = True

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="PaymentPass must be READY",
    ):
        CommerceService().prepare_payment(
            InvalidPaymentPass()
        )


def test_commerce_service_rejects_non_demo_payment():

    class InvalidPaymentPass:
        is_ready = True
        is_demo_only = False

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="DEMO execution mode",
    ):
        CommerceService().prepare_payment(
            InvalidPaymentPass()
        )

FILE: tests\ai_runtime\commerce\test_discovery_to_commerce.py

"""
Integration test for:

DealCard
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)


def test_deal_can_become_commerce_action():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/001"
        ),
    )

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=link,
    )

    action = CommerceAction(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal_id=deal.deal_id,
        action_type=(
            CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
        ),
        destination_url=(
            deal.commerce_link.click_url
        ),
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert (
        result.deal_id
        == deal.deal_id
    )

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == deal.commerce_link.click_url
    )

"""
Tests for Flash Deal commerce integration.

These tests verify that a FlashDealCard can produce both:
- the existing CommerceAction
- the new demo-only PaymentPass

No payment is executed.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentExecutionMode,
    PaymentPassStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


def make_card() -> FlashDealCard:
    """Create a deterministic FlashDealCard for tests."""

    return FlashDealCard(
        deal_id="deal-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "Look what I found!"
        ),
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
        ),
        current_price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        savings=199.01,
        relevance_reason=(
            "Matches the user's preference for "
            "powerful mobile photography devices."
        ),
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        more_information_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
        get_deal_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_flash_deal_commerce_still_creates_existing_action():

    card = make_card()

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="user-001",
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == "user-001"
    assert action.deal_id == "deal-001"
    assert (
        action.action_type
        is CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )
    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )


def test_flash_deal_creates_demo_payment_pass():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
        )
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.payment_pass_id
        == "payment-pass-001"
    )
    assert payment_pass.user_id == "user-001"
    assert payment_pass.deal_id == card.deal_id
    assert payment_pass.product_name == card.product_name
    assert payment_pass.vendor_name == card.vendor_name
    assert payment_pass.amount == card.current_price
    assert payment_pass.currency == card.currency
    assert (
        payment_pass.checkout_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False


def test_flash_deal_payment_pass_preserves_checkout_boundary():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.checkout_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False


def test_flash_deal_payment_pass_rejects_empty_user():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Commerce user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="",
            card=make_card(),
        )

"""
Tests for the hackathon PaymentPass.

The PaymentPass is a demo-only checkout handoff.

These tests verify:
- valid construction
- immutable/read-only behaviour
- READY status
- DEMO execution mode
- no executed payment
- validation
- safe checkout URL contract
- deterministic demo rendering
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentExecutionMode,
    PaymentPass,
    PaymentPassStatus,
)


def make_payment_pass() -> PaymentPass:
    """Create a valid PaymentPass for tests."""

    return PaymentPass(
        payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        user_id="user-001",
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        amount=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        checkout_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_payment_pass_creates_valid_demo_handoff():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert (
        payment_pass.payment_pass_id
        == "payment-pass-001"
    )
    assert payment_pass.deal_id == "deal-001"
    assert payment_pass.user_id == "user-001"
    assert payment_pass.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"
    assert payment_pass.vendor_name == "Demo Vendor"
    assert payment_pass.amount == 999.99
    assert payment_pass.currency == "EUR"
    assert (
        payment_pass.checkout_url
        == "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
    )


def test_payment_pass_is_ready():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert payment_pass.status is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    assert payment_pass.is_ready is True


def test_payment_pass_is_demo_only():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert (
        payment_pass.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )
    assert payment_pass.is_demo_only is True


def test_payment_pass_never_reports_payment_as_executed():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False


def test_payment_pass_formats_amount():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert (
        payment_pass.display_amount()
        == "EUR 999.99"
    )


def test_payment_pass_renders_demo_handoff():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    output = payment_pass.render_demo()

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output
    assert "Product: iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Vendor: Demo Vendor" in output
    assert "Amount: EUR 999.99" in output
    assert "Payment status: READY" in output
    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output
    assert "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]" in output
    assert "No payment has been executed." in output
    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "field,value",
    [
        ("payment_pass_id", ""),
        ("deal_id", ""),
        ("user_id", ""),
        ("product_name", ""),
        ("vendor_name", ""),
        ("currency", ""),
        ("checkout_url", ""),
    ],
)
def test_payment_pass_rejects_empty_required_text(
    field: str,
    value: str,
):

    values = {
        "payment_pass_id": "payment-pass-001",
        "deal_id": "deal-001",
        "user_id": "user-001",
        "product_name": "iPhone 17 Pro",
        "vendor_name": "Demo Vendor",
        "amount": 999.99,
        "currency": "EUR",
        "checkout_url": (
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    }

    values[field] = value

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=f"PaymentPass {field} cannot be empty",
    ):
        PaymentPass(**values)


def test_payment_pass_rejects_negative_amount():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="amount cannot be negative",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=-1.0,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=(
                "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
            ),
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "checkout_url",
    [
        "ftp://example.com/checkout",
        "vendor.example.com/checkout",
    ],
)
def test_payment_pass_rejects_non_http_checkout_url(
    checkout_url: str,
):

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="checkout_url must use HTTP or HTTPS",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=999.99,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=checkout_url,
        )


def test_payment_pass_rejects_non_demo_execution_mode():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="execution_mode must be DEMO",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=999.99,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=(
                "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
            ),
            execution_mode="LIVE",
        )


def test_payment_pass_rejects_non_ready_status():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="status must be READY",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=999.99,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=(
                "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
            ),
            status="EXECUTED",
        )


def test_payment_pass_is_immutable():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
        payment_pass.amount = 100.0

"""
Tests for the hackathon Commerce Service.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionResult,
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)


def make_action() -> CommerceAction:
    return CommerceAction(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        action_type=(
            CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
        ),
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/001"
        ),
    )


def test_commerce_action_is_created():

    action = make_action()

    assert action.user_id == "user-001"

    assert action.deal_id == "deal-001"

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == "https://example.com/product/001"
    )

    assert action.action_id


def test_commerce_service_returns_vendor_handoff():

    action = make_action()

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        CommerceActionResult,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == action.destination_url
    )

    assert result.deal_id == action.deal_id


def test_commerce_action_rejects_empty_user():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        CommerceAction(
            user_id="",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            action_type=(
                CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
            ),
            destination_url=(
                "https://example.com/product/001"
            ),
        )


def test_commerce_action_rejects_invalid_url():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="HTTP or HTTPS",
    ):
        CommerceAction(
            user_id="user-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            action_type=(
                CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
            ),
            destination_url="invalid-url",
        )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Discovery domain models.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    CommerceLinkType,
    DealCard,
    FlashIdea,
)


def test_commerce_link_uses_vendor_destination():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    assert link.vendor == "Example Vendor"

    assert (
        link.destination_url
        == "https://example.com/product/123"
    )

    assert (
        link.link_type
        == CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
    )

    assert (
        link.click_url
        == link.destination_url
    )


def test_commerce_link_supports_affiliate_url():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
        link_type=CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE,
        tracking_url=(
            "https://example.com/track/123"
        ),
    )

    assert (
        link.link_type
        == CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE
    )

    assert (
        link.click_url
        == "https://example.com/track/123"
    )


def test_commerce_link_rejects_invalid_url():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="HTTP or HTTPS",
    ):
        CommerceLink(
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            destination_url="not-a-url",
        )


def test_deal_card_contains_external_vendor_link():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=link,
        previous_price=129.0,
        discount_percentage=31.0,
        source="parallel",
    )

    assert deal.product_name == "Travel Backpack"

    assert deal.vendor == "Example Vendor"

    assert deal.price == 89.0

    assert deal.currency == "EUR"

    assert deal.commerce_link.click_url == (
        "https://example.com/product/123"
    )

    assert deal.deal_id


def test_deal_card_rejects_negative_price():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="price cannot be negative",
    ):
        DealCard(
            product_name="Travel Backpack",
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            price=-1,
            currency="EUR",
            commerce_link=link,
        )


def test_deal_card_rejects_vendor_mismatch():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Vendor A",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="vendor must match",
    ):
        DealCard(
            product_name="Travel Backpack",
            vendor="Vendor B",
            price=89.0,
            currency="EUR",
            commerce_link=link,
        )


def test_flash_idea_connects_character_to_deal():

    idea = FlashIdea(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        title="Yeti found this",
        message=(
            "I found a travel backpack "
            "that matches your style."
        ),
    )

    assert idea.character_id == "yeti"

    assert idea.deal_id == "deal-001"

    assert (
        idea.title
        == "Yeti found this"
    )

    assert (
        "travel backpack"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert idea.idea_id

"""
Tests for DiscoveryNotification.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.notification import (
    DiscoveryNotification,
)


def test_notification_is_created_from_flash_deal():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-001",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    assert notification.notification_id == (
        "notification-001"
    )

    assert notification.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert notification.character_id == "yeti"

    assert notification.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert notification.product_name == (
        deal.product_name
    )


def test_notification_preserves_flash_deal_identity():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-002",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    assert notification.deal is deal

    assert notification.get_deal_url == (
        deal.get_deal_url
    )

    assert notification.more_information_url == (
        deal.more_information_url
    )


def test_notification_contains_personalisation():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-003",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason == (
        deal.relevance_reason
    )


def test_notification_renders_compact_message():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-004",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    output = notification.render()

    assert "Yeti" in output

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert "Why this may interest you:" in output

    assert deal.relevance_reason in output


def test_notification_rejects_empty_notification_id():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    try:
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "notification_id cannot be empty" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected ValueError"
        )


def test_notification_rejects_empty_user_id():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    try:
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-005",
            user_id="",
            deal=deal,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "user_id cannot be empty" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected ValueError"
        )

FILE: tests\ai_runtime\discovery\test_discovery_service.py

"""
Tests for the hackathon DiscoveryService.

Flow:

DealCard
    +
Memory
    +
CharacterContext
    ↓
DiscoveryService
    ↓
FlashDealCard
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


def make_deal() -> DealCard:
    """
    Create deterministic discovery data for tests.
    """

    return DealCard(
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor="Demo Vendor",
        price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        commerce_link=CommerceLink(
            vendor="Demo Vendor",
            destination_url=(
                "https://example.com/product"
            ),
        ),
        source="test",
        description="Test discovery product.",
    )


def make_request() -> DiscoveryRequest:
    """
    Create a deterministic discovery request.
    """

    return DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal=make_deal(),
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/product.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
        ),
    )


def make_preference_memory() -> MemoryEntry:
    """
    Create explicit user preference memory.
    """

    return MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content=(
            "prefer powerful mobile photography "
            "devices"
        ),
    )


def test_discovery_service_creates_flash_deal():

    request = make_request()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request
    )

    assert result.deal_id == request.deal.deal_id

    assert result.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"

    assert result.vendor_name == "Demo Vendor"

    assert result.character_id == "yeti"

    assert result.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert result.current_price == 999.99

    assert result.discount_percentage == 17.0


def test_discovery_service_applies_preference_memory():

    request = make_request()

    memory = make_preference_memory()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert (
        "powerful mobile photography devices"
        in result.relevance_reason
    )


def test_discovery_service_does_not_require_memory():

    request = make_request()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[],
    )

    assert result.relevance_reason == (
        "This discovery may be relevant to your interests."
    )


def test_discovery_service_ignores_non_preference_memory():

    request = make_request()

    non_preference_type = next(
        memory_type
        for memory_type in MemoryType
        if memory_type != MemoryType.PREFERENCE
    )

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=non_preference_type,
        content=(
            "Discussed photography during the "
            "previous conversation."
        ),
    )

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert result.relevance_reason == (
        "This discovery may be relevant to your interests."
    )


def test_discovery_service_preserves_deal_identity():

    deal = make_deal()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message="I found something for you.",
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/product.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="A useful discovery.",
        highlights=(
            "Useful",
            "Practical",
        ),
    )

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request
    )

    assert result.deal_id == deal.deal_id


def test_discovery_service_preserves_character_context():

    request = make_request()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request
    )

    assert result.character_id == "yeti"

    assert result.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert result.character_message == (
        "Look what I found! "
        "This could be perfect for you."
    )


def test_discovery_service_rejects_empty_user_id():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        DiscoveryRequest(
            user_id="",
            deal=make_deal(),
            character=CharacterContext(
                character_id="yeti",
                character_name="Yeti",
                message="I found something.",
            ),
            product_image_url=(
                "https://example.com/product.jpg"
            ),
            tagline="A useful discovery.",
            highlights=("Useful",),
        )


def test_character_context_rejects_empty_character_id():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="character_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        CharacterContext(
            character_id="",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message="I found something.",
        )


def test_discovery_request_rejects_empty_highlights():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="at least one highlight",
    ):
        DiscoveryRequest(
            user_id="user-001",
            deal=make_deal(),
            character=CharacterContext(
                character_id="yeti",
                character_name="Yeti",
                message="I found something.",
            ),
            product_image_url=(
                "https://example.com/product.jpg"
            ),
            tagline="A useful discovery.",
            highlights=(),
        )
