"""
Tests for the hackathon Flash Deal Card presentation contract.

The tests validate the user-facing card described by the
hackathon UX:

Character
    ↓
Product
    ↓
Pricing / Deal
    ↓
Why relevant to you
    ↓
Vendor
    ↓
GET DEAL / MORE INFORMATION
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    CommerceLinkType,
    DealCard,
)


def make_deal(
    *,
    affiliate: bool = False,
) -> DealCard:
    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="eBay",
        destination_url=(
            "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
        ),
        link_type=(
            CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE
            if affiliate
            else CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
        ),
        tracking_url=(
            "https://tracking.example.com/deal/001"
            if affiliate
            else None
        ),
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor="eBay",
        price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        commerce_link=link,
        source="parallel",
        description="Pro smartphone with advanced camera system.",
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_is_created_from_deal():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason=(
            "This matches your interest in powerful "
            "mobile photography."
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
            "A19 Bionic chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "6.3-inch Super Retina XDR display",
        ],
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id
    assert card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert card.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert card.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"
    assert card.tagline == "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful."

    assert len(card.highlights) == 4

    assert card.current_price == 999.99
    assert card.previous_price == 1199.00
    assert card.discount_percentage == 17.0

    assert card.savings == 199.01

    assert card.vendor_name == "eBay"

    assert (
        card.more_information_url
        == "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_contains_personal_relevance():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason=(
            "You often look for strong mobile "
            "photography performance."
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
        ],
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == "You often look for strong mobile "
        "photography performance."
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_exposes_discount():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason="Relevant to you.",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=["Pro camera system"],
    )

    assert card.has_discount is True
    assert card.has_previous_price is True

    assert card.display_discount == "17% OFF"
    assert card.display_savings == "Save EUR 199.01"


def test_flash_deal_card_uses_affiliate_click_url():

    deal = make_deal(
        affiliate=True
    )

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason="Relevant to you.",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"

        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=["Pro camera system"],
    )

    assert (
        card.more_information_url
        == "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == "https://tracking.example.com/deal/001"
    )

                "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
            ),
        )

                "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
            ),
            get_deal_url=(
                "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
            ),
        )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="at least one highlight",
    ):
        FlashDealCard(
            deal_id="deal-001",
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",

            current_price=999.99,

            savings=199.01,
            relevance_reason="Relevant to you.",
            vendor_name="eBay",
            more_information_url=(
                "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
            ),
            get_deal_url=(
                "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
            ),
        )


def test_flash_deal_card_rejects_invalid_discount():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="discount_percentage must be between 0 and 100",
    ):
        FlashDealCard(
            deal_id="deal-001",
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            character_message="Look what I found!",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            product_image_url=(
                "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
            ),
            tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
            highlights=("Pro camera system",),
            current_price=999.99,
            currency="EUR",
            previous_price=1199.00,
            discount_percentage=120.0,
            savings=199.01,
            relevance_reason="Relevant to you.",
            vendor_name="eBay",
            more_information_url=(
                "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
            ),
            get_deal_url=(
                "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
            ),
        )


def test_flash_deal_card_allows_no_discount():

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.00,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=CommerceLink(
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            destination_url=(
                "https://example.com/backpack"
            ),
        ),
    )

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="I found something for you.",
        relevance_reason="This matches your travel interests.",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/backpack.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Lightweight and practical.",
        highlights=[
            "Lightweight",
            "Water resistant",
        ],
    )

    assert card.has_discount is False
    assert card.has_previous_price is False
    assert card.display_discount is None
    assert card.display_savings is None
    assert card.savings is None

"""
Tests for the FlashDealPresenter.

The presenter must remain a pure presentation layer over
FlashDealCard.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_presenter import (
    FlashDealPresenter,
)


def test_presenter_renders_character():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "## Yeti" in output

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_product():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert (
        "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful."
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_highlights():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### HIGHLIGHTS" in output

    for highlight in card.highlights:
        assert highlight in output


def test_presenter_renders_verified_pricing():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### PRICE" in output

    assert (
        f"{card.currency} "
        f"{card.current_price:.2f}"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        f"{card.currency} "
        f"{card.previous_price:.2f}"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        card.display_discount
        in output
    )

    assert (
        card.display_savings
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_personalisation():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert (
        "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_vendor():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### AVAILABLE FROM" in output

    assert card.vendor_name in output


def test_presenter_renders_commerce_actions():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### ACTIONS" in output

    assert (
        f"[GET DEAL]({card.get_deal_url})"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "[MORE INFORMATION]"
        f"({card.more_information_url})"
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_does_not_mutate_card():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original = {
        "deal_id": card.deal_id,
        "product_name": card.product_name,
        "price": card.current_price,
        "vendor": card.vendor_name,
        "relevance": card.relevance_reason,
        "get_deal_url": card.get_deal_url,
        "more_information_url": (
            card.more_information_url
        ),
    }

    FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert card.deal_id == original["deal_id"]

    assert (
        card.product_name
        == original["product_name"]
    )

    assert (
        card.current_price
        == original["price"]
    )

    assert (
        card.vendor_name
        == original["vendor"]
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == original["relevance"]
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == original["get_deal_url"]
    )

    assert (
        card.more_information_url
        == original["more_information_url"]
    )

"""
Tests for personalised Flash Ideas.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_idea import (
    FlashIdeaBuilder,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


def make_deal() -> DealCard:
    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/backpack"
        ),
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="Minimalist Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=129.0,
        discount_percentage=31.0,
        commerce_link=link,
        source="parallel",
    )


def test_flash_idea_uses_user_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="prefers minimalist travel products",
    )

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert idea.character_id == "yeti"

    assert (
        idea.deal_id
        == make_deal().deal_id
        or idea.deal_id
    )

    assert (
        "minimalist travel products"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "Minimalist Travel Backpack"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "31% off"
        in idea.message
    )


def test_flash_idea_works_without_memory():

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[],
    )

    assert (
        "I found something you might like"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "Minimalist Travel Backpack"
        in idea.message
    )


def test_flash_idea_contains_commerce_url():

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[],
    )

    assert (
        idea.metadata["commerce_url"]
        == "https://example.com/product/backpack"
    )


def test_non_preference_memory_does_not_become_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for travel gear",
    )

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert (
        "I found something you might like"
        in idea.message
    )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Memory contracts.
"""

from datetime import datetime

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


def test_memory_entry_creates_valid_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers lightweight laptops.",
    )

    assert memory.user_id == "user-001"

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
    )

    assert (
        memory.content
        == "Prefers lightweight laptops."
    )

    assert memory.memory_id

    assert isinstance(
        memory.created_at,
        datetime,
    )


def test_memory_entry_supports_discovery_context():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for a laptop under EUR 1000.",
    )

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT
    )


def test_memory_entry_supports_saved_items():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        content="MacBook Air M4",
        metadata={
            "category": "laptop",
        },
    )

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM
    )

    assert (
        memory.metadata["category"]
        == "laptop"
    )


def test_memory_requires_user_id():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        MemoryEntry(
            user_id="",
            memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
            content="Prefers lightweight products.",
        )


def test_memory_requires_content():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="content cannot be empty",
    ):
        MemoryEntry(
            user_id="user-001",
            memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
            content="",
        )


def test_memory_to_dict_is_persistence_safe():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers lightweight laptops.",
    )

    data = memory.to_dict()

    assert data["memory_id"] == memory.memory_id

    assert data["user_id"] == "user-001"

    assert (
        data["memory_type"]
        == "preference"
    )

    assert (
        data["content"]
        == "Prefers lightweight laptops."
    )

    assert isinstance(
        data["created_at"],
        str,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        data["updated_at"],
        str,
    )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Memory Service.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.store import (
    MemoryStore,
)


def test_memory_service_remembers_preference(
    tmp_path,
):

    store = MemoryStore(
        tmp_path / "memory.db"
    )

    service = MemoryService(
        store=store
    )

    memory = service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers minimalist products.",
    )

    assert memory.user_id == "user-001"

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
    )

    assert (
        memory.content
        == "Prefers minimalist products."
    )


def test_memory_service_retrieves_preferences(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Likes minimalist design.",
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for travel gear.",
    )

    preferences = service.preferences(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(preferences) == 1

    assert (
        preferences[0].content
        == "Likes minimalist design."
    )


def test_memory_service_retrieves_discovery_context(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for lightweight travel gear.",
    )

    contexts = service.discovery_context(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(contexts) == 1

    assert (
        contexts[0].content
        == "Looking for lightweight travel gear."
    )


def test_memory_service_retrieves_saved_items(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        content="Minimalist travel backpack",
        metadata={
            "price": "89",
            "currency": "EUR",
        },
    )

    saved_items = service.saved_items(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(saved_items) == 1

    assert (
        saved_items[0].content
        == "Minimalist travel backpack"
    )

    assert (
        saved_items[0].metadata["price"]
        == "89"
    )


def test_memory_service_does_not_mix_users(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Likes hiking products.",
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-002",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Likes photography products.",
    )

    memories = service.list_for_user(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(memories) == 1

    assert (
        memories[0].content
        == "Likes hiking products."
    )


def test_memory_service_deletes_memory(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    memory = service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        content="Travel backpack",
    )

    assert service.delete(
        memory.memory_id
    ) is True

    assert (
        service.get(memory.memory_id)
        is None
    )

FILE: tests\ai_runtime\podcast\test_agent.py

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import MemoryEntry, MemoryType
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.agent import PodcastDiscoveryAgent
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import LearningContext


def context() -> LearningContext:
    return LearningContext(
        world_id="French World",
        target_language="fr",
        learner_level="B2",
        learning_goal="Learn French through current affairs.",
    )


def test_agent_is_provider_agnostic_and_uses_preferences() -> None:
    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers technology podcasts.",
        metadata={
            "topics": "artificial intelligence, European regulation",
        },
    )

    intent = PodcastDiscoveryAgent().build_search_intent(
        user_id="user-001",
        learning_context=context(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert intent.learning_context.target_language == "fr"
    assert intent.learning_context.world_id == "French World"
    assert "artificial intelligence" in intent.topics
    assert "European regulation" in intent.topics
    assert intent.language_required is True


def test_agent_ignores_non_preference_memory() -> None:
    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Discussed artificial intelligence.",
    )

    intent = PodcastDiscoveryAgent().build_search_intent(
        user_id="user-001",
        learning_context=context(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert intent.topics == ("French World",)

FILE: tests\ai_runtime\podcast\test_challenge.py

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.challenge import (
    ComprehensionQuestion,
    PodcastLearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.challenge_service import (
    PodcastLearningChallengeService,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastRecommendation,
    PodcastSearchResult,
)


def make_recommendation() -> PodcastRecommendation:
    result = PodcastSearchResult(
        provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
        external_id="podcast-001",
        title="L'IA en Europe",
        description="AI and European regulation in French.",
        publisher="Demo Publisher",
        languages=("fr",),
        url="https://podcasts.apple.com/demo/ai-europe",
        metadata={"learner_level": "B2"},
    )

    return PodcastRecommendation(
        recommendation_id="recommendation-001",
        user_id="user-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="I found this for you!",
        result=result,
        relevance_reason="It matches your interests.",
        match_score=90,
    )


def test_challenge_is_created_from_recommendation() -> None:
    challenge = PodcastLearningChallengeService().create_challenge(
        recommendation=make_recommendation(),
    )

    assert challenge.user_id == "user-001"
    assert challenge.podcast_id == "podcast-001"
    assert challenge.target_language == "fr"
    assert challenge.learner_level == "B2"
    assert challenge.vocabulary
    assert challenge.comprehension_questions
    assert challenge.discussion_prompt
    assert challenge.metadata["content_basis"] == (
        "verified_podcast_metadata"
    )


def test_challenge_does_not_claim_transcript_analysis() -> None:
    challenge = PodcastLearningChallengeService().create_challenge(
        recommendation=make_recommendation(),
    )

    assert challenge.metadata["content_basis"] == (
        "verified_podcast_metadata"
    )


def test_challenge_evaluation_passes() -> None:
    service = PodcastLearningChallengeService()

    challenge = service.create_challenge(
        recommendation=make_recommendation(),
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        challenge=challenge,
        answers=(0, 0),
    )

    assert result.score == 2
    assert result.total_questions == 2
    assert result.passed is True
    assert result.vocabulary_review_required is False


def test_challenge_evaluation_requires_review() -> None:
    service = PodcastLearningChallengeService()

    challenge = service.create_challenge(
        recommendation=make_recommendation(),
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        challenge=challenge,
        answers=(0, 1),
    )

    assert result.score == 1
    assert result.passed is True
    assert result.vocabulary_review_required is True


def test_challenge_rejects_wrong_answer_count() -> None:
    service = PodcastLearningChallengeService()

    challenge = service.create_challenge(
        recommendation=make_recommendation(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Number of answers must equal",
    ):
        service.evaluate(
            challenge=challenge,
            answers=(0,),
        )


def test_question_validates_correct_option() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="correct_option is out of range",
    ):
        ComprehensionQuestion(
            question="Question",
            options=("A", "B"),
            correct_option=2,
        )


def test_vocabulary_requires_content() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="VocabularyItem meaning cannot be empty",
    ):
        VocabularyItem(
            term="test",
            meaning="",
            example="Example",
        )

FILE: tests\ai_runtime\podcast\test_providers.py
============================================================
import json

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.apple import ApplePodcastProvider
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.spotify import SpotifyPodcastProvider


class FakeResponse:
    def __init__(self, payload: dict):
        self.payload = payload

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        return False

    def read(self):
        return json.dumps(self.payload).encode("utf-8")


def intent() -> PodcastSearchIntent:
    return PodcastSearchIntent(
        user_id="user-001",
        learning_context=LearningContext(
            world_id="French World",
            target_language="fr",
            learner_level="B2",
        ),
        topics=("artificial intelligence",),
    )


def test_apple_normalises_language(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "src.ai_runtime.podcast.apple.urlopen",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: FakeResponse(
            {
                "results": [
                    {
                        "kind": "podcast",
                        "collectionId": 123,
                        "collectionName": "IA en France",
                        "artistName": "Demo Publisher",
                        "language": "French",
                        "collectionViewUrl": (
                            "https://podcasts.apple.com/example"
                        ),
                    }
                ]
            }
        ),
    )

    results = ApplePodcastProvider().search(intent())

    assert len(results) == 1
    assert results[0].provider == PodcastProviderName.APPLE
    assert results[0].languages == ("fr",)


def test_spotify_normalises_episode_language(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "src.ai_runtime.podcast.spotify.urlopen",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: FakeResponse(
            {
                "episodes": {
                    "items": [
                        {
                            "id": "episode-001",
                            "name": "IA en Europe",
                            "description": "European AI regulation",
                            "languages": ["fr"],
                            "duration_ms": 1800000,
                            "release_date": "2026-08-10",
                            "external_urls": {
                                "spotify": (
                                    "https://open.spotify.com/episode/episode-001"
                                )
                            },
                            "images": [],
                        }
                    ]
                }
            }
        ),
    )

    results = SpotifyPodcastProvider(
        access_token="test-token"
    ).search(intent())

    assert len(results) == 1
    assert results[0].provider == PodcastProviderName.SPOTIFY
    assert results[0].languages == ("fr",)
    assert results[0].duration_seconds == 1800


============================================================
FILE: tests\ai_runtime\podcast\test_service.py
============================================================
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.service import PodcastRecommendationService


def intent() -> PodcastSearchIntent:
    return PodcastSearchIntent(
        user_id="user-001",
        learning_context=LearningContext(
            world_id="French World",
            target_language="fr",
            learner_level="B2",
        ),
        topics=("artificial intelligence", "European regulation"),
    )


def candidate(title: str, language: str, description: str) -> PodcastSearchResult:
    return PodcastSearchResult(
        provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
        external_id=title,
        title=title,
        description=description,
        publisher="Demo Publisher",
        languages=(language,),
        url="https://example.com/podcast",
    )


def test_wrong_language_is_rejected() -> None:
    ranked = PodcastRecommendationService.rank_candidates(
        candidates=[
            candidate(
                "AI Europe English",
                "en",
                "artificial intelligence European regulation",
            ),
            candidate(
                "IA Europe Français",
                "fr",
                "artificial intelligence European regulation",
            ),
        ],
        intent=intent(),
    )

    assert len(ranked) == 1
    assert ranked[0][0].title == "IA Europe Français"


def test_preferences_rank_candidates_after_language_filter() -> None:
    ranked = PodcastRecommendationService.rank_candidates(
        candidates=[
            candidate("French Daily", "fr", "general culture"),
            candidate(
                "French AI Europe",
                "fr",
                "artificial intelligence and European regulation",
            ),
        ],
        intent=intent(),
    )

    assert ranked[0][0].title == "French AI Europe"


def test_unknown_language_is_rejected() -> None:
    ranked = PodcastRecommendationService.rank_candidates(
        candidates=[
            candidate(
                "Unknown",
                "unknown",
                "artificial intelligence",
            )
        ],
        intent=intent(),
    )

    assert ranked == []


============================================================
FILE: tests\api\test_chat.py
============================================================
import pytest

from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from backend.api.main import app
from backend.api import routes


class FakeCompanionService:
    """
    Test double for the application service.

    This test deliberately does not invoke Parallel.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
    ):
        self.character = character
        self.received_context = None
        self.received_use_research = None

    async def interact(
        self,
        context,
        use_research=False,
    ):
        self.received_context = context
        self.received_use_research = (
            use_research
        )

        return {
            "character": self.character.name,
            "conversation": {
                "character": self.character.name,
                "message": (
                    f"{self.character.name}: "
                    f"Test response"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
                    if use_research
                    else None
                ),
            },
            "research": (
                {
                    "query": context.request,
                    "test": True,
                }
                if use_research
                else None
            ),
            "quality": {
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
            "memory_count": 0,
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_false(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=False
    to CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "Explain artificial intelligence"
                ),
                "research": False,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is False
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_true(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=True
    to CompanionService.

    This is an API contract test and therefore
    intentionally does not call Parallel.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "What are the latest "
                    "European AI regulation "
                    "developments?"
                ),
                "research": True,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is True
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == (
            "What are the latest "
            "European AI regulation "
            "developments?"
        )
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is not None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        == "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
    )

"""
Tests for Challenge Generator.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceResponse,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)


class FakeContentGenerator:

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="innovation",
                definition="A new idea or approach.",
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question="Quel est votre avis ?"
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generate_learning_challenge():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI Regulation",
        summary="AI regulation is evolving.",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[
            "governance"
        ],
    )

    result = await generator.generate(
        intelligence
    )

    assert result.topic == "AI Regulation"
    assert result.language == "French"
    assert result.vocabulary[0].term == "innovation"
    assert len(result.discussion_questions) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generator_rejects_empty_summary():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI",
        summary="",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[],
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        await generator.generate(
            intelligence
        )

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,

def test_learning_challenge_creation():

    vocabulary = VocabularyItem(
        term="réglementation",
        definition="Rules and legal requirements.",
        example="La réglementation européenne évolue.",
    )

    question = DiscussionQuestion(
        question="Quel est votre avis sur cette réglementation ?"
    )

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Challenge",
        topic="Artificial Intelligence Regulation",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary="A challenge about AI regulation.",
        vocabulary=[vocabulary],
        discussion_questions=[question],
    )

    assert challenge.title == "AI Act Challenge"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == "réglementation"
    assert challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty == "B2"


def test_models_are_immutable():

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title="Test",
        topic="Test topic",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary="Summary",
        vocabulary=[],
        discussion_questions=[],
    )

    try:
        challenge.title = "Changed"
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True

import pytest

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)


def test_conversation_request_is_created():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are a friendly learning companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )

    assert request.context_length == 0


def test_conversation_request_accepts_intelligence_context():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are a friendly learning companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain the latest AI regulation."
        ),
        conversation_context=(
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: Latest AI regulation"
        ),
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert request.context_length > 0

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_empty_instruction():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="system_instruction cannot be empty",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction=" ",
            user_prompt="Explain AI",
        )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_empty_prompt():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_prompt cannot be empty",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction="You are helpful.",
            user_prompt=" ",
        )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_blank_context():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="conversation_context cannot be blank",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction="You are helpful.",
            user_prompt="Explain AI",
            conversation_context=" ",
        )


def test_conversation_request_summary_is_safe():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction="You are helpful.",
        user_prompt="Explain AI.",
        conversation_context="Private context.",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    summary = request.summary()

    assert summary["character_id"] == "yeti"

    assert summary["character_name"] == "Yeti"

    assert summary["has_context"] == "True"

    assert "Private context." not in summary

    assert "Explain AI." not in summary

"""
Tests for AI Evaluation Criteria.

from src.evaluation.criteria import (
    COMPLETENESS,
    DEFAULT_CRITERIA,
    EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
    FACTUALITY,
    LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
    SOURCE_COVERAGE,
)


def test_default_criteria_contains_expected_criteria():

    names = {
        criterion.name
        for criterion in DEFAULT_CRITERIA
    }

    assert names == {
        "factuality",
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_factuality_has_highest_weight():

    assert FACTUALITY.weight == 2.0


def test_educational_value_weight():

    assert EDUCATIONAL_VALUE.weight == 1.5


def test_source_coverage_weight():

    assert SOURCE_COVERAGE.weight == 1.5


def test_all_criteria_have_valid_weights():

    for criterion in DEFAULT_CRITERIA:
        assert criterion.weight > 0


def test_all_criteria_have_descriptions():

    for criterion in DEFAULT_CRITERIA:
        assert criterion.description.strip()


def test_language_level_criterion():

    assert LANGUAGE_LEVEL.name == "language_level"


def test_completeness_criterion():

    assert COMPLETENESS.name == "completeness"

"""
Tests for DeterministicEvaluator.
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.evaluation.deterministic_evaluator import (
    DeterministicEvaluator,
)


def _create_valid_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    return LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Learning Challenge",
        topic="AI Act",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary=(
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework."
        ),
        vocabulary=[
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "Organisation et contrôle "
                    "d'une activité."
                ),
            )
        ],
        discussion_questions=[
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la gouvernance de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                )
            )
        ],
    )


def test_evaluator_returns_expected_criteria():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    names = {
        score.criterion
        for score in scores
    }

    assert names == {
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_valid_challenge_scores_completeness():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "completeness"
    )

    assert score.score == 1.0


def test_valid_challenge_scores_language_level():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "language_level"
    )

    assert score.score == 1.0


def test_valid_challenge_scores_educational_value():

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        _create_valid_challenge()
    )

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "educational_value"
    )

    assert score.score == 1.0


def test_empty_vocabulary_reduces_educational_score():

    challenge = _create_valid_challenge()

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title=challenge.title,
        topic=challenge.topic,
        language=challenge.language,
        learner_level=challenge.learner_level,
        summary=challenge.summary,
        vocabulary=[],
        discussion_questions=challenge.discussion_questions,
    )

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(challenge)

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "educational_value"
    )

    assert score.score == 0.5


def test_invalid_language_level_scores_zero():

    challenge = _create_valid_challenge()

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title=challenge.title,
        topic=challenge.topic,
        language=challenge.language,
        learner_level="INVALID",
        summary=challenge.summary,
        vocabulary=challenge.vocabulary,
        discussion_questions=challenge.discussion_questions,
    )

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(challenge)

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "language_level"
    )

    assert score.score == 0.0


def test_missing_summary_reduces_completeness():

    challenge = _create_valid_challenge()

    challenge = LearningChallenge(
        title=challenge.title,
        topic=challenge.topic,
        language=challenge.language,
        learner_level=challenge.learner_level,
        summary="",
        vocabulary=challenge.vocabulary,
        discussion_questions=challenge.discussion_questions,
    )

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(challenge)

    score = next(
        score
        for score in scores
        if score.criterion == "completeness"
    )

    assert score.score < 1.0

FILE: tests\evaluation\test_engine.py
============================================================
"""
Tests for AI Evaluation Engine.
"""

import pytest

from src.evaluation.engine import EvaluationEngine
from src.evaluation.models import CriterionScore


CRITERIA = [
    {
        "name": "factuality",
        "description": "Factual accuracy.",
        "weight": 2.0,
    },
    {
        "name": "completeness",
        "description": "Completeness.",
        "weight": 1.0,
    },
]


def _create_engine() -> EvaluationEngine:
    from src.evaluation.models import EvaluationCriterion

    return EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            EvaluationCriterion(**criterion)
            for criterion in CRITERIA
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )


def test_engine_calculates_weighted_score():

    engine = _create_engine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        [
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="factuality",
                score=1.0,
                reasoning="Fully supported.",
            ),
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=0.5,
                reasoning="Partially complete.",
            ),
        ]
    )

    # (1.0 * 2 + 0.5 * 1) / 3
    assert result.overall_score == pytest.approx(
        0.8333333333
    )

    assert result.passed is True


def test_engine_can_fail_evaluation():

    engine = _create_engine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        [
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="factuality",
                score=0.5,
                reasoning="Partially supported.",
            ),
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=0.4,
                reasoning="Incomplete.",
            ),
        ]
    )

    assert result.overall_score == pytest.approx(
        0.4666666667
    )

    assert result.passed is False


def test_engine_rejects_empty_criteria():

    from src.evaluation.engine import EvaluationEngine

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        EvaluationEngine(criteria=[])


def test_engine_rejects_invalid_passing_score():

    from src.evaluation.models import EvaluationCriterion

    criterion = EvaluationCriterion(
        name="factuality",
        description="Factual accuracy.",
        weight=1.0,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        EvaluationEngine(
            criteria=[criterion],
            passing_score=1.5,
        )


def test_engine_rejects_unknown_criterion():

    engine = _create_engine()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        engine.evaluate(
            [
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="unknown",
                    score=0.9,
                    reasoning="Invalid.",
                )
            ]
        )


def test_engine_rejects_invalid_score():

    engine = _create_engine()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        engine.evaluate(
            [
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="factuality",
                    score=1.5,
                    reasoning="Invalid score.",
                )
            ]
        )


def test_engine_rejects_duplicate_criteria():

    engine = _create_engine()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        engine.evaluate(
            [
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="factuality",
                    score=0.9,
                    reasoning="First score.",
                ),
                CriterionScore(
                    criterion="factuality",
                    score=0.8,
                    reasoning="Duplicate score.",
                ),
            ]
        )

"""
Tests for AI Evaluation domain models.
"""

from src.evaluation.models import (
    CriterionScore,
    EvaluationCriterion,
    EvaluationResult,
)


def test_evaluation_criterion_creation():

    criterion = EvaluationCriterion(
        name="factuality",
        description="Information is factually supported.",
        weight=2.0,
    )

    assert criterion.name == "factuality"
    assert criterion.weight == 2.0


def test_criterion_score_creation():

    score = CriterionScore(
        criterion="factuality",
        score=0.9,
        reasoning="Claims are supported by sources.",
    )

    assert score.criterion == "factuality"
    assert score.score == 0.9


def test_evaluation_result_creation():

    result = EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.85,
        scores=[
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="factuality",
                score=0.9,
                reasoning="Supported.",
            )
        ],
        passed=True,
    )

    assert result.overall_score == 0.85
    assert result.passed is True
    assert result.evaluator == "deterministic"


def test_evaluation_result_is_immutable():

    result = EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.8,
        scores=[],
        passed=True,
    )

    try:
        result.overall_score = 0.5
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True

FILE: tests\evaluation\test_report.py

"""
Tests for AI Evaluation Quality Report.
"""

from src.evaluation.models import (
    CriterionScore,
    EvaluationResult,
)

from src.evaluation.report import (
    EvaluationReport,
)


def _create_result() -> EvaluationResult:
    return EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.85,
        scores=[
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=1.0,
                reasoning="All required components are present.",
            ),
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="language_level",
                score=0.7,
                reasoning="CEFR level is valid.",
            ),
        ],
        passed=True,
    )


def test_report_contains_overall_score():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "Overall Score: 0.85" in output


def test_report_contains_pass_status():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "Status: PASS" in output


def test_report_contains_evaluator():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "Evaluator: deterministic" in output


def test_report_contains_criteria():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert "completeness: 1.00" in output
    assert "language_level: 0.70" in output


def test_report_contains_reasoning():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    output = report.generate(
        _create_result()
    )

    assert (
        "All required components are present."
        in output
    )


def test_failed_report_shows_fail():

    report = EvaluationReport()

    result = EvaluationResult(
        overall_score=0.45,
        scores=[
            CriterionScore(
                criterion="completeness",
                score=0.45,
                reasoning="Several components are missing.",
            )
        ],
        passed=False,
    )

    output = report.generate(result)

    assert "Overall Score: 0.45" in output
    assert "Status: FAIL" in output

"""
Tests for AI Governance Policy Engine.
"""

from src.governance.engine import (
    GovernancePolicyEngine,
)

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    RiskLevel,
)


def test_low_risk_capability_is_allowed():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="content_formatting",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert result.risk_level == RiskLevel.LOW


def test_external_research_can_be_allowed():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="live_web_research",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            allows_external_calls=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW

    assert any(
        "External calls" in reason
        for reason in result.reasons
    )


def test_human_review_policy_requires_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="high_impact_decision",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            requires_human_review=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.REVIEW


def test_high_risk_capability_requires_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="high_risk_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.REVIEW


def test_critical_risk_is_blocked():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="critical_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK


def test_side_effects_are_blocked():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="external_modification",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            allows_side_effects=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK

    assert (
        "Uncontrolled side effects are prohibited."
        in result.reasons
    )


def test_side_effect_block_takes_precedence_over_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="dangerous_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
            requires_human_review=True,
            allows_side_effects=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK


def test_critical_risk_takes_precedence_over_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="critical_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
            requires_human_review=True,
        )
    )

    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK

"""
Tests for AI Governance domain models.
"""

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    GovernanceResult,
    RiskLevel,
)


def test_governance_policy_creation():

    policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_web_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )

    assert policy.capability == "live_web_research"
    assert policy.risk_level == RiskLevel.MEDIUM
    assert policy.allows_external_calls is True
    assert policy.allows_side_effects is False


def test_governance_result_creation():

    result = GovernanceResult(
        capability="live_web_research",
        decision=GovernanceDecision.ALLOW,
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        reasons=("External research is permitted.",),
    )

    assert result.capability == "live_web_research"
    assert result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert result.risk_level == RiskLevel.MEDIUM
    assert len(result.reasons) == 1


def test_risk_levels_are_defined():

    assert RiskLevel.LOW.value == "low"
    assert RiskLevel.MEDIUM.value == "medium"
    assert RiskLevel.HIGH.value == "high"
    assert RiskLevel.CRITICAL.value == "critical"


def test_governance_decisions_are_defined():

    assert GovernanceDecision.ALLOW.value == "allow"
    assert GovernanceDecision.REVIEW.value == "review"
    assert GovernanceDecision.BLOCK.value == "block"


def test_governance_policy_is_immutable():

    policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="test",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
    )

    try:
        policy.capability = "changed"
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True


def test_governance_result_is_immutable():

    result = GovernanceResult(
        capability="test",
        decision=GovernanceDecision.ALLOW,
        risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
        reasons=(),
    )

    try:
        result.decision = GovernanceDecision.BLOCK
        assert False
    except AttributeError:
        assert True

============================================================
FILE: tests\integration\test_api_provider_wiring.py

"""
API provider wiring integration tests.

Verifies that the API composition layer creates an
LLMService through the LLMProviderFactory and injects
that service into ConversationAgent.

The real OpenAI API is never called.
"""

import pytest

from backend.api import routes
from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)
from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)
from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


class FakeProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider used by the integration test.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.received_request = request

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Fake AI response",
        )


class FakeConversationAgent:
    """
    Test double for ConversationAgent.

    Captures the injected LLMService so the API
    composition boundary can be verified.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
        llm_service=None,
    ):
        self.character = character
        self.llm_service = llm_service


def create_character():
    """
    Create deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def test_create_companion_service_injects_llm_service(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify API composition creates an LLMService
    through the provider factory and injects it
    into ConversationAgent.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory_create(
        provider="openai",
    ):
        fake_provider = FakeProvider()

        captured["provider"] = fake_provider
        captured["provider_name"] = provider

        return fake_provider

    def fake_conversation_agent(
        character,
        llm_service=None,
    ):
        agent = FakeConversationAgent(
            character=character,
            llm_service=llm_service,
        )

        captured["character"] = character
        captured["agent"] = agent
        captured["llm_service"] = llm_service

        return agent

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes.LLMProviderFactory,
        "create",
        staticmethod(
            fake_factory_create
        ),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "ConversationAgent",
        fake_conversation_agent,
    )

    character = (
        routes.character_registry.get(
            "yeti"
        )
    )

    service = (
        routes.create_companion_service(
            character
        )
    )

    assert service is not None

    assert (
        captured["character"]
        == character
    )

    assert (
        captured["llm_service"]
        is not None
    )

    assert isinstance(
        captured["llm_service"],
        LLMService,
    )

    assert (
        captured["provider"]
        is captured[
            "llm_service"
        ].llm_provider
    )

    assert (
        captured["provider_name"]
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        captured["agent"].llm_service
        is captured["llm_service"]
    )

============================================================
FILE: tests\integration\test_conversation_agent_provider_integration.py
============================================================
"""
API provider wiring integration test.

Verifies that the API composition layer injects
the LLM provider into ConversationAgent.

The OpenAI API call is replaced with a fake provider.
"""

import pytest

from backend.api import routes
from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Fake provider replacing OpenAI.

    Ensures API composition injects an LLM provider
    without calling external services.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.received_request = request

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output=(
                "Fake AI response"
            ),
        )


def create_character():

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_receives_provider():

    provider = FakeLLMProvider()

    from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
        ConversationAgent,
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="integration-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert (
        result.output["message"]
        == "Fake AI response"
    )

    assert (
        result.output["provider_id"]
        == "fake_llm"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.user_prompt
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

"""
Integration test for the character-driven discovery notification.

Flow:

Memory
    ↓
DiscoveryService
    ↓
FlashDealCard
    ↓
DiscoveryNotification
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
Vendor handoff
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_memory,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.notification import (
    DiscoveryNotification,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)


def build_flash_deal():
    """
    Build the same application-level FlashDealCard used
    by the hackathon discovery flow.
    """

    deal = build_demo_deal()
    memory = build_demo_memory()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    return memory, card


def test_notification_is_created_from_real_discovery_result():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-001",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    assert notification.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert notification.character_id == (
        card.character_id
    )

    assert notification.character_name == (
        card.character_name
    )

    assert notification.deal is card

    assert notification.product_name == (
        card.product_name
    )


def test_notification_preserves_personalisation():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-002",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason == (
        card.relevance_reason
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason


def test_notification_render_is_character_driven():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-003",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    rendered = notification.render()

    assert "Yeti:" in rendered

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in rendered
    )

    assert card.product_name in rendered

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        in rendered
    )


def test_notification_does_not_change_commerce_flow():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-004",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=notification.user_id,
        card=notification.deal,
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )


def test_notification_does_not_create_a_shop():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-005",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    # The notification exposes one discovery event,
    # not a catalogue, cart, or shopping dashboard.

    assert notification.deal is card

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "products",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "cart",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "checkout",
    )

FILE: tests\integration\test_flash_deal_commerce_flow.py

"""
Integration test for the complete Flash Deal commerce flow.

Flow:

Memory / personalisation
        ↓
DealCard
        ↓
FlashDealCard
        ↓
CommerceAction
        ↓
CommerceService
        ↓
External vendor handoff

No payment is executed by the test.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
)


def test_complete_flash_deal_to_commerce_flow():
    """
    Validate the complete hackathon commerce boundary.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    commerce = FlashDealCommerce()

    action = commerce.create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id


def test_commerce_flow_does_not_change_discovery_data():
    """
    Commerce must not mutate the discovered Flash Deal.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original_product = card.product_name
    original_price = card.current_price
    original_vendor = card.vendor_name
    original_deal_id = card.deal_id

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert card.product_name == original_product
    assert card.current_price == original_price
    assert card.vendor_name == original_vendor
    assert card.deal_id == original_deal_id

FILE: tests\integration\test_hackathon_complete_vertical_slice.py
============================================================
"""
End-to-end hackathon vertical-slice test.

Validates the working EasyChatBot journey:

Governance
    ↓
Live Intelligence
    ↓
Learning Challenge
    ↓
Quality Evaluation
    ↓
Memory
    ↓
DiscoveryService
    ↓
FlashDealCard
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
Vendor handoff

No real purchase or external payment is executed.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_memory,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)


def test_complete_hackathon_vertical_slice():

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # MEMORY
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    assert memory.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert memory.content

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVERY INPUT
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    deal = build_demo_deal()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVERY SERVICE
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id

    assert card.character_id == "yeti"

    assert card.product_name == deal.product_name

    assert card.vendor_name == deal.vendor

    assert card.relevance_reason

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # COMMERCE ACTION
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # COMMERCE SERVICE
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # SAFETY / SCOPE BOUNDARY
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        result.message
        == (
            "Continue to the approved vendor "
            "to complete the purchase."
        )
    )
