FILE: tests\integration\test_hackathon_demo_final.py
============================================================
"""
Final end-to-end acceptance test for the EasyChatBot hackathon demo.

This test executes the real hackathon demonstration and verifies
the complete product story:

Governance
    ↓
Live Intelligence
    ↓
Learning
    ↓
Evaluation
    ↓
Personalised Discovery
    ↓
Character Notification
    ↓
Commerce Boundary
    ↓
Vendor Handoff

No real external purchase is performed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real hackathon demo."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )


def test_complete_hackathon_demo_succeeds():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demonstration failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Governance
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 1 — GOVERNANCE" in output
    assert "Decision: ALLOW" in output
    assert "Governance check: PASSED" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Live Intelligence
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 2 — LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output
    assert "Topic: AI Act" in output
    assert "Language: French" in output
    assert "Learner level: B2" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Learning
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "Summary:" in output
    assert "Vocabulary:" in output
    assert "Discussion questions:" in output

    assert "gouvernance" in output
    assert "réglementation" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Evaluation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 3 — AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output
    assert "Status: PASS" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Personalised Discovery
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
        in output
    )

    assert "## Yeti" in output
    assert "### NEW FIND!" in output
    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful." in output

    assert "### HIGHLIGHTS" in output
    assert "Pro camera system" in output
    assert "High-performance chip" in output

    assert "### PRICE" in output
    assert "Current price:" in output
    assert "Discount:" in output
    assert "Savings:" in output

    assert (
        "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        in output
    )

    assert "### AVAILABLE FROM" in output
    assert "Demo Vendor" in output

    assert "### ACTIONS" in output
    assert "[GET DEAL]" in output
    assert "[MORE INFORMATION]" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Character notification
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION" in output
    assert "Yeti:" in output
    assert "Look what I found!" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Commerce boundary
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION" in output
    assert "Commerce action: INITIATE_PURCHASE" in output
    assert "Status: REDIRECT" in output
    assert "Approved commerce boundary" in output
    assert "Vendor handoff" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Payment boundary
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final success
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS" in output

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
        "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        in output
    )


def test_demo_preserves_correct_execution_order():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "STEP 1 — GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "STEP 2 — LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "STEP 3 — AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    notification = output.index(
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < notification
    assert notification < commerce
    assert commerce < success

FILE: tests\integration\test_hackathon_demo_reliability.py
============================================================
"""
Reliability tests for the unified EasyChatBot hackathon demo.

H4.3 — Demo Reliability

These tests verify that the hackathon demonstration:

- starts successfully
- completes within a reasonable time
- does not require external API credentials
- produces the expected final success marker

The test intentionally executes the demo as a separate process,
matching how the hackathon presentation will actually run it.

It does not modify or bypass H1, H2, or H3 validation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def _run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """
    Execute the unified demo with a bounded runtime.

    A timeout protects CI and the hackathon environment from
    a demo process hanging indefinitely.
    """

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
        timeout=30,
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_completes_within_timeout():
    """
    Verify that the demo completes within 30 seconds.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demo did not complete successfully.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_is_self_contained():
    """
    Verify that the deterministic hackathon demonstration
    reaches its expected final state.

    The demo uses controlled providers and therefore should
    not require Parallel, LLM, or other external credentials.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    assert (
        "Governance check: PASSED"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Overall Score: 1.00"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Status: PASS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in result.stdout
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_script_exists():

    assert DEMO_PATH.exists()
    assert DEMO_PATH.is_file()

FILE: tests\integration\test_hackathon_discovery_commerce_flow.py
============================================================
"""
End-to-end integration test for the hackathon discovery journey.

Flow:

User Memory
    ↓
Deal Discovery
    ↓
Personalisation
    ↓
Character FlashDealCard
    ↓
GET DEAL
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
Vendor handoff

The test validates the complete hackathon vertical slice
without executing a real purchase or external payment.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
    build_demo_memory,
)


def test_hackathon_discovery_to_commerce_flow():
    """
    Validate the complete personalised discovery journey.
    """

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. USER MEMORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    assert memory.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert memory.content

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. DEAL DISCOVERY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    deal = build_demo_deal()

    assert deal.deal_id

    assert deal.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"

    assert deal.vendor == "Demo Vendor"

    assert deal.price == 999.99

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card(
        deal=deal,
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id

    assert card.character_id == "yeti"

    assert card.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert card.product_name == deal.product_name

    assert card.relevance_reason

    assert (
        "mobile photography"
        in card.relevance_reason
    )

    assert card.has_discount is True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. COMMERCE ACTION
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. CONTROLLED COMMERCE SERVICE
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. FINAL SAFETY BOUNDARY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    # The hackathon demonstrates a vendor handoff.
    # It does not claim that a payment occurred.

    assert (
        result.message
        == (
            "Continue to the approved vendor "
            "to complete the purchase."
        )
    )


def test_hackathon_flow_preserves_layer_separation():
    """
    Verify that commerce consumes the discovery result rather
    than changing it.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original = {
        "deal_id": card.deal_id,
        "product_name": card.product_name,
        "price": card.current_price,
        "vendor": card.vendor_name,
        "relevance": card.relevance_reason,
        "get_deal_url": card.get_deal_url,
    }

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert card.deal_id == original["deal_id"]

    assert (
        card.product_name
        == original["product_name"]
    )

    assert (
        card.current_price
        == original["price"]
    )

    assert (
        card.vendor_name
        == original["vendor"]
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == original["relevance"]
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == original["get_deal_url"]
    )

"""
Integration tests for the Flash Deal presentation in the
unified hackathon demonstration.

The test verifies that the real executable demo uses the
FlashDealPresenter and still preserves the discovery,
notification, and commerce journeys.
"""

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real hackathon demonstration."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )


def test_demo_renders_flash_deal_presentation():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demo failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    assert (
        "STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
        in output
    )

    assert "## Yeti" in output

    assert (
        "### NEW FIND!"
        in output
    )

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert (
        "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful."
        in output
    )

    assert "### HIGHLIGHTS" in output

    assert "Pro camera system" in output

    assert (
        "### PRICE"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Current price:"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Discount:"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Savings:"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "### AVAILABLE FROM"
        in output
    )

    assert "Demo Vendor" in output

    assert "### ACTIONS" in output

    assert "[GET DEAL]" in output

    assert "[MORE INFORMATION]" in output


def test_demo_keeps_character_notification_after_card():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    card_position = output.index(
        "### NEW FIND!"
    )

    notification_position = output.index(
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
    )

    assert notification_position > card_position

    assert "Yeti:" in output

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in output
    )


def test_demo_keeps_commerce_boundary_after_discovery():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    notification_position = output.index(
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
    )

    commerce_position = output.index(
        "STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION"
    )

    assert commerce_position > notification_position

    assert (
        "Commerce action: INITIATE_PURCHASE"
        in output
    )

    assert "Status: REDIRECT" in output

    assert (
        "Approved commerce boundary"
        in output
    )

    assert "Vendor handoff" in output

    assert (
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )


def test_demo_keeps_original_hackathon_readiness_marker():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
        "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in output
    )

"""
Acceptance tests for the Windows-safe judge-facing EasyChatBot demo.

The judge demo now includes the Payment Pass contract. The commerce
assertions therefore validate the current presentation contract:

GET DEAL
    ->
Payment Pass
    ->
READY / DEMO
    ->
No payment executed
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_judge_demo.py"
)


def run_judge_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real judge-facing demonstration."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )


def test_judge_demo_succeeds():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Judge demo failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    assert (
        "EasyChatBot - JUDGE DEMONSTRATION"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Discover -> Understand -> Personalise -> "
        "Recommend -> Act"
        in output
    )

    assert "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS" in output


def test_judge_demo_contains_all_capabilities():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "1 - GOVERNANCE" in output
    assert "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output
    assert "3 - LEARNING" in output
    assert "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output
    assert "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY" in output
    assert "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY" in output
    assert "7 - FLASH DEAL" in output
    assert "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY" in output


def test_judge_demo_shows_personalised_discovery():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "Yeti" in output
    assert "Look what I found!" in output
    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful." in output
    assert "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU" in output
    assert "Demo Vendor" in output


def test_judge_demo_shows_commerce_boundary():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "GET DEAL -> vendor handoff" in output

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output

    assert "Payment status: READY" in output

    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]" in output

    assert "No payment has been executed." in output

    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    assert "[PASS] Payment execution: NO" in output

    assert "[PASS] Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "EasyChatBot is not a shop." in output


def test_judge_demo_preserves_execution_order():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "1 - GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    learning = output.index(
        "3 - LEARNING"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    character = output.index(
        "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY"
    )

    deal = output.index(
        "7 - FLASH DEAL"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY"
    )

    payment = output.index(
        "PAYMENT PASS"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < learning
    assert learning < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < character
    assert character < deal
    assert deal < commerce
    assert commerce < payment
    assert payment < success

FILE: tests\integration\test_hackathon_payment_pass_demo.py
============================================================
"""
Integration acceptance tests for the hackathon Payment Pass.

The test verifies the complete safe commerce path:

FlashDealCard
    ↓
FlashDealCommerce
    ↓
PaymentPass
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
READY / DEMO
    ↓
Vendor checkout

No real payment is executed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentExecutionMode,
    PaymentPassStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


def make_card() -> FlashDealCard:
    """Create deterministic hackathon deal data."""

    return FlashDealCard(
        deal_id="deal-payment-demo-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "Look what I found! "
            "This could be perfect for you."
        ),
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
        current_price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        savings=199.01,
        relevance_reason=(
            "Matches the user's preference for "
            "powerful mobile photography devices."
        ),
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        more_information_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
        get_deal_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_complete_payment_pass_flow():

    card = make_card()

    commerce = FlashDealCommerce()

    payment_pass = commerce.create_payment_pass(
        user_id="user-001",
        card=card,
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert prepared is payment_pass

    assert prepared.deal_id == card.deal_id
    assert prepared.user_id == "user-001"

    assert (
        prepared.product_name
        == card.product_name
    )

    assert (
        prepared.vendor_name
        == card.vendor_name
    )

    assert (
        prepared.amount
        == card.current_price
    )

    assert (
        prepared.currency
        == card.currency
    )

    assert (
        prepared.checkout_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert (
        prepared.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        prepared.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )

    assert prepared.payment_executed is False


def test_payment_pass_cannot_become_executed():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False

    assert (
        payment_pass.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )


def test_payment_pass_preserves_vendor_checkout():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert (
        prepared.checkout_url
        == "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
    )


def test_payment_pass_demo_render_is_explicit():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    output = prepared.render_demo()

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output
    assert "Payment status: READY" in output
    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output
    assert "No payment has been executed." in output
    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

"""
Final hackathon readiness checks for EasyChatBot.

H4.4 — Hackathon Readiness

This test verifies that the core hackathon deliverables exist
and that the repository contains the executable demonstrations
for the completed workstreams.

It intentionally does not implement new business logic.

Validated capabilities:

H1 — Live Intelligence
H2 — AI Quality Evaluation
H3 — AI Safety & Governance
H4 — Unified Hackathon Demonstration
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


REQUIRED_FILES = (
    # H1 — Live Intelligence
    "src/ai_agents/live_intelligence_agent/handler.py",
    "src/providers/search_provider/interface.py",
    "src/providers/search_provider/parallel_adapter.py",
    "backend/providers/parallel/parallel_client.py",

    # H2 — AI Quality Evaluation
    "src/evaluation/models.py",
    "src/evaluation/criteria.py",
    "src/evaluation/engine.py",
    "src/evaluation/deterministic_evaluator.py",
    "src/evaluation/report.py",

    # H3 — Governance
    "src/governance/models.py",
    "src/governance/engine.py",

    # H4 — Demonstration
    "examples/live_challenge_demo.py",
    "examples/evaluation_demo.py",
    "examples/governance_demo.py",
    "examples/hackathon_demo.py",

    "tests/integration/test_live_challenge_flow.py",
    "tests/integration/test_parallel_search_provider.py",
    "tests/integration/test_live_challenge_evaluation.py",
    "tests/integration/test_live_intelligence_governance.py",
    "tests/integration/test_hackathon_demo_flow.py",
    "tests/integration/test_hackathon_demo_reliability.py",
)


def test_required_hackathon_files_exist():
    """
    Verify that all required hackathon deliverables exist.
    """

    missing_files = [
        path
        for path in REQUIRED_FILES
        if not (
            REPOSITORY_ROOT / path
        ).is_file()
    ]

    assert not missing_files, (
        "Hackathon readiness check failed. "
        "Missing required files:\n"
        + "\n".join(
            f" - {path}"
            for path in missing_files
        )
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_exists():

    demo_path = (
        REPOSITORY_ROOT
        / "examples"
        / "hackathon_demo.py"
    )

    assert demo_path.exists()
    assert demo_path.is_file()


def test_hackathon_demo_is_not_empty():

    demo_path = (
        REPOSITORY_ROOT
        / "examples"
        / "hackathon_demo.py"
    )

    content = demo_path.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    assert content.strip()

    required_directories = (
        "src/evaluation",

        "tests/evaluation",

    assert not missing_directories, (
        "Missing required hackathon directories:\n"
        + "\n".join(
            f" - {path}"
            for path in missing_directories
        )
    )


def test_hackathon_readiness_marker():

    readiness_statement = (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> "

    demo_path = (

        / "examples"

============================================================
FILE: tests\integration\test_live_challenge_evaluation.py
============================================================
"""
Integration test for H2 evaluation of the H1 learning challenge.

Flow:

H1 Live Intelligence / Challenge Engine
        ↓
LearningChallenge
        ↓
H2 DeterministicEvaluator
        ↓
CriterionScore[]
        ↓
EvaluationEngine
        ↓
EvaluationResult

This test intentionally uses deterministic test doubles.

It does not:
- call the Parallel API
- call an LLM
- modify H1 components
- use the Conversation Agent
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.evaluation.criteria import (
    COMPLETENESS,
    EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
    LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
    SOURCE_COVERAGE,
)

from src.evaluation.deterministic_evaluator import (
    DeterministicEvaluator,
)

from src.evaluation.engine import (
    EvaluationEngine,
)


def _create_h1_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Create a representative H1 learning challenge.

    This mirrors the structure produced by the H1
    Challenge Engine without calling external services.
    """

    return LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Learning Challenge",
        topic="AI Act",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary=(
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework for artificial "
            "intelligence."
        ),
        vocabulary=[
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "La manière dont une organisation "
                    "est dirigée et contrôlée."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La gouvernance de l'IA devient "
                    "essentielle."
                ),
            ),
            VocabularyItem(
                term="réglementation",
                definition=(
                    "Ensemble des règles qui encadrent "
                    "une activité."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La réglementation européenne évolue."
                ),
            ),
        ],
        discussion_questions=[
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la réglementation de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Quels sont les avantages d'une "
                    "réglementation européenne de l'IA ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
        ],
    )


def test_h1_learning_challenge_can_be_evaluated():
    """
    Validate the complete H1 → H2 evaluation boundary.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    assert len(scores) == 4

    score_names = {
        score.criterion
        for score in scores
    }

    assert score_names == {
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_h1_learning_challenge_produces_quality_result():
    """
    Evaluate the H1 challenge through the H2 scoring engine.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        scores
    )

    assert result.overall_score == 1.0

    assert result.passed is True

    assert len(result.scores) == 4

    assert result.evaluator == "deterministic"


def test_h1_learning_challenge_contains_high_quality_components():
    """
    Verify the H1 challenge satisfies the basic quality
    dimensions currently supported by deterministic evaluation.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    for score in scores:
        assert 0.0 <= score.score <= 1.0
        assert score.reasoning.strip()

    assert all(
        score.score == 1.0
        for score in scores
    )

"""
Integration test for Live Challenge vertical slice.

Flow tested:

Search Provider
        ↓
Live Intelligence Agent
        ↓
Challenge Generator
        ↓
Learning Challenge

This test validates the Sprint H1 integration boundaries.

Important:
- Uses test doubles only.
- Does not call the real Parallel API.
- Does not modify or depend on the Conversation Agent.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)


class FakeSearchProvider:
    """
    Controlled search provider for integration testing.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        return [
            SearchResult(
                title="AI Act Update",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                content="European AI regulation update.",
                source="test-provider",
            )
        ]


class FakeIntelligenceProcessor:
    """
    Simulates intelligence synthesis without
    requiring an LLM provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
        return (
            "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
            "artificial intelligence.",
            [
                "AI governance",
                "risk management",
            ],
        )


class FakeChallengeContentGenerator:
    """
    Simulates educational content generation.
    """

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[VocabularyItem]:
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "Organisation and control of activities."
                ),
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[DiscussionQuestion]:
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA "
                    "est-elle importante ?"
                ),
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_challenge_vertical_slice():
    """
    Validate the complete Sprint H1 backend flow:

    Search Provider
        ↓
    Live Intelligence Agent
        ↓
    Challenge Generator
        ↓
    Learning Challenge
    """

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 1 — Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 2 — Execute Live Intelligence research
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        request=LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 3 — Validate intelligence output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert intelligence.topic == "AI Act"

    assert intelligence.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert intelligence.key_information == [
        "AI governance",
        "risk management",
    ]

    assert len(intelligence.sources) == 1
    assert intelligence.sources[0].title == "AI Act Update"
    assert intelligence.sources[0].source == "test-provider"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 4 — Create Challenge Generator
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 5 — Generate learning challenge
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 6 — Validate final learning output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert challenge.title == (
        "AI Act Learning Challenge"
    )

    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.learner_level == "B2"

    assert challenge.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert len(challenge.vocabulary) == 1

    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == (
        "gouvernance"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.vocabulary[0].definition
        == "Organisation and control of activities."
    )

    assert len(challenge.discussion_questions) == 1

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].question
        == "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA est-elle importante ?"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty
        == "B2"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.created_by_agent
        == "live_intelligence_agent"
    )

============================================================
FILE: tests\integration\test_live_intelligence_governance.py
============================================================
"""
Integration test for H3 governance of the H1 Live Intelligence capability.

Flow:

H1 Live Intelligence Capability
        |
        v
Governance Policy
        |
        v
Governance Policy Engine
        |
        v
Governance Decision

This test validates the governance boundary only.

It does not:
- execute the Conversation Agent
- execute the Parallel API
- modify H1 implementation
- modify H2 evaluation
- perform external side effects
"""

from src.governance.engine import (
    GovernancePolicyEngine,
)

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    RiskLevel,
)


def create_live_intelligence_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Define the governance policy for H1 Live Intelligence.

    Live research:
    - may access external information
    - must not create external side effects
    - is considered medium risk
    - does not require human approval for passive research
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


def test_live_intelligence_capability_is_governed():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    policy = create_live_intelligence_policy()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        policy
    )

    assert (
        result.capability
        == "live_intelligence_research"
    )

    assert (
        result.risk_level
        == RiskLevel.MEDIUM
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    )


def test_live_intelligence_allows_external_research():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        create_live_intelligence_policy()
    )

    assert any(
        "External calls are explicitly permitted."
        in reason
        for reason in result.reasons
    )


def test_live_intelligence_does_not_allow_side_effects():

    policy = create_live_intelligence_policy()

    assert policy.allows_side_effects is False


def test_live_intelligence_with_side_effects_is_blocked():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    unsafe_policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=True,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        unsafe_policy
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK
    )


def test_live_intelligence_high_risk_requires_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    high_risk_policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        high_risk_policy
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.REVIEW
    )

FILE: tests\integration\test_parallel_search_provider.py
============================================================
"""
Integration test for the real Parallel Search Provider.

Sprint H1.8 — Real Provider Validation

This test validates:

Application
    ↓
SearchProvider interface
    ↓
ParallelSearchProvider
    ↓
Parallel API
    ↓
Normalised SearchResult

The test is intentionally isolated from:
- Conversation Agent
- Challenge Engine
- Live Intelligence Agent

The test is skipped when PARALLEL_API_KEY is not configured.
"""

import os

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)
from src.providers.search_provider.parallel_adapter import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_search_provider_returns_normalised_results():
    """
    Validate that the real Parallel provider returns
    normalised SearchResult objects.
    """

    api_key = os.getenv("PARALLEL_API_KEY")

    if not api_key:
        pytest.skip(
            "PARALLEL_API_KEY is not configured"
        )

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
        api_key=api_key,
    )

    results = await provider.search(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        max_results=3,
    )

    assert isinstance(results, list)

    assert len(results) > 0

    for result in results:
        assert isinstance(result, SearchResult)

        assert result.title.strip()
        assert result.url.strip()
        assert result.source.strip()

        # Content may legitimately be empty depending
        # on the provider response, so it is not required
        # for the provider contract test.

FILE: tests\integration\test_personalised_discovery_journey.py
============================================================
"""
Acceptance test for the complete personalised discovery journey.

The test verifies the application-level journey:

Memory
    ↓
DiscoveryService
    ↓
FlashDealCard
    ↓
DiscoveryNotification
    ↓
CommerceAction
    ↓
CommerceService
    ↓
Vendor handoff

No real payment is executed.
"""

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def test_personalised_discovery_journey_is_demonstrated():

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Personalised discovery journey failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Discovery
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "STEP 4 — PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
        in output
    )

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert "Demo Vendor" in output

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Character notification
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
        in output
    )

    assert "Yeti:" in output

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Why this may interest you:"
        in output
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Commerce
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "STEP 5 — COMMERCE ACTION"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Commerce action: INITIATE_PURCHASE"
        in output
    )

    assert "Status: REDIRECT" in output

    assert (
        "Approved commerce boundary"
        in output
    )

    assert "Vendor handoff" in output

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Safety boundary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final result
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in output
    )


def test_original_hackathon_capability_flow_remains_intact():

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "STEP 1 — GOVERNANCE" in output

    assert "STEP 2 — LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output

    assert "STEP 3 — AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output

    # Preserve the original hackathon readiness contract.
    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in output
    )

import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)


def test_intelligence_context_creation():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="European Commission",
        url="https://example.com",
        source="European Commission",
        content="AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Current regulatory information.",
    )

    assert context.query == (
        "latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert context.source_count == 1

    assert context.has_sources is True

    assert context.has_summary is True


def test_intelligence_context_is_immutable():
    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest AI news",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.query = "changed"


def test_intelligence_context_rejects_empty_query():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Intelligence query cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceContext(
            query="",
        )


def test_intelligence_source_requires_title():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="title cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceSource(
            title="",
            url="https://example.com",
            source="example",
        )


def test_intelligence_context_to_context_text():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="AI Regulation",
        url="https://example.com/ai",
        source="Example",
        content="European AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Regulatory developments.",
    )

    text = context.to_context_text()

    assert "LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in text

    assert (
        "latest European AI regulation"
        in text
    )

    assert "AI Regulation" in text

    assert "https://example.com/ai" in text

    assert "European AI regulation information." in text

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.intelligence.research_service import (
    ResearchService,
)


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
            ),
        )


class FailingResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
            error="Research unavailable",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_returns_intelligence_context():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    result = await service.research(
        user_id="demo-user",
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_rejects_empty_query():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Research query cannot be empty",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="",
        )

async def test_research_service_propagates_failure():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FailingResearchAgent()

    with pytest.raises(
        RuntimeError,
        match="Research unavailable",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="Latest European AI regulation",

"""
Tests for Search Provider Interface.

Validates:
- SearchResult data model behaviour
- SearchProvider abstract contract
"""

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


def test_search_result_creation():
    """
    SearchResult should store normalised provider output.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="AI Act Update",
        url="https://example.com/article",
        content="Latest AI regulation news.",
        source="example",
    )

    assert result.title == "AI Act Update"
    assert result.url == "https://example.com/article"
    assert result.content == "Latest AI regulation news."
    assert result.source == "example"


def test_search_result_is_immutable():
    """
    SearchResult is frozen and should not allow mutation.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="Test",
        url="https://example.com",
        content="Content",
        source="test",
    )

    with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
        result.title = "Changed"


def test_search_provider_cannot_be_instantiated():
    """
    SearchProvider is an abstract interface.
    """

    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        SearchProvider()

FILE: tests\providers\test_openai_provider.py

"""
Tests for OpenAIProvider.

These tests never call the real OpenAI API.

The OpenAI AsyncOpenAI client is injected with a fake client
so provider behaviour can be tested deterministically.
"""

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.llm.openai_provider import (
    OpenAIProvider,
)


class FakeResponses:
    """Fake OpenAI Responses API."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        response=None,
        error=None,
    ):
        self.response = response
        self.error = error
        self.received_kwargs = None

    async def create(
        self,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        self.received_kwargs = kwargs

        if self.error is not None:
            raise self.error

        return self.response


class FakeOpenAIClient:
    """Fake AsyncOpenAI client."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        response=None,
        error=None,
    ):
        self.responses = FakeResponses(
            response=response,
            error=error,
        )


def create_request(
    *,
    context=None,
):
    """Create a valid conversation request."""

    return ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are Yeti, a friendly AI companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
        conversation_context=context,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )


def test_openai_provider_initialises():
    """Provider should initialise with a valid model."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    assert provider.provider_id == "openai"
    assert provider.model == "gpt-5-mini"


def test_openai_provider_rejects_empty_model():
    """Provider should reject an empty model identifier."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="OpenAI model cannot be empty",
    ):
        OpenAIProvider(
            model="",
            client=client,
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_generates_response():
    """Successful OpenAI response should become ProviderResult."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Artificial intelligence is the field of AI.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is True
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert (
        result.output
        == "Artificial intelligence is the field of AI."
    )
    assert result.error is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_builds_correct_request():
    """Provider should correctly translate ConversationRequest."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Test response.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    request = create_request(
        context=(
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: AI regulation"
        ),
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        request,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    sent = (
        client.responses.received_kwargs
    )

    assert sent is not None

    assert sent["model"] == "gpt-5-mini"

    messages = sent["input"]

    assert messages[0] == {
        "role": "system",
        "content": (
            "You are Yeti, a friendly AI companion."
        ),
    }

    assert messages[1] == {
        "role": "user",
        "content": (
            "Additional context:\n"
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: AI regulation"
        ),
    }

    assert messages[2] == {
        "role": "user",
        "content": (
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
    }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_without_context():
    """Provider should not create a context message when absent."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Test response.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is True

    messages = (
        client.responses.received_kwargs["input"]
    )

    assert len(messages) == 2

    assert messages[0]["role"] == "system"
    assert messages[1]["role"] == "user"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_handles_api_error():
    """OpenAI exceptions should become failed ProviderResult."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        error=RuntimeError(
            "OpenAI service unavailable"
        ),
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is False
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert result.output is None
    assert (
        result.error
        == "OpenAI service unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_handles_empty_response():
    """Empty OpenAI output should produce a failed result."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is False
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert result.output is None
    assert (
        result.error
        == "OpenAI returned an empty response."
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_rejects_none_request():
    """Provider should reject a missing conversation request."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Conversation request cannot be None",
    ):
        await provider.generate(None)

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
    ParallelClientWrapper,

class FakeSearchResult:

    def model_dump(self):
        return {
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ]
        }


class FakeParallelClient:

    def search(
        self,
        *,
        search_queries,
        mode,
        objective,
    ):
        assert len(search_queries) == 1
        assert mode == "advanced"
        assert objective == (
            "Latest artificial intelligence news"
        )

        return FakeSearchResult()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_client_normalizes_response():

    wrapper = object.__new__(
        ParallelClientWrapper
    )

    wrapper.api_key = "test-key"

    wrapper._client = FakeParallelClient()

    result = await wrapper.search(
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["query"] == (
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["results"] == {
        "results": [
            {
                "title": "Example",
                "url": "https://example.com",
            }
        ]
    }

"""
Parallel Provider Tests.

These tests validate the provider layer
without calling the external Parallel API.

The real API integration is demonstrated
through the hackathon demo flow.
"""

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class MockParallelClient:
    """
    Fake Parallel client for automated tests.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ):

        return {
            "query": query,
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example source",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ],
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_provider_returns_results(
    monkeypatch,
):

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
    client=MockParallelClient()
)

    provider.client = MockParallelClient()

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest AI news",
    )

    result = await provider.execute(
        context
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        ProviderResult,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.provider_id == (
        "parallel_search"
    )

    assert result.output["query"] == (
        "Latest AI news"
    )

    assert len(
        result.output["results"]
    ) == 1

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,

    CharacterRegistry,

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_clara_uses_clara_profile():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    clara = registry.get(
        "clara"
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        clara
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Clara"
    )

    profile = result.output[
        "character_profile"
    ]

    assert profile["character_id"] == (
        "clara"
    )

    assert profile["name"] == (
        "Clara"
    )

    assert profile["description"] == (
        "A professional learning companion "
        "focused on technology and career topics."
    )

    assert profile["personality"] == (
        "Analytical, structured and supportive."
    )

    assert profile["learning_style"] == (
        "Structured and practical learning."
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)


class FakeConversationAgent:

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_context = None

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        self.received_context = context

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "character": "Yeti",
                "personality": (
                    "Curious and encouraging"
                ),
                "learning_style": (
                    "Conversation based learning"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    context.intelligence_context
                ),
            },
        )


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
                sources=(
                    IntelligenceSource(
                        title=(
                            "European AI Regulation"
                        ),
                        url=(
                            "https://example.com/ai"
                        ),
                        source="test",
                        content=(
                            "Current regulatory "
                            "information."
                        ),
                    ),
                ),
                summary=(
                    "Recent European AI "
                    "regulatory developments."
                ),
            ),
        )


class FakeQualityAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
        )


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def create_memory_service(
    tmp_path,
):
    return MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_passes_research_to_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=(
            conversation_agent
        ),
        research_agent=(
            FakeResearchAgent()
        ),
        quality_agent=(
            FakeQualityAgent()
        ),
        memory_service=(
            create_memory_service(
                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest "
            "European AI regulation"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert (
        result["research"]
        is not None
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result["research"],
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    received_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_context
    )

    assert (
        received_context is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context
        is not context
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context
        is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.query
        == context.request
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.source_count
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is received_context.intelligence_context
    )

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_without_research_preserves_normal_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=(
            conversation_agent
        ),
        research_agent=(
            FakeResearchAgent()
        ),
        quality_agent=(
            FakeQualityAgent()
        ),
        memory_service=(
            create_memory_service(
                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=False,
    )

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        is context
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        .intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )

import pytest

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_stores_memory(
    tmp_path,
):

    character = CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description="Learning companion",
        personality="Friendly",
        learning_style="Conversation",
    )

    memory_service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=character,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "I want to improve French speaking"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context
    )

    assert result["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    memories = (
        service.memory_service.recall(
            "demo-user"
        )
    )

    assert len(memories) == 1

    assert memories[0].content == (
        "I want to improve French speaking"
    )

    assert memories[0].category == (
        "conversation"
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

    CharacterProfile,
)

    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "conversation_agent"
    )

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert result.output["personality"] == (
        "Curious and encouraging"
    )

    assert result.output["learning_style"] == (
        "Conversation based learning"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output["conversation_request"],
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_includes_live_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query=(
            "Latest European AI regulation"
        ),
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="European AI Regulation",
                url="https://example.com/ai",
                source="parallel",
                content=(
                    "Current regulatory information."
                ),
            ),
        ),
        summary=(
            "Recent regulatory developments."
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest European "
            "AI regulation"
        ),
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert isinstance(
        request,
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "European AI Regulation"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "Current regulatory information."
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        result.output["intelligence_context"]
        == request.conversation_context
    )

============================================================
FILE: tests\runtime\test_conversation_agent_provider.py
============================================================
"""
Tests for ConversationAgent LLM provider integration.

These tests verify the architecture:

ConversationAgent
    |
    v
LLMService
    |
    v
Injected LLMProvider
    |
    v
ProviderResult

No real provider API is called.
"""

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider for ConversationAgent tests.

    The provider is injected into ConversationAgent and
    therefore wrapped by LLMService internally.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: ProviderResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Capture the request and return the configured result.
        """

        self.received_request = request

        return self.result


def create_character() -> CharacterProfile:
    """
    Create the deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_executes_injected_provider():
    """
    ConversationAgent should execute an injected provider
    through the LLMService boundary.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output=(
                "Artificial intelligence is "
                "the field of building systems "
                "that perform tasks requiring intelligence."
            ),
        )
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.output["message"] == (
        "Artificial intelligence is "
        "the field of building systems "
        "that perform tasks requiring intelligence."
    )

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert provider.received_request is not None

    assert (
        provider.received_request.user_prompt
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.character_id
        == "yeti"
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service is not None
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service.llm_provider
        is provider
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_handles_provider_failure():
    """
    Provider failures should be converted into a failed
    AgentResult without exposing provider SDK details.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=False,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            error="Provider unavailable",
        )
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert result.output["provider_error"] == (
        "Provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_without_provider_keeps_demo_behaviour():
    """
    ConversationAgent should preserve deterministic
    hackathon behaviour when no LLM service/provider
    is configured.
    """

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert (
        result.output["message"]
        == (
            "Yeti: Let's explore this together. "
            "You asked: Explain artificial intelligence"
        )
    )

    assert (
        "provider_id"
        not in result.output
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service
        is None
    )


def test_conversation_agent_rejects_both_service_and_provider():
    """
    ConversationAgent should reject ambiguous dependency
    injection when both LLMService and LLMProvider are supplied.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Test response",
        )
    )

    from backend.services.llm_service import (
        LLMService,
    )

    service = LLMService(
        provider
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Provide either llm_service or "
            "llm_provider, not both"
        ),
    ):
        ConversationAgent(
            character=create_character(),
            llm_service=service,
            llm_provider=provider,
        )

import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def test_execution_context_creation():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    assert context.user_id == (
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert context.request == (
        "Hello"
    )

    assert context.execution_id

    assert context.created_at

    assert context.metadata == {}

    assert context.intelligence_context is None

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is False


def test_execution_context_is_immutable():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.request = "Changed"


def test_execution_context_can_carry_intelligence():

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain this to me",
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    assert context.intelligence_context is intelligence

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is True

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context.query
        == "Latest European AI regulation"
    )
